### 4.4 Rule register (B4)

**(1) User**: Decision trees › Rules: read-only table of every rule (code, kind, market, source clause, version, dates
in force, state, who built and approved it). "As at" shows the version in force on a day (for auditors). Signed
conclusions show "Rules used".

**(2) Business rules** (`rule_register/register.py`)
- Rules are **data**, one JSON file per rule version in `backend/app/rule_register/data/` (61 files). There is **no
  write route**. A change is a new file in a release, built by one person and approved by the owner or a named
  regulatory approver, both recorded in the file's `approval` block (staff accounts listed in
  `register.py:STAFF_ACCOUNTS`, a code-controlled list; a recorded shortcut).
- Required fields: `code, rule, version, kind, market, held_as (DATA|CODE), title, plain, source, effective_from,
  effective_to, law_in_force_from, change{kind: INITIAL|LAW_CHANGE|CORRECTION}, review, approval` + `content` +
  `content_hash`.
- Effective dating: in force from `effective_from` up to (not including) `effective_to`; for a day the **highest
  version whose period contains it** wins; a CORRECTION has the same period and replaces at once. A floor is picked
  by the **awareness day in the authority's time zone** (`floor_day`). States: `IN_FORCE`, `SCHEDULED`, `RETIRED`,
  `REPLACED`. Today every file has `effective_from = effective_to = null` (from the start, open).
- `load()` refuses the whole register on any problem: missing field, duplicate, unknown catalog id, content-hash
  mismatch, overlap (other than corrections), coverage gap, end before start.
- **Pins**: `pinned: true` CODE rules carry `code_hash` = SHA-256 of the function's AST without docstrings
  (`code_hash`, `pin_problems`). Pinned: `reportability.service:awareness`, `markets:market_clock`,
  `markets:conclusion_problem`, `deadlines:count`, `facts:needed`, `complaint_disposition.rules:routine_unrecorded`.
  DATA rule books pin their file SHA-256 (LF-normalised for .csv/.json): `us_emdr.csv`, `us_emdr_v2.csv`,
  `eu_mir.csv`, `gb_mir.csv`, the MIR manifest, the MvPI PDF.
- Role floors: `ROLE_RULES = {(US, IMPORTER): US-IMP-floor, (US, USER_FACILITY): US-UF-floor}`.
- Engine (`engine.py`): operators `any, all, ref, is_yes, is_no, is_unknown, maybe, type`; three-valued Kleene logic.
  `evaluate` must equal `legacy_v1.evaluate` byte for byte for version 1 (`tests/test_b4_equivalence.py`).
- Kinds: REPORTABILITY_FLOOR, DEADLINE_COUNTING, REPORTABILITY, TRIAGE, INTAKE, ROUTINE_PATH, DISPOSITION,
  GUIDE_GOVERNANCE, REPORT_FORM, INVESTIGATION, ANALYTICS.
- **Consultant status**: all 61 versions `review.status = "STARTER"` ("Starter, for your consultant to confirm.").

**(3) Backend**: `rule_register/register.py`, `engine.py`, `router.py`; consumers `reportability/markets.py`
(`_InForce` mapping of `QUESTION_SETS`/`REPORT_TYPES`), `assessments.floor_at`.

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.rule_register.router` (2):
- GET `/api/v1/rule-register` (`as_at` optional)
- GET `/api/v1/rule-register/{code}`

**(5) Tables**: none for the register itself; `complaint_market_assessments.floor_code`, `rules_applied`, `date_calc`
(migration `tr27ruleregister`).

**(6) Frontend**: `features/workspace/sections/RuleRegister.tsx`, `features/workspace/ruleRegister.ts`.

**(7) Tests**: `test_b4_equivalence.py`, `test_b4_effective_dating.py`, `test_b4_register_api.py`,
`test_b4_window_floor.py`, `test_rule_engine_unit.py`, `test_rule_pins_unit.py`, `test_rule_register_unit.py`,
`test_b8_rule_book.py`, `test_b9_rule_book.py`, `test_b9b_other_floor.py`; frontend `ruleRegister.test.tsx`.

**(8) Invariants**: never edit an existing rule file or `legacy_v1.py`; a logic change = new version file with
correct `content_hash`; editing a pinned function or rule-book file without a new version fails
`test_rule_pins_unit.py`; on a Python minor upgrade re-pin the AST hashes (`code_hash` shortcut comment).

**(9) Known limits**: no in-app approval path for rule versions; approvals are recorded in files; all rules starter.

### 4.5 Decision guides (versioned) and decision trees

**(1) User**: Complaints › Decision guides (or Decision trees › Edit): each market's guide in versions. Version 1 is
MDARIX's starter (`US-starter-1` ...), read only. "Start a new version" copies the version in use (`US-v2`...). The
customer adds explanation and examples beside locked core questions, adds up to `EXTRA_MAX` extra questions (never
part of the conclusion), adds worked complaints to the test matrix, runs tests, sends for approval (approvers get a
My Work task), may withdraw, a second person approves with password + code, which **publishes** it. Export PDF/DOCX
for audit. Decision trees tab shows one tree per market and, for a complaint, the path it took.

**(2) Rules** (`decision_guides/rules.py`): locked fields `text, definition, note, clause, basis`; core questions
cannot be removed or reordered; conclusion logic stays `markets.evaluate` (one engine); every question needs a clause
and a basis in `standards.py`; starter test rows cannot be removed; sending needs every row passing; approval re-runs
the matrix and blocks on changed rows; approver may not be an editor or an administrator; a published version is
never edited; an assessment keeps the guide version it started with (`service.py`). Signature meaning
(`rules.py:354`): "I approve {version} of the {title} for use ... The guide points to a conclusion; a person
concludes and signs."

**(3) Backend**: `decision_guides/router.py`, `rules.py`, `service.py`, `trees.py`, `render.py` (PDF via
`reports/pdf.py`, DOCX via python-docx, 21 CFR 11.50 manifestation).

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.decision_guides.router` (10):
- GET `/api/v1/decision-guides`
- POST `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/drafts`
- GET `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}`
- PUT `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}`
- POST `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}/tests`
- POST `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}/approval-request`
- POST `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}/withdraw`
- POST `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}/approve`
- POST `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}/discard`
- GET `/api/v1/decision-guides/{market}/versions/{version}/guide.{kind}` (data.export; pdf|docx)

`backend.app.decision_guides.trees` (1): GET `/api/v1/decision-trees` (`?complaint_id=` optional)

**(5) Tables**: `decision_guide_versions` (model `db/models/guides_communications.py`, migration `tr06guidescomms`),
`signed_approval_records` (`DECISION_GUIDE_APPROVAL`), `workflow_tasks`.

**(6) Frontend**: `sections/DecisionGuides.tsx`, `DecisionTrees.tsx`, `TreeEdit.tsx`, `TreeAnswer.tsx`, `TreeSign.tsx`;
`workspace/decisionGuides.ts`, `decisionTrees.ts`; routes `/app/decision-trees` (`app/router.ts:107`).

**(7) Tests**: `test_decision_guides_api.py`; frontend `decisionGuides.test.tsx`.

### 4.6 Authority reports: obligation lifecycle

**(1) What the user sees and does** (Reports tab `/app/reports`, and the complaint's report)
1. A signed Reportable conclusion creates the market's report (INITIAL obligation) with the market's worksheet; the
   list (`GET /reports`, views `open`, `my-approval`, `submitted-month`) shows state, deadline and next step.
2. The worksheet shows every form field in the authority's order with value, status and source: *from record*,
   *entered by a person*, *confirmed*, missing classes (must have before submission / may be unknown initially /
   follow up later), *not used*, and *enter in the authority's route* for patient/reporter fields.
3. The person enters values, confirms record values, "enters once" a product or organization fact for every report,
   picks IMDRF codes (code picker) or asks for **AI code suggestions** (4.6.1), and "Confirm all codes" (mapping
   suggestions only, never AI ones).
4. "Trace this value" shows the record and import row behind a value, who changed it, and the snapshot it was frozen in.
5. **Send for approval** freezes a snapshot (and, on the new navigation, waits for every mandatory field). US: the
   report number is assigned (organization's next, never reused, advisory lock); the eMDR XML is built and frozen.
   EU/GB: the MIR XML is built, XSD-validated and frozen. Patient/reporter fields may be typed here if allowed.
6. A **second person approves** with password + authenticator code (never a preparer, never the conclusion's signer,
   never an administrator). India/Canada official forms are frozen at approval.
7. Download: worksheet PDF/DOCX (draft marked "DRAFT - NOT APPROVED"), the official form, `emdr.xml`, `mir.xml`.
8. Person submits outside MDARIX, then **Record submission**: route, time, submitted content and a **difference
   review** (no differences, or each classified MATERIAL / NON_MATERIAL; material needs reopen + re-approval).
   Optional file (max 2 MB, malware-scanned).
9. Record evidence: receipts, acknowledgement, acceptance or rejection.
10. Then: complete; follow-up (US supplemental, 30 calendar days from new information, 21 CFR 803.56); India
    follow-up/final; Canada update/final; MIR follow up/final/"updated final"; request for information (creates a
    separate RESPONSE obligation with its own due date); cancel an unsent follow-up with a reason; reopen an approved,
    unsubmitted snapshot (correction needs new approval).

**(2) Business rules** (`authority_reports/rules.py`)
- States: `NOT_STARTED`, `NO_WORKSHEET`, `DRAFT`, `CORRECTION_REQUIRED`, `APPROVED`, `SUBMITTED_PENDING`, `ACCEPTED`,
  `SUBMITTED_NO_ACK`, `FOLLOW_UP_DUE`, `COMPLETE`, `CANCELLED`. Status path stages: Created, Complete, Approval,
  Approved, Submitted, Acknowledged. `CANCELLABLE = DRAFT, CORRECTION, APPROVED` (never a submitted one).
- After submission, when to check acceptance (`rules.follow_up`, G2b rows 1-4): wait 1 h for eMDR (1 day other
  routes), earliest follow-up 15 min eMDR (1 h other), resubmission margin 4 h before the deadline end; row 4 = act now.
  A submission after the deadline end is flagged `late`.
- Report deadlines use the same `deadlines.end_of` as the watch.
- MIR follow-up/final: "Your date (1.2f): no fixed legal period" (MDR Art. 89(5) sets none, starter). India chain:
  "No legal clock: your date". Canada final due on the date in A5.
- US number: unique across every US report of the organization incl. cancelled ones and the other role's numbers
  (`assign_number`, `tests/test_b9_number_reuse.py`); a follow-up shares the initial's number; a sample report's
  practice number is never reused.
- Every value comes from fixed mappings (`worksheet.SOURCES`): MDARIX never invents a patient outcome, device id, date,
  investigation result, root cause or conclusion. Unconfirmed code suggestions are never frozen.
- Files for an authority come only from the snapshot approved **now** (`_approved_only`, 409
  `SNAPSHOT_NOT_APPROVED`); MIR bytes are re-hashed against `mir_sha256` on download (409 `MIR_CHANGED`).
- Signature meaning (`router.py:2276`): "I approve version {n} of the {form} for {ref} as the content to submit.
  MDARIX does not submit it."
- Changes need `regulatory.review` or `regulatory.approve`; downloads need `data.export`; AI suggestions also `ai.run`.
- Every action is audited on the data-plane trail via `_audit` (e.g. `REPORT_CREATED_FROM_CONCLUSION`,
  `REPORT_CANCELLED`, `REPORT_SNAPSHOT_APPROVED`, `REPORT_SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL_REFUSED`, `REPORT_FOLLOW_UP_DUE`,
  `REPORT_INFORMATION_REQUESTED`); downloads audited (`_audit_download`).

**4.6.1 AI code suggestions** (`authority_reports/ai_codes.py`): on request the model proposes IMDRF codes from the
complaint's own words. A proposal is kept only if the code exists in the newest loaded IMDRF release, in an annex the
form asks for, is still selectable, and its quoted words are in the complaint description; others are dropped with
the reason. Proposals are stored as an AI result (`ai_executions`), shown as "AI suggestion", never stored as a code.
A person enters one field by field via `PUT /fields/{id}` with `codes` + `ai_execution_id` (audited with origin).
Feature `code_suggestions` (MEDIUM risk) is off unless platform, organization and switch allow; in a free trial only
on sample data (`genai/sample_gate.py`).

**(3) Backend**: `authority_reports/router.py` (4113 lines: `report_list`, `start`, `follow_conclusion`,
`follow_signed`, `detail`, `set_field`, `enter_once`, `confirm_all_codes`, `ai_code_suggestions`, `assign_number`,
`request_snapshot`, `ask_for_approval`, `approve_snapshot`, `standing`, `reopen`, `record_submission`,
`record_evidence`, `follow_up`, `complete`, `start_follow_up`, `create_india_report`, `create_canada_report`,
`create_mir_report`, `cancel_follow_up`, `request_for_information`, `export`, `mir_file`, `emdr_file`,
`freeze_official_form`), `rules.py`, `worksheet.py` (`load`, `build`, `frozen`, `mandatory`, `confirm_suggestions`,
`nca_for`, `northern_ireland`, `us_recipients`), `forms.py` (form constants), `trace.py`, `ai_codes.py`, `render.py`.

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.authority_reports.router` (25):
- Lifecycle: GET `/api/v1/reports`; POST `/api/v1/reports`; GET `/api/v1/reports/{obligation_id}`;
  POST `/api/v1/reports/{obligation_id}/complete`; POST `/api/v1/reports/{obligation_id}/reopen`

**(5) Tables** (model `db/models/authority_reports.py`, data plane): `report_obligations` (market, country, kind,

### 4.7 Authority report files per market

| Market | Form (code, edition) | Submission routes recorded | Files MDARIX produces | Module |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| US | `FDA_3500A`, Form FDA 3500A (09/2025) | `EMDR`, `MFR_COPY` (importer/user facility sends to manufacturer) | worksheet PDF = eMDR content laid out as 3500A (`emdr_pdf.py`); `form=official`: FDA's own fillable 3500A filled (`fda_3500a.py`); `emdr.xml` HL7 ICSR R2 validated against FDA schemas (`emdr.py`) | `emdr.py`, `emdr_pdf.py`, `fda_3500a.py` |
| EU / NI | `EU_MIR`, MIR 7.3.1 (title string says build SB-11154; vendored XSDs are SB-11252: discrepancy, not verified which is right) | `EU_NCA`, `MHRA_MORE_EU` (NI) | `mir.xml` (5 report types, XSD-validated, `MirRefused` otherwise), PDF of the XML (`mir_pdf.py`) | `mir_xml.py`, `mir_pdf.py`, `mir_rules.py` |
| GB | `GB_MIR`, GB MIR 7.2.1 (04/06/2025) | `MHRA_MORE` | `mir.xml` for upload in MORE; PDF labelled as a record copy | same |
| Canada | `HC_MDPR`, HC Pub. 110180 (April 2018); types Preliminary, Update, Final, Preliminary & Final | `HC_EMAIL` | worksheet PDF/DOCX; official form **not available**: 409 `FORM_FILE_NEEDED` | `hc_mdpr.py` |
| India | `MVPI_MDAE`, MDAE form v1.2 | `MVPI_EMAIL`, `MVPI_ADRMS` | worksheet; `form=official` filled MvPI PDF; `form=adrms` ADRMS entry sheet | `mvpi_mdae.py` |
| Other | `ATTACHED` (attached-1) | `AUTHORITY_ROUTE` | none: customer attaches own file | `attached.py` |
| Response | `RESPONSE` (response-starter-1) | parent's routes | worksheet | `router.py:attach_request` |

Rules per file:
- **eMDR** (`emdr.py`): kinds `M-I, M-F, I-I, I-F, U-I, U-F` (manufacturer/importer/user facility, initial/follow-up).
  `check` blocks the file for any required block missing per the rule book (initial: B5, D2 procode, G1 email, G8,
  H1 and FDA's seven H6 code kinds; follow-up: G6 and G8; plus B1), any value too long or outside Windows-1252.
  `left_out` lists approved values the file does not carry (shown, not blocking). Contact office (G1) and manufacturer
  (D3) come from the organization's US responsible party and only while it reads exactly as approved. Batch ID =
  establishment registration number + time. Rule books `rules/us_emdr.csv` (v1) and `us_emdr_v2.csv` (v2 adds
  importer/user-facility rows; `emdr-rule-book-2`, a CORRECTION). `scripts/export_us_emdr_rules.py` exists.
- **3500A official PDF**: AcroForm filled, XFA and version-check scripts dropped, record page appended; a box ticked
  only when the value is FDA's own choice word for word ("Unknown" ticks Unknown, never No).
- **MIR**: blanks stay blank; never "Unknown" unless a person put it there; personal fields only from the snapshot;
  a value that does not fit is written as is so the XSD refuses it (never dropped to pass). Rule books
  `rules/eu_mir.csv`, `gb_mir.csv` (stricter of help text vs XSD, B8-D6); `forms.py must_in` must agree with them
  (tested). EU NCA contact list `rules/eu_nca.csv` + `data/mir_forms/eu-nca-2026-09/`.
- **MvPI**: 172 generic field names mapped **by position**; `mvpi_mdae.py:25 MAP_CHECKED = False` (B9-D17: a person
  must check the map against the printed form; the record page says it is unchecked).
- **Health Canada**: `data/hc_forms/manifest.json` lists no file (download from canada.ca failed 11 Nov 2026; only a
  canada.ca copy may be used). Tests for read-back are skipped until the owner provides the file.
- PDFs contain no date/time so the same file renders the same bytes; titles say "prepared by MDARIX" and never claim
  an authority made them; drafts marked "DRAFT - NOT APPROVED".

**Tests**: `test_fda_3500a_emdr.py` (compares with FDA's example files and XSDs), `test_release_0_10_96_emdr.py`,
`test_b9_emdr_section_f.py`, `test_b8_mir_xml.py`, `test_b8_gb_mir_xml.py`, `test_b8_mir_files.py`,
`test_b8_mir_pdf.py`, `test_b8_gb_end_to_end.py`, `test_b9_mvpi_form.py`, `test_b9_hc_form.py`,
`test_b9_frozen_forms.py`, `test_b8_export_personal_data.py`. **Fixtures**: `tests/fixtures/emdr_examples/` (FDA's
eMDR Implementation Package examples, byte-identical with SHA-256 in `manifest.json`: Manufacturer Initial - Typical
.xml/.pdf, Manufacturer Follow-up .xml/.pdf, Importer Initial/Follow-up, User Facility Initial/Follow-up; the
importer/user-facility ones are "local tests only until the owner confirms permission"). Schemas:
`data/fda_forms/emdr_schemas/`, `data/mir_forms/eu-7.3.1-SB-11252/`, `data/mir_forms/gb-7.2.1/`.

**Invariants**: never edit vendored forms/XSDs/examples (hash-pinned in manifests and the register); never return a
file that fails its schema; never guess a choice box.

### 4.9 Signer training

**(1) User**: before signing a market conclusion (or a disposition), the person reads each document a signature relies
on (each market's question set or the organization's published guide version, the harm scale `scale-starter-1`,
`disposition-starter-*`, `coding-starter-1`) and confirms "I have read ..., version n".

**(2) Rules**: nobody records training for someone else; a new document version makes earlier records out of date at
once; in a trial it is a "practice acknowledgement"; since RSK-011 (12 Nov 2026) signing is refused in **every**
organization without the record (`assessment_router.py:631`). MDARIX runs no training.

**(3) Backend**: `signer_training/router.py` (`is_trial` is imported widely), `service.py` (`documents`,
`for_market`, `missing`, `holders`, `acknowledge`).

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.signer_training.router` (2): GET `/api/v1/signer-training`;
POST `/api/v1/signer-training/{document_code}/acknowledge` (409 `VERSION_CHANGED` if stale).

**(5) Tables**: `signer_training_records` (model `db/models/market_assessment.py`, migration `tr01marketassessments`).

**(6) Frontend**: `MarketAssessment.tsx`, `TreeSign.tsx`, `TrustRecords.tsx`.

### 4.10 Oversight: reminders, escalation, deadline list, event closure, Value Centre

**(1) User**: Readiness › Oversight shows every dated item overdue or due soon (triage targets, reporting deadlines,
report obligations, replies due, approvals waiting, CAPA checks, tasks) with owner, due time and escalation; filters
by product/market/category; who is away; working calendar. People record their own absence with a backup. The event
page shows owner, stage line, closure checklist and the event decision record; the event owner signs "ready to close
in the QMS" with password (+ code), with a reason if items are open; records the QMS filing reference. Offline daily
deadline list (CSV/PDF and a daily e-mail from 06:00 local). Value Centre: medians and P90 of time to triage, to
reportability decision, to approved report, and field completeness. Urgent e-mail alert is opt-in.

**(2) Rules**: an alert never closes or changes an item; MDARIX never closes a complaint (the QMS does); the stage line
is a summary, never a gate; checklist items: triage complete, first-contact check signed, every relevant market signed
and no reassessment open, every obligation met, disposition or decision signed, information requests closed, no open
task, customer told; open CAPAs listed but not blocking. Escalation: owner (or backup while away) gets the reminder;
at the due time escalate to backup and the escalation role (`ESCALATION_ROLES = LEADER, REVIEWER, APPROVER,
REGULATORY_REVIEWER`); nobody else reachable -> owner told. Exports carry IDs/owners/dates/states only. Deadline list
sent even when empty ("silence means a problem"); the last recipient cannot switch it off. Value Centre never breaks
figures down by person. Settings changes need `quality.review` or `regulatory.approve`.

**(3) Backend**: `oversight/router.py`, `rules.py`, `service.py` (`due_items`, `sweep`), `closure.py`,
`deadline_list.py`, `record_page.py`, `service_work.py`, `urgent_alert.py`, `value_centre.py`. Scheduler:
`due-alerts` (15 min), `deadline-list` (15 min, once per local day), `reporting_reminders` (6 h).

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.oversight.router` (23):
- View/export: GET `/api/v1/oversight`; GET `/api/v1/oversight/export.csv`; GET `/api/v1/oversight/deadlines.csv`;
  GET `/api/v1/oversight/deadlines.pdf`; GET `/api/v1/oversight/value-centre`; GET `/api/v1/oversight/value-centre.csv`
- Settings: GET `/api/v1/oversight/settings`; PUT `/api/v1/oversight/settings`;
  PUT `/api/v1/oversight/settings/alerts-and-files`
- Alerts: GET `/api/v1/oversight/alerts`; POST `/api/v1/oversight/alerts/read`
- Absences: GET `/api/v1/oversight/absences`; POST `/api/v1/oversight/absences`;
  POST `/api/v1/oversight/absences/{absence_id}/cancel`
- Event: GET `/api/v1/oversight/events/{complaint_id}`; GET `.../events/{complaint_id}/activity`;
  GET `.../events/{complaint_id}/decision-record.pdf`; PUT `.../events/{complaint_id}/filing`;
  PUT `.../events/{complaint_id}/owner`; POST `.../events/{complaint_id}/ready`; POST `.../events/{complaint_id}/send`;
  PUT `.../events/{complaint_id}/service`
- Records: GET `/api/v1/oversight/latest-signed`

**(5) Tables** (model `db/models/oversight.py`, migration `tr07oversight`; `tr12sampleservice` for service work):
`oversight_settings`, `due_alerts`, `user_absences`, `event_closures`; plus `workflow_tasks`, `event_outbox`.

**(6) Frontend**: `sections/Oversight.tsx`, `Records.tsx`, `DecisionRecord.tsx`, `ValueCentre.tsx`;
`workspace/oversight.ts`, `home.ts`.

**(7) Tests**: `test_oversight_api.py`, `test_oversight_provisional.py`, `test_b6a_deadline_list.py`,

### 4.11 Ready-to-use QMS documents

**(1) User**: Records › Reports › Ready-to-use documents: pick one of five templates (`MANAGEMENT_REVIEW`,
`CAPA_LOG`, `INVESTIGATION_REPORT`, `RISK_REVIEW_EXTRACT`, `CHANGE_IMPACT`), a period (30/90/180/365 days) or a case /
change record; generate a draft from the records; write each judgement section; sign (password + code); record the
QMS filing reference and minutes spent; download PDF (exactly as signed) or DOCX.

**(2) Rules**: data sections are never edited (correct the record, regenerate); MDARIX never writes a judgement
section; the signed PDF is rendered once, stored in object storage, and its SHA-256 is the signature's
`content_fingerprint`; a signed version is never edited; administrators cannot sign; clauses print "pending approval"
until confirmed (none are). No patient/reporter text: complaints by ID, product and codes only. Signature meaning
(`templates.py:151`). Generate needs `data.export`; write/sign/file need `quality.review`.

**(3) Backend**: `qms_documents/router.py`, `service.py`, `templates.py`, `content.py`, `render.py`.

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.qms_documents.router` (8): GET `/api/v1/documents/types`; GET `/api/v1/documents`;
POST `/api/v1/documents`; GET `/api/v1/documents/{version_id}`; PUT `/api/v1/documents/{version_id}/judgement/{key}`;
POST `/api/v1/documents/{version_id}/sign`; PUT `/api/v1/documents/{version_id}/filing`;
GET `/api/v1/documents/{version_id}/{kind}`.

**(5) Tables**: no own table: a version is a `report_jobs` row (`report_code`, `status` DRAFT|SIGNED, `context`)
plus `signed_approval_records` (`QMS_DOCUMENT`).

**(6) Frontend**: `sections/Documents.tsx`, `workspace/documents.ts`.

**(7) Tests**: `test_qms_documents_api.py`, `test_qms_documents_rules.py`; frontend `documents.test.tsx`.

**(9) Known limit**: writer/signer separation is described as an MVP addition in `service.py` docstring (not enforced
in this code; not verified further).

### 4.12 Risk register and the post-market risk file

**(1) User**: Risk & Traceability (`/app/risk-traceability`): risk register with failure modes, controls,
verification evidence, requirements; the ISO 14971 chain requirement -> risk -> failure mode -> control ->
verification with gaps; CSV export; "ask for review" of one risk. Records › Risk file input (`RiskFile.tsx`): per
product, whether post-market data (harm/malfunction complaints, significant rises, decided signals) reached its risk
file; product states `NOT_REVIEWED`, `REASSESSMENT_OVERDUE`, `REASSESSMENT_OPEN`, `NO_RISK_REGISTER`, `REVIEWED`,
`QUIET`.

**(2) Rules**: read only; MDARIX never changes a risk or decides what the risk file says; hazard/harm/severity/
probability are reported "not captured" because the import has no such columns (no invented score); residual-risk
state only from recorded links. CSV is formula-safe and audited; review request audited `RISK_REVIEW_REQUESTED`.
`/api/v1/risk/*` is parked (404) under `MDARIX_FEATURE_SET=mvp`.

**(3) Backend**: `backend/app/risk/service.py`, `risk_router.py`, `post_market/risk_file.py`, `post_market/router.py`.

`backend.app.post_market.router` (2): GET `/api/v1/post-market/risk-file`;
GET `/api/v1/post-market/risk-file/{product_id}`.

### 4.14 Regulatory copilot and eSTAR preparation (`regulatory_router`, root `regulatory_intelligence/`)

**(1) User**: draft eSTAR, GSPR, CER, IFU sections from the organization's records; AI text is advisory, every section
needs human review; status DRAFT -> IN_REVIEW -> APPROVED -> ARCHIVED; approval is a signature; eSTAR *preparation*
package download.

**(2) Rules**: the rules draft (`regulatory_intelligence/service.py`) is the controlled result; the model writes
section text from authorized, tenant-scoped context only (`copilot.py`); every section is checked like an Ask answer;
`RULES_ONLY` when AI is off/unavailable. Citations only from `catalog.OFFICIAL_SOURCES` (public metadata; ISO text is
never bundled). eSTAR: MDARIX "must not claim that a home-grown XML file is an FDA eSTAR submission" (`estar.py`):
it is a preparation package. Approval: `regulatory.approve`, never an administrator, never an author of any version,
re-authenticated (`verify_signing_credentials`), audited `REGULATORY_DOCUMENT_APPROVED`. Transitions:
DRAFT->{IN_REVIEW, ARCHIVED}, IN_REVIEW->{DRAFT, APPROVED, ARCHIVED}, APPROVED->{ARCHIVED}. Parked under
`MDARIX_FEATURE_SET=mvp`.

GET `/api/v1/regulatory/documents`; GET `/api/v1/regulatory/documents/{document_id}`;
POST `/api/v1/regulatory/documents/{document_id}/status`; GET `/api/v1/regulatory/documents/{document_id}/estar`
(data.export).

`z43regulatorydocuments`).

`test_estar_generation.py`; frontend `regulatoryModel.test.ts`.

### 4.16 Electronic signatures: re-authentication, authenticator confirmation, two-database commit

**Re-authentication for every signature** — `backend/app/governance_router.py:439 verify_signing_credentials(db, ctx,
*, password, mfa_code, record_refusal)`. Used by market conclusions, other-country conclusions, report snapshot
approval, attached "Submitted", guide approval, QMS document signing, oversight "ready to close", regulatory document
approval, case decisions, signed reviews (`signed_reviews/signing.py`). Behaviour:
- current password, then (when `MDARIX_REQUIRE_MFA_FOR_SIGNATURES` is true, the default) a current authenticator code
  from an enrolled account; recovery codes not accepted;
- wrong passwords and codes count together on the "sign" throttle: 5 lock signing for 15 min (`mfa.LOCK_MINUTES`);
  separate from the sign-in lock;
- each refusal rolls back `db`, calls `record_refusal(action)` (audited `SIGNATURE_REAUTHENTICATION_FAILED`,
  `MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_SIGNATURE`, `SIGNATURE_MFA_CODE_REUSED`, `SIGNATURE_MFA_FAILED`), commits, raises 403;
- error codes are signature-specific (`INVALID_REAUTHENTICATION`, `SIGNATURE_MFA_NOT_SET_UP`,
  `SIGNATURE_CODE_ALREADY_USED`, `SIGNATURE_CODE_INVALID`, `SIGNATURE_TEMPORARILY_LOCKED`), **never `MFA_REQUIRED`**
  (that would send the browser to /mfa and lose the form; `tests/test_signing_code_refusals.py`);
- a code already used (e.g. at sign-in) is refused but not counted toward the lock;
- callers must re-authenticate **first**, before staging anything in the control session (comment at
  `assessment_router.py:659`, `authority_reports/router.py:2367`).

**Authenticator confirmation for bulk data operations** — `backend/app/tenant_export_api.py:62
confirm_with_authenticator(db, user, code, *, tenant_id, action, request)`: required for a full organization export
(own or platform), a purge, and support-access approval (`support_access_api.py:271`). Enrolled account must give a
current code (no recovery codes; failures count on the sign-in code lock); an account whose role must use MFA but has
none is refused `MFA_ENROLLMENT_REQUIRED`; only where no MFA policy applies (local) does an unenrolled account pass.
Refusal audited `{action}_REFUSED_MFA`, error `AUTHENTICATOR_CODE_REQUIRED`. Test: `tests/test_export_purge_mfa.py`.

**Who decides support access** — `backend/app/support_access_api.py:73 require_decision_maker(db, user)`: in a free
trial the trial owner only; otherwise a Customer Administrator. (Support access itself belongs to the administration
section; listed here because the task names it.)

**Signature record** — `signed_approval_records` (control plane): `object_type`, `object_id`, `object_version`,
`decision_type`, `decision` (VARCHAR 12), `signer_user_id`, `signer_role`, `signature_meaning` (21 CFR 11.50),
`content_fingerprint` (SHA-256 of canonical JSON, or of the signed PDF for QMS documents), `signature_hash`
(`human_decision/rules.py:526`: SHA-256 of fingerprint + signer + version), `status` (SIGNED | PENDING),
`invalidated_at`, `invalidation_reason`. Object/decision types in this section: `ComplaintMarketAssessment`/
`MARKET_REPORTABILITY_CONCLUSION`, `ReportSnapshot`/`REPORT_SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL`, `DecisionGuideVersion`/
`DECISION_GUIDE_APPROVAL`, `QmsDocumentVersion`/`QMS_DOCUMENT`.

**Two-database commit** — `backend/app/signing_commit.py:commit(data, control_db, what)`: on a shared plane one
transaction; on a dedicated plane a durable state machine PREPARE (data-plane record + receipt audit row
`SIGNATURE_RECORD_SAVED` + advisory lock) -> PENDING signature (control) -> SAVED (data commit) -> SIGNED (`settle`
writes the carried audit events exactly once). A PENDING signature is never valid. Failure before SAVED abandons the
signature (`SIGNATURE_ABANDONED`). Scheduler job `signature-settlement` (10 min) settles. Tests
`tests/test_signing_recovery.py`.

**Decision signatures and verification** — `backend.app.governance_router` (6; primary owner is the decision section,
listed here for the signature endpoints): POST `/api/v1/governance/decisions/{decision_id}/sign`;
POST `/api/v1/governance/decisions/{decision_id}/material-change` (sets the decision to REQUIRES_REVIEW);
GET `/api/v1/governance/decisions/{decision_id}/signature-history`;
GET `/api/v1/governance/decisions/{decision_id}/signature-verification` (no secrets or signing material);
GET `/api/v1/governance/signed-records` (latest signatures of every kind); GET `/api/v1/governance/effective-access`.
Frontend `features/signature/SignatureVerificationWorkspace.tsx`, `sections/TrustRecords.tsx`, `AuditTab.tsx`.
Tests `test_signing_code_refusals.py`, `test_triage_signing_refusals.py`, `test_case_signed_before.py`, frontend
`SignatureVerificationWorkspace.test.ts`, `signaturesSeal.test.tsx`.

**Invariants**: one re-auth function for every signature; re-auth before staging; never return `MFA_REQUIRED` from a
signature; signature valid only when `status = SIGNED`; never sign from a seed path with a real password check
bypassed except the documented demo seeding (4.21).

### 4.17 Audit trail, hash chain, case audit and signed reviews

**Hash chain** (`backend/app/audit_chain.py`): the database chains every `audit_events` row on insert (migration
`md18auditchain`: `chain_seq`, `previous_hash`, `event_hash` = SHA-256 of canonical content + previous hash) and
rejects updates and deletes (`audit_events_immutable`, from `o31auditimmutability`). `verify_tenant_chain` calls the
SQL function `public.mdarix_verify_audit_chain(tenant, after_seq, after_hash)` and returns `valid, checked,
first_broken_seq, last_seq, last_hash, verified_at`. Scheduler `audit-chain-verification` (24 h) runs `verify_all`
with anchors in `scheduler_runs.state` (a rewritten-and-rehashed chain still breaks its anchor; a cell verifies only
its own organization). Retention: 7 years (`enterprise_audit.retention_until`, also the DB default since
`md23pilotreadiness`). Audit events are created only via `enterprise_audit.make_audit_event` (details sanitised by
`safe_details`, user agent cut to 512).

Endpoints — `backend.app.audit_chain_api` (2): GET `/api/v1/admin/audit-chain` (`verify_own_audit_chain`: an
organization admin or a free trial's owner, via `access/authz.admin_or_trial_owner`); GET
`/api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/audit-chain` (`verify_tenant_audit_chain`, platform admin). Frontend
`customer-admin/sections/Audit.tsx`, `workspace/sections/TrustRecords.tsx`. Tests `test_audit_hash_chain.py`,
`test_day35_audit_immutability.py`, `test_retention_audit.py`, `test_audit_compliance_controls.py`,
`test_audit_input_limits.py`. Related audit history/evidence-package export endpoints live in
`backend.app.access.observability` (`/api/v1/admin/audit-history`, `/export`, `/evidence-package`; other section).

**Case audit** (`backend/app/case_audit/`): per case timeline (merged, deduplicated, oldest first, filters, paging),
replay "as known at" a moment or a decision, AI runs (model, versions, context hash, guardrails, human review,
disclosure), integrity (signatures, brief fingerprints, audit chain), export JSON/CSV (data.export, audited
`CASE_AUDIT_EXPORTED` with SHA-256). Read only. Endpoints — `backend.app.case_audit.router` (5): GET
`/api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/trust/timeline`; `/trust/replay`; `/trust/ai-runs`; `/trust/integrity`;
`/trust/export`. Frontend `sections/CaseAudit.tsx`, `workspace/caseAudit.ts`. Tests `test_case_audit.py`,
`test_case_audit_unit.py`; frontend `caseAudit.test.ts`.

**Signed reviews** (`backend/app/signed_reviews/`, switch `MDARIX_B6_REVIEWS`): audit trail review (read by Leader,
Quality Reviewer or customer admin; signed by Leader or Quality Reviewer only; a signer whose own actions are flagged
needs a second reviewer's confirmation; frequency 1-12 months, monthly default, set by a Leader with reason), access
review (every active user marked Keep/Change/Remove; signed by a customer admin once every row is marked and their own
row was marked by someone else), configuration snapshot (signed by a customer admin only; never a key or secret).
Content is built on the server at signing, hashed, stored as one audit event `details["material"]`, signed as a
`SignedApprovalRecord`; never edited (a correction names the earlier review in `corrects`). All in the control DB.
Endpoints — `backend.app.signed_reviews.router` (14): GET `/api/v1/reviews/audit-trail`;
GET `/api/v1/reviews/audit-trail/events`; PUT `/api/v1/reviews/audit-trail/frequency`;
POST `/api/v1/reviews/audit-trail/sign`; POST `/api/v1/reviews/audit-trail/{review_id}/confirm`;
GET `/api/v1/reviews/access`; POST `/api/v1/reviews/access/marks`; POST `/api/v1/reviews/access/sign`;
GET `/api/v1/reviews/configuration`; GET `/api/v1/reviews/configuration/compare`;
POST `/api/v1/reviews/configuration/sign`; GET `/api/v1/reviews/signed/{review_id}` (`intact` flag);
GET `/api/v1/reviews/signed/{review_id}/download`; GET `/api/v1/reviews/{kind}/signed`. Frontend
`customer-admin/sections/Reviews.tsx`. Tests `test_b6a_signed_reviews.py`.

### 4.18 Exports and data governance

**CSV safety** (`backend/app/csv_safe.py`): every CSV goes through `safe_cell`/`writer` (cells starting `= + - @`,
tab or CR prefixed with an apostrophe). `controlled_export.prepare_export` bounds exports by tenant, fields
(`AI_PROHIBITED_FIELDS`) and size (1000 records).

**Cases export and QMS-entry export** — `backend.app.intake.case_export` (2): GET `/api/v1/intake/cases/export.csv`
(contacts masked "Contact held: yes"; unmasked only with `unmask_reason` by `data.export`, **never in a free trial**;
raw e-mail never exported); GET `/api/v1/intake/qms-entry/export.csv` (MDARIX Connect: complaints made from Cases with
no QMS ID, in the complaints template column order + `mdarix_ref`; the next import with `mdarix_ref` merges back).
Both UTF-8 BOM, max 20,000 rows, audited `CASES_EXPORTED` / `QMS_ENTRY_EXPORTED` with filter, count and SHA-256;
switch `MDARIX_ROUTINE_PATH`. Frontend `sections/CasesList.tsx`. Test `test_case_export_b7.py`.

**Other CSV/PDF exports in this section**: oversight list, deadlines CSV/PDF, Value Centre CSV, risk register CSV,
case audit export, report worksheets/files, guide and QMS document downloads (all `data.export`, audited).

**Full organization export ("your data")** — `backend/app/tenant_export.py` + `tenant_export_api.py`: request
records an outbox event; the worker writes a zip: every table with rows for the organization (discovered from the DB,
so new tables are included) as JSON Lines under `data/`, stored files under `files/`, readable CSVs under `readable/`,
`manifest.json` with counts, SHA-256 per entry and the audit chain's verified head. Credentials never exported
(passwords, secrets, tokens, recovery codes, encrypted keys). Built on disk, stored encrypted, downloadable for
`EXPORT_DAYS = 7` days, then deleted by `export-expiry` (1 h job). Contacts masked unless `unmask_contacts` (needs
data.export); `include_personal` (patient/reporter details from submitted reports) only together with unmasked
(`PERSONAL_DATA_NEEDS_UNMASKED`). Authenticator code required. Person export: a customer admin answers one person's
access request as JSON (audited).

**Purge** — `backend/app/tenant_purge.py`: platform admin; organization must be SUSPENDED/TERMINATED/PURGING, not
the operator's own, **not under legal hold**; password + authenticator + typed organization key + reason; export first
unless a waiver reason. Stage 1 deletes rows all-or-nothing (tables discovered by `tenant_id`); stage 2 deletes stored
files and dedicated-plane rows with retries; stays PURGING on failure (finish with
`scripts/complete_tenant_purge.py` or `purge-completion` job). Keeps: tenant row (ARCHIVED), subscription/plan/licence
history, pseudonymized accounts and trial request, consented published feedback, `erasure_log` (re-applied after
restore). The audit trail is deleted as a whole and restarted with one record of the deletion.

**Legal hold** — `backend/app/legal_hold_api.py` (mounted through `tenant_export_api.router`): platform admin with a
fully verified session sets/clears with reason (`LEGAL_HOLD_SET` / `LEGAL_HOLD_CLEARED`); while set, purge,
personal-data erasure, trial retention, purge completion and erasure re-apply refuse
(`TENANT_PURGE_REFUSED_LEGAL_HOLD`, `TRIAL_RETENTION_SKIPPED_LEGAL_HOLD`). Runbook `docs/runbooks/DATA_REQUESTS.md`.

Endpoints — `backend.app.tenant_export_api` (8): GET `/api/v1/admin/exports`; POST `/api/v1/admin/exports`;
GET `/api/v1/admin/exports/{export_id}/download`; GET `/api/v1/admin/people/{user_id}/export`;
GET `/api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/exports`; POST `/api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/exports`;
GET `/api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/exports/{export_id}/download`;
POST `/api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/purge`.
`backend.app.legal_hold_api` (2): GET and PUT `/api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/legal-hold`.

Tables: `tenant_exports` (model `db/models/stage2.py`, migration `md19tenantexports`), `erasure_log`
(`db/models/pilot_support.py`), `tenants.legal_hold`, `legal_hold_set_at` (migration `md23pilotreadiness`).
Frontend `customer-admin/sections/Organization.tsx` (export request), `admin/customers/TenantPages.tsx`,
`dataGovernance.ts` (legal hold). Tests `test_export_purge_mfa.py`, `test_person_export.py`,
`test_purge_completion_job.py`, `test_legal_hold.py`, `test_day25_retention_export.py`.

### 4.19 Validation evidence and the validation pack

**(1) User**: an organization admin, a free trial's owner or a platform admin opens Validation evidence: each
requirement, the tests that cover it and their last recorded result ("No run recorded" without a run); downloads the
release validation pack as a **dry run** (every document watermarked "development results, not validation
evidence"). Only a platform admin records a test run (pytest JUnit XML upload).

**(2) Rules**: the mapping comes from the repository (`docs/validation/requirements.csv`, `requirement(<scenario>)`
markers copied into JUnit properties by `validation/pytest_requirements.py`, imported in `tests/conftest.py`); the
JUnit carries commit, dirty-tree flag, hook version and package versions; the pack generator refuses a JUnit of another
commit, a changed tree or an older hook. The pack (`validation/pack.py`): URS, FS/DS, IQ record, OQ protocol and
results log, RTM, deviations, G13 figures, draft VSR, release approval record (Markdown, DOCX, PDF; tables CSV/XLSX;
SHA-256 manifest); a release pack is written only when no check fails. **It never states that a release is
validated.** STATUS.md: "No formal validation package (IQ/OQ/PQ, Part 11 traceability) exists yet."

**(3) Backend**: `backend/app/access/validation_evidence.py`; root `validation/` (`pack.py`, `evidence_view.py`,
`pytest_requirements.py`, `docx_render.py`, `xlsx_write.py`, `csa_package.py` with older requirement/test constants);
scripts `scripts/validation_pack.py`, `run_validation.py`, `validation_signing_pdfs.py`.

**(4) Endpoints** — `backend.app.access.validation_evidence` (3): GET `/api/v1/admin/validation-evidence`;
GET `/api/v1/admin/validation-evidence/pack`; POST `/api/v1/platform-admin/validation-runs`.

**(5) Tables**: `validation_test_runs` (model `db/models/validation_runs.py`, migration `tr33validationruns`).

**(6) Frontend**: `features/validation/ValidationEvidence.tsx` (in `admin/areas/SecurityArea.tsx` and
`sections/Records.tsx`).

**(7) Tests**: `test_validation_evidence.py`, `test_validation_pack.py`; frontend `validationEvidence.test.tsx`.

### 4.20 Legal texts (root `legal/`)

`legal/privacy.md`, `terms.md`, `trial-agreement.md`, `SUBPROCESSORS.md`, `README.md`. Lawyer-written; engineering
only places approved files. Served by `backend/app/site_info.py` GET `/api/v1/public/legal/{document}` (module
`backend.app.site_info`, 3 endpoints, owned by the website section) and rendered by `frontend/src/site/legal.tsx`.
Staging/production refuse to start unless all three exist, carry no DRAFT banner or `[PLACEHOLDER]`, company details
are set and `MDARIX_LEGAL_TEXT_APPROVED=true` (`backend/app/config_check.py:167`). Mounted read-only in production
(`docker-compose.prod.yml`). Trial signup acceptance recorded (migration `md48legalacceptance`). Tests
`test_legal_compliance_0_10_33.py`, `test_auth_signup_acknowledgement.py`. STATUS.md has both "owner-approved legal
texts" (17 Nov) and an older "drafts awaiting a lawyer" limit: whether the files in the tree are final is **not
verified**.

### 4.21 Demo golden path (`backend/app/demo_golden_path/`)

Seeds the demo workspace's worked case ACD-INV-401 (synthetic data) from signal to a signed decision with conditions,
a CAPA follow-up, late complaint and late evidence; called by `backend/app/demo_workspace.py` (`seed_golden_path`,
`golden_path_status`). The signature is made with the real signing functions (`human_decision.rules` fingerprint,
signature hash, snapshot hash) so verification reports VALID; **no password is checked**, the action is audited
`DEMO_SEEDED` by `system:demo-workspace`, never `CASE_DECISION_SIGNED`. Idempotent (`_already_seeded`). Helpers are
reused by `trial/sample_decision.py` and `trial/sample_persona_work.py`. Test `test_demo_golden_path_unit.py`.
Invariant: keep it demo-only; never reuse its no-password signing path for real tenants.

---

### 4.22 Reference data (`data/`)

- `data/code_lists/` + `manifest.json`: IMDRF Annexes A-G (Release 2026, revised June 2026), FDA CDRH NCIt subsets
  (26.08e, with FDA's IMDRF mapping), FDA product codes (`foiclass.zip`, 2026-11-02), EMDN v2026; each with SHA-256,
  source URL and publication date. GMDN licensed, never loaded. Loaded by `scripts/load_code_lists.py`.
- `data/fda_forms/`: FDA 3500A fillable PDF (09-15-2025), `emdr_schemas/` (Con170227.xsd + coreschemas), manifest.
- `data/mir_forms/`: EU 7.3.1 SB-11252 XSDs/XSL/form/help/changelog, GB 7.2.1 XSDs/help/implementation guide,
  `eu-nca-2026-09/` vigilance contact points, manifest.
- `data/mvpi_forms/`: MvPI MDAE form v1.2 PDF + manifest. `data/hc_forms/`: manifest with **no file** + empty
  `field_map.json`.
- `data/golden/`: AI golden dataset (owned by the AI section). `data/source-templates/`: import templates (owned by
  the import section).
- In-code rule books: `backend/app/authority_reports/rules/*.csv`.

Invariant: these files are byte-identical copies of publishers' files, pinned by SHA-256; never edit, reformat or
re-save them (Windows line-ending normalisation applies only to .csv/.json rule books).

### 4.23 Scheduler jobs in this area (`backend/app/scheduler.py`)

`reporting_reminders` (6 h), `due-alerts` (15 min), `deadline-list` (15 min, once per local day from 06:00),
`signature-settlement` (10 min), `audit-chain-verification` (24 h), `export-expiry` (1 h), `purge-completion`,
`trial-retention` (respects legal hold).

---

### 4.24 Invariants Gemini must not break (checklist)

1. No code path submits to an authority; no outbound call to FDA/EUDAMED/MHRA/Health Canada/MvPI.
2. No automatic reportability conclusion, report approval, guide publication, QMS document signature or closure.
3. Every signature: `verify_signing_credentials` first; administrators refused; second person where required.
4. `Unknown` is never `No`; `PENDING` blocks Not reportable.
5. Rule register: no write path; new version file for any change; `content_hash` and pins must pass; never edit
   `legacy_v1.py`.
6. One deadline function (`reportability/deadlines.py`); GB and India count awareness as day 1.
7. Files for authorities only from the currently approved snapshot; frozen bytes re-hashed on download.
8. Patient/reporter data only in a frozen snapshot, only when allowed; never in logs, errors, audit, CSVs.
9. Audit events only via `make_audit_event`; never UPDATE/DELETE `audit_events` (the DB refuses); purge is the only
   deletion and is legal-hold gated.
10. `signed_approval_records.decision` max 12 characters; `status = SIGNED` is the only valid state.
11. Router mount order in `main.py` (4.0).
12. Vendored forms, XSDs, code lists and FDA example fixtures stay byte-identical.
13. Every CSV through `csv_safe`; every export audited.
14. Tenant and actor only from the request context; business data on `get_data_plane_db`, signatures/audit on `get_db`.
15. Switch defaults as in 4.0 (user facility stays off).

### 4.25 Known limits and items not verified

- All rules, question sets, counting rules, form mappings and templates are **starter** text; no consultant
  confirmation is recorded anywhere in code.
- Health Canada official form missing (409 `FORM_FILE_NEEDED`); MvPI field map not checked by a person
  (`MAP_CHECKED = False`).
- EU MIR: `forms.py` title/version and docstring say build SB-11154, the vendored XSDs and register entry are
  SB-11252 (inconsistency; not verified which the authority currently publishes).
- FDA importer/user-facility example fixtures are "local tests only until the owner confirms permission".
- No public-holiday calendar for authority clocks.
- STATUS.md: "Risk and regulatory reviews have no separation-of-duties rule: the investigator may complete a review"
  (case reviews, not market conclusions).
- QMS document writer/signer separation is not enforced in this code (described as MVP work).
- `qms_documents/router.py` and `reportability/assessment_router.py` docstrings mention mvp-1.0's `_PILOT_API` gate;
  in this branch the name appears only in docstrings (grep of `backend/`), so no such gate exists here.
- Whether every e2e journey exercises Send for approval / approve / record submission end to end: not verified.
