## 5. AI in MDARIX

Source of every claim: the worktree `feat/app-wide-layout` (0.11.07 candidate), read on 19 Nov 2026. Paths are
relative to the repository root. Endpoints are taken from Appendix A. Anything marked **not verified** was not checked
in code or by running anything. Nothing was run: no app, no tests, no AI provider.

### 5.0 Rules Gemini must keep (read first)

1. **Do not name the model in the product or in the demo.** On screen and in the voice-over, say "AI" or "MDARIX AI".
   Never say a provider or model name (for example the values in `genai/provider.py:22-45`). Some screens show the
   provider and model today (see 5.11). Keep those screens off camera. Hiding them in code is an owner decision.
   Never rename or blank the provider or model in stored records: they are the audit trail.
2. **AI is off unless every switch is on.** The levels are: platform provider, platform feature list, organization
   switch, organization feature switch, budget, and (free trial only) the trial chain and the person's own flag.
   Every refusal falls back to rule-based answers. Do not add a path that calls a model without
   `GenAIProvider.complete(feature=...)`.
3. **AI proposes, people decide.** No feature may have risk HIGH (`genai/features.py:117-126`: the import fails).
   A model-written result is not usable downstream until a person accepts or edits it
   (`backend/app/ai_review.py:139-155`).
4. **Tests never call a real provider** (`tests/conftest.py:76-97`). Do not set `MDARIX_TEST_ALLOW_LIVE_PROVIDERS=1`.
5. **Switching on Gemini on Vertex for real data is the owner's decision.** The code is ready (5.13). The release gate
   is not met on this branch (5.9).

### 5.1 Map of the AI code

| Area | Files | Role |
|---|---|---|
| Provider boundary | `genai/provider.py` (1,056 lines) | `GenAIProvider.complete`: the only door to a model. It handles redaction, PII masking, feature, platform, organization and trial gates, provider chains, rest periods and the call record |
| Feature register | `genai/features.py` | The 8 AI features, their risk, and which are offered |
| Provider policy | `genai/policy.py` | Which providers may receive customer data in staging and production. Also model pinning and the Vertex region |
| Organization controls | `genai/governance.py` | Request scope, organization switch, feature switch, budget, trial and per-person flag, shared rest periods |
| PII masking | `genai/pii.py` | Masks personal data before anything leaves |
| Trial sample gate | `genai/sample_gate.py` | Free trials send unedited sample data only |
| Vetted register | `genai/vetted.py`, `genai/vetted_providers.json` | Which free services the trial chain may use, and checks on the upstream that answered |
| Call record | `genai/call_record.py` | One `ai_calls` row per request actually sent |
| Ask (model path) | `genai/ask.py`, `genai/structured_answer.py`, `genai/grounding.py` | Evidence search, grounded context, citation check, structured answer |
| Labels | `genai/epistemic.py` | "AI interpretation" label; notice shown when rules answered instead of AI |
| Other genai | `genai/isolation.py` (tenant cache and vector keys), `genai/validation.py` (contract check, cost reconciliation) | Helpers |
| Evidence extraction | `ai/extractor.py`, `ai/schemas.py`, `ai/execution.py` | Proposes observations from evidence; `record_ai_execution` |
| Ask foundation (no model) | `ask_mdarix/*.py`, `backend/app/ask_router.py` | Deterministic interpreter and authorized retrieval. **It never calls a model** |
| Data boundary | `backend/app/ai_boundary.py` | Fail-closed field allowlist used by `ask_mdarix` retrieval |
| Settings and gates | `backend/app/ai_settings.py`, `backend/app/ai_release_gate.py` | Switches, budget, `/ai/status`, the four-bar release gate |
| Review and governance | `backend/app/ai_review.py`, `backend/app/ai_governance.py` | Accept, edit or reject; governance screen; lock at signing |
| Case AI run | `backend/app/case_ai/router.py`, `backend/app/case_ai/runner.py` | All case AI steps in one background pass |
| Code suggestions | `backend/app/authority_reports/ai_codes.py` | IMDRF code proposals on report drafts |
| Evaluation | `evaluation/*.py`, `evaluation/*.json`, `backend/app/evaluation_router.py`, `artifacts/real-golden-evaluation*.json` | Golden dataset, harness, real-pipeline scoring, release record |
| Embeddings | `retrieval/embedding_provider.py`, `.model-cache/` | Local embedding model. Evidence text never leaves the server |
| Recorded sample AI | `backend/app/trial/sample_ai.py` | Pre-recorded AI proposals on the sample case. No model is called |

### 5.2 Providers in code (and Vertex status)

All providers are declared in `OPENAI_COMPATIBLE` (`genai/provider.py:22-45`):

| Name in code | How it is called | Notes in code |
|---|---|---|
| `controlled` | No model | The default (`provider.py:290`). Returns `PROVIDER_NOT_CONFIGURED` (`provider.py:476-492`) and the rules answer |
| `groq` | OpenAI-compatible | Default model is set in code (`provider.py:17`). Also the trial chain's direct last provider (`vetted_providers.json`) |
| `cerebras`, `mistral`, `openrouter` | OpenAI-compatible | Fallback candidates. OpenRouter `:free` models are refused in production (`policy.py:61-73`) |
| `gemini` | OpenAI-compatible, Google AI Studio endpoint | Address and moving default model at `provider.py:26` |
| `anthropic` | Its own Messages API (`provider.py:94-98, 556-567`) | Production only under a commercial agreement |
| **`vertex`** | Vertex AI's OpenAI-compatible endpoint, **no API key** | **PRESENT on this branch.** Address from `vertex_url(project, location)` (`provider.py:67-75`). Token from the VM metadata server (`provider.py:78-91`). Default region `asia-south1` (`provider.py:60`). Default model at `provider.py:36`. Sends `reasoning_effort` (`provider.py:580-582`) |
| `ollama` | OpenAI-compatible, local | Self-hosted. Allowed in production (`policy.py:27, 74-75`) |
| `omniroute`, `freellmapi` | Self-hosted gateways on loopback | Trial chain only. The upstream that answered is read from reply headers and must be vetted (`genai/vetted.py:1-14`, `provider.py:705-722`) |
| `github` | — | Retired. Skipped with a warning (`provider.py:107`) |

**Paid and trial chains never mix:**
- `paid_provider_names()` = `vertex`, `anthropic` and every contracted provider (`provider.py:919-923`). None of them may
  stand in the trial chain (`provider.py:951-975`).
- Trial-chain providers are never used for a paid organization in staging or production (`provider.py:937-941`;
  `policy.py:121-136`).

**Primary and fallbacks.**
- The primary provider reads `MDARIX_GENAI_*`. A fallback named `x` reads `MDARIX_GENAI_X_*` (`provider.py:288-307`).
- Fallbacks come from `MDARIX_GENAI_FALLBACKS` (`provider.py:1021-1045`).
- A provider that failed rests per process (`_COOLDOWN_UNTIL`) and in the shared table `genai_provider_rest`
  (`provider.py:1004-1018`; `governance.py:427-471`).

### 5.3 Request flow and gates (`GenAIProvider.complete`, `genai/provider.py:351-474`)

Every refusal happens before any network code (docstring, `provider.py:365-382`). The order:

1. The feature must be known: `features.known` raises `GENAI_FEATURE_UNKNOWN` (`features.py:146-153`). A feature
   that is not offered is refused (`provider.py:375-377`).
2. Redaction: `SENSITIVE` regex and `redact_context` (`provider.py:114, 260-270`). PII masking follows when
   `pii.enabled()` (`provider.py:387-391`).
3. Input token cap: `GENAI_INPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT` (`provider.py:394-395`).
4. Nothing configured and no trial chain: controlled mode. No model is called and nothing is read
   (`provider.py:400-401`).
5. Platform feature list `MDARIX_AI_FEATURES` (`features.check_platform`, `provider.py:402`). The default is empty, so
   all features are off.
6. Provider policy: `policy.allowed` (`provider.py:405-413`). In staging and production only `ollama` or a contracted
   provider with zero data retention (ZDR) cleared is allowed. Otherwise `GENAI_PROVIDER_NOT_ALLOWED`.
7. Organization checks in `governance.check_allowed` (`governance.py:194-240`). In order:
   - no tenant scope in production: refused (`GENAI_NO_TENANT_SCOPE`);
   - trial while the trial chain is empty: `GENAI_OFF_FOR_FREE_TRIAL`;
   - `tenants.ai_processing_allowed` false: `GENAI_DISABLED_FOR_ORGANIZATION`;
   - trial and the person's `auth_users.ai_access` is not `ACTIVE`: `GENAI_USER_AI_INACTIVE`;
   - `tenants.ai_features[feature]` is not true: `GENAI_FEATURE_OFF_FOR_ORGANIZATION`;
   - the month's tokens plus this request exceed the budget: `GENAI_MONTHLY_BUDGET_REACHED`.

   Every refusal is counted in `ai_usage_monthly.refused`.
8. Free trial: `sample_gate.check` runs (`provider.py:499`). Then the trial chain runs: no retries, a short timeout
   per provider and a 20 s deadline overall (`provider.py:47-55, 494-541`). If every provider fails or rests, the
   answer is `GENAI_AI_BUSY`.
9. Paid organization: the paid chain runs in order. Resting providers go last (`provider.py:440-474`).
10. Answered: `_finish` records usage and writes the `ai_calls` row (`provider.py:870-876`). **If the call record
    cannot be written, the answer is not shown** (`call_record.py:100-109`).

Stable refusal codes and the words users see are in `REFUSAL_MESSAGES` (`provider.py:131-176`). Every caller catches
`GenAIUnavailable` and falls back to rules.

### 5.4 The AI features (`genai/features.py:47-114`)

| Key | Risk | Offered | Caller (file:line) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ask` | LOW | yes | `backend/app/stage3_workflow_router.py:376` |
| `summaries` | LOW | yes | `backend/app/signals/insight.py:769`, `backend/app/complaint_review/explain.py:145`, `genai/validation.py:74` |
| `case_analysis` | MEDIUM | yes | `investigator/model_provider.py:255`, `hypothesis_engine/model_engine.py:97`, `challenger/model_engine.py:100` |
| `drafting` | MEDIUM | yes | `briefs/model_writer.py:133`, `briefs/service.py:364` |
| `evidence_extraction` | MEDIUM | yes | `ai/extractor.py:136` |
| `code_suggestions` | MEDIUM | yes | `backend/app/authority_reports/ai_codes.py:123` |
| `decision_advisory` | MEDIUM | **no** (`offered=False`) | `decision_center/service.py:229`. Always refused |
| `regulatory_drafting` | MEDIUM | **no** | `regulatory_intelligence/copilot.py:214`. Always refused |

Grep found no other `feature="..."` callers outside tests. Unknowns, failure chains and counterfactuals do not call
`complete` themselves. The case run's failure-chain step is parked (`case_ai/runner.py:38-43`).

### 5.5 `ai/` root

**`ai/extractor.py`**
- `sanitize_evidence_text` (`extractor.py:35-40`) only **flags** the 7 phrases in `PROMPT_INJECTION_KEYWORDS`
  (`extractor.py:24-32`). It returns the text unchanged.
- `AIEvidenceExtractor.extract_evidence` (`extractor.py:57-113`) tries the model first (`_provider_extraction`,
  `extractor.py:115-172`, feature `evidence_extraction`, records = that evidence).
- The model text is cut to the input limit, and a warning says how much was analysed (`extractor.py:127-168`).
- On any failure, or in controlled mode, the rule extractor writes the result. Its provenance is `RULES`
  (`extractor.py:44`).
- Either way, `record_ai_execution` stores who actually wrote the result (`extractor.py:96-111`).

**Caller and endpoint**
- `evidence/services/evidence_service.py:136`.
- Endpoint: `POST /api/v1/evidence/{evidence_id}/process` (`backend/app/evidence/router.py:171`; owned by the evidence
  section).

**`ai/schemas.py`**: `EvidenceExtractionResult` and related types. `extraction_method` (GENAI or DETERMINISTIC) and
`model_provenance` are set by the extractor, never taken from the model (`schemas.py:110-114`).

**`ai/execution.py` `record_ai_execution`** (`execution.py:13-59`)
- Writes one `ai_executions` row.
- `model_version` is never empty or "latest": it falls back to `"unrecorded"` (`execution.py:39-40`).
- Runs the automatic trust check `trust_engine.auto.check_saved` as the row is saved (`execution.py:55-58`).
- The default `orchestration_version="R1-Day8"` is a persisted identifier. Keep it (AGENTS.md naming rule).

**Invariant:** a model-proposed observation counts as evidence only after a person accepts or edits *that
observation* (`ai_review.py:11-17, 118-137`).

### 5.6 Ask MDARIX (two endpoints, only one calls a model)

**A. `POST /api/v1/ask/`** (`backend/app/ask_router.py:206-551` (returns at `:521`)). This is the deterministic foundation. **It never
calls a model.**
- Uses `ask_mdarix.QueryInterpreter` (regex intent and dates, `ask_mdarix/interpreter.py:16-147`) and
  `build_retrieval_plan` (entity allowlist, at most 1,000 records, `ask_mdarix/retrieval.py:22-36`).
- `AuthorizedRetrievalService` and `LifecycleRetrievalService` read tenant-filtered rows through
  `ai_boundary.build_context` (`ask_mdarix/authorized_retrieval.py:14,102`; `ask_mdarix/lifecycle_retrieval.py:9,52`).
- Checks, in order: permission `ai.run` (`ask_router.py:214-226`), tenant active, plan entitlement `ASK_MDARIX`
  (`ask_router.py:73-77`), refusal of external write-back wording (`ask_router.py:107-111`), product, version and
  investigation scope, session ownership.
- Writes audit events `ASK_QUERY_RECEIVED`, `ASK_RETRIEVAL_EXECUTED`, `ASK_QUERY_PROCESSED`,
  `ASK_AUTHORIZATION_DENIED` and `ASK_RETRIEVAL_FAILED`.
- **Dependency failure:** any retrieval exception returns 503 `RETRIEVAL_UNAVAILABLE` with an audit event
  (`ask_router.py:404-422`). `fault_injection.dependency_failure_simulated()` is a server-side test seam
  (`ask_router.py:369-374`). `ask_mdarix/model_boundary.dependency_failure` returns `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`
  (`model_boundary.py:32-33`).
- **Model boundary:** `ask_mdarix/model_boundary.build_model_context` builds the allowlisted context but returns
  `"model_invocation": "NOT_IMPLEMENTED"` (`model_boundary.py:15-35`).
- Client fields `tenant_id`, `role` and `entitlement` are accepted for display only and never used as authority
  (`ask_router.py:43-48, 351-352`).

**B. `POST /api/v1/stage3/interactions`** (`backend/app/stage3_workflow_router.py:249-549`). **This is the Ask screen
and the one that calls the model.**
- The screen calls it in `frontend/src/features/ask/AskMdarixPage.tsx:107`. It uses raw `fetch`, not `requestJson`;
  AGENTS.md asks for `requestJson` in new code.
- Gate: `require_permission(AI_RUN)`, own-records check, rate limit `search:user`.
- Retrieval: `genai.ask.search_evidence` runs a tenant-scoped meaning and structure search, narrowed to the case when
  one is chosen (`genai/ask.py:54-151`). `genai.grounding.build_authorized_context` adds the case context through a
  server allowlist (`grounding.py:20-40, 131`).
- **Abstain:** with no case and no evidence found, `GENAI_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE` is raised and no model is called
  (`stage3_workflow_router.py:358-360`).
- The model call: `provider.complete(prompt, context, feature="ask", records=..., user_text=question)`
  (`stage3_workflow_router.py:373-381`). A large context is compacted to the input budget (`genai/ask.py:191-231`).
- **Answer check:**
  - `read_model_reply` keeps only citations of records the model was given. It drops causal, regulatory-decision and
    certainty statements (`structured_answer.py:1-19, 448-517`).
  - `check_answer` reports unknown citations and causal wording (`genai/ask.py:234`).
  - Invalid JSON is never shown as text (`stage3_workflow_router.py:392-394`).
- The structured answer always contains a "From the records" part. The model part is added only when the model
  answered (`structured_answer.py:1-19`).
- Without a model, counting questions get fixed-rule answers (`_rule_answers`, `stage3_workflow_router.py:478-489`).
- **Persistence and audit:** the trust check `assess_answer`, then an `ai_interactions` row with the full response
  and provenance, then audit `GENAI_EXECUTION_RECORDED` (`stage3_workflow_router.py:490-531`).
- `ai_call_id` is returned so the person can review it (`stage3_workflow_router.py:399-401`).
- Refusals return `status: REFUSED, code, user_message` (`stage3_workflow_router.py:546-548`).
- Other Ask endpoints: `GET /api/v1/stage3/interactions` (`:581`, history) and
  `POST /api/v1/stage3/interactions/{interaction_id}/feedback` (`:552`, writes `ai_interaction_feedback`).

**Frontend:** `frontend/src/features/ask/AskMdarixPage.tsx`, `StructuredAnswer.tsx`, `structuredModel.ts`,
`refusal.ts`, `pendingQuestion.ts`. Tests sit next to them (`*.test.ts(x)`).

### 5.7 Case AI, code suggestions and other model users

**Case AI run**
- `POST /api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/ai-run` (`case_ai/router.py:72`, `ai.run`).
  - `trigger=AUTO` never starts a run (owner decision AI-D6). The answer is `ON_REQUEST_ONLY`.
  - `trigger=MANUAL` while AI does not answer returns `state: AI_OFF`.
  - A run already active returns 409 (`case_ai/router.py:1-11`).
- `GET` on the same path returns the latest run (`case_ai/router.py:61`).
- The runner does ANALYSIS, HYPOTHESES, CHALLENGES, UNKNOWNS, FAILURE_CHAINS (parked) and BRIEF in one background pass
  under `tenant_scope` (`case_ai/runner.py:1-46, 128-215, 309`).
- Each step records whether AI or the rules wrote it (`runner.py:144-154`).
- Runs still active after 30 min count as stale (`runner.py:44-56`).
- Table: `case_ai_runs` (migration `md42caseairun`).

**AI code suggestions**
- `POST /api/v1/reports/{obligation_id}/codes/ai-suggestions` (`authority_reports/router.py:1458`, DECIDERS plus
  `ai.run`).
- `ai_codes.suggest` (`ai_codes.py:99`) keeps a proposal only if:
  - its code is in the newest loaded IMDRF release and in an annex the form asks for, and is still selectable;
  - its quoted words appear in the complaint's description (`ai_codes.py:1-14, 41`).
- Proposals are saved as an `ai_executions` row and labelled `LABEL` (`ai_codes.py:26`). Nothing is entered as a code.
- A person enters each code through `PUT /fields/{id}` with `ai_execution_id`. "Confirm all codes" never confirms an
  AI suggestion (`ai_codes.py:7-9`).
- Trial organizations use sample data only: `records=[complaints/description]` (`ai_codes.py:125`).

**Other model-calling endpoints (owned by other sections; listed so they are not forgotten)**

| Endpoint | Feature |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/v1/triage/signals/{signal_id}/summary` (`signals/router.py:1248`) | `summaries` |
| `POST /api/v1/complaints/{complaint_id}/second-look/explain` (`complaint_review/router.py:109`) | `summaries` |
| `POST /api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/analysis` | `case_analysis` |
| `POST .../hypotheses` | `case_analysis` |
| `POST .../challenges` | `case_analysis` |
| `POST .../briefs` | `drafting` |
| `POST .../decision-advisory` | `decision_advisory` (not offered, always refused) |
| `POST /api/v1/regulatory/draft` (`regulatory_router.py:62`) | `regulatory_drafting` (not offered, always refused) |

**Recorded sample AI (demo-relevant)**
- `backend/app/trial/sample_ai.py:1-29` seeds one AI result on `SAMPLE-INV-001` with provider `sample-recorded`. No
  model is called and no AI usage is counted.
- The UI chip says "Recorded with the sample · no model was called" (`frontend/src/features/shared/AiReview.tsx:10`).
- The result is still not usable downstream until a person accepts it.

### 5.8 Switches, settings and status (who controls what)

| Control | Where it is stored | Default | Changed by |
|---|---|---|---|
| Platform provider | `MDARIX_GENAI_PROVIDER` | `controlled` (`provider.py:290`) | Operator (environment) |
| Platform features | `MDARIX_AI_FEATURES` | empty, all off (`features.py:11, 132-136`) | Operator |
| Trial chain | `MDARIX_AI_TRIAL_CHAIN` | empty, trials get no AI (`provider.py:47, 914-916`) | Operator (owner decision) |
| Organization switch | `tenants.ai_processing_allowed` | `false` (`db/models/foundation.py:73`) | Customer admin or trial owner: `PUT /api/v1/admin/ai-settings`. Platform admin: `PUT /api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/ai-settings` |
| Per-feature switch | `tenants.ai_features` JSONB | `{}` (`foundation.py:76`) | Same endpoints. Needs a reason and fresh re-authentication (`ai_settings.py:20-25, 210-251`) |
| Budget | `tenants.ai_monthly_token_budget` | null, so `MDARIX_GENAI_TENANT_MONTHLY_TOKENS` or 2,000,000 tokens. Trial: `MDARIX_AI_TRIAL_MONTHLY_TOKENS` or 200,000 (`governance.py:33, 87-91, 156-165`) | Platform admin |
| Person's AI flag (trials only) | `auth_users.ai_access` | `ACTIVE` (`foundation.py:961`) | Platform admin: `access/platform_trials.py:256,296,311` |

**`backend/app/ai_settings.py` endpoints**
- `GET /api/v1/admin/ai-settings` (`get_customer_ai_settings`, `:268`). Returns usage, features, `models`,
  `provider_name`, masking and `release_gate`. Also `can_change`, which is false for a trial with no chain.
- `PUT /api/v1/admin/ai-settings` (`:281`). Switching on needs:
  - `acknowledgement: true` (`:134`);
  - the release gate, in production (`:305`);
  - re-authentication (`:309`);
  - for trials, `trial_acknowledgement`.

  Audited `AI_PROCESSING_SETTING_CHANGED` and `AI_FEATURE_SETTING_CHANGED`. Stale `lock_version` returns 409.
- `GET` and `PUT /api/v1/platform-admin/tenants/{tenant_id}/ai-settings` (`:340`, `:351`). Platform admin with
  customer-approved support access (`:345, 364`).
- `GET /api/v1/ai/status` (`:488`), for any signed-in user. Returns `enabled`, `available`, `budget`, `reason_code`
  (`PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` / `AI_DISABLED` / `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED` / `USER_AI_INACTIVE`) and `manual_paths`.
  - Used by `frontend/src/features/workspace/useAiStatus.ts:5`.
  - `ai_ready()` is always false for a free trial (`ai_settings.py:465-474`).

**Provider health**
- `GET /api/v1/stage3/genai-health` (`stage3_workflow_router.py:1173`) returns `GenAIProvider().health()`: configured,
  provider, **model**, fallbacks, resting state.
- `GET /api/v1/analytics/ai/provider-monitoring` (`stage3_router.py:635`).

**Platform console AI endpoints**
- `GET /api/v1/platform-admin/console/ai-activity` (`access/platform_console.py:345`)
- `GET /api/v1/platform-admin/ai/models` (`access/platform_reference.py:42`)
- `GET /api/v1/admin/ai-summary` (`access/observability.py:478`)
- `GET /api/v1/admin/ai-executions` (`access/observability.py:665`)
- Trial AI metrics and access: `access/platform_trials.py:256-344`

**Frontend**
- Customer admin: `features/customer-admin/sections/Security.tsx` (AI processing), `AiReleaseGate.tsx`,
  `AiGovernance.tsx`.
- Platform admin: `features/admin/areas/AiControlCenter.tsx`, `features/admin/customers/TenantPages.tsx`.

### 5.9 Release gate and evaluation

**Release gate** (`backend/app/ai_release_gate.py:1-14`; `GET /api/v1/ai/release-gate`, `:189`, any signed-in user)
- The four bars:
  1. `PROVIDER_CONTRACTED`: the provider is in `MDARIX_GENAI_CONTRACTED_PROVIDERS` and ZDR is cleared (`:39-51`).
  2. `DATASET_SIGNED_OFF`
  3. `GOLDEN_EVALUATION_PASSED` (15 of 15, with the configured model)
  4. `TRUST_CHECK_AUTOMATIC`
- The gate is enforced **only when `MDARIX_ENV=production`** (`:164-168`). There, switching AI on is refused with
  409 `AI_RELEASE_GATE_NOT_MET` (`:171-186`).
- **State on this branch:**
  - `evaluation/` holds only `GOLDEN_DATASET_QUALIFICATION.json`. `GOLDEN_DATASET_HUMAN_SIGN_OFF.json` and
    `AI_RELEASE_RECORD.json` (named in `evaluation/release_record.py:26-28`) are **absent**. So bars 2 and 3 cannot
    be met, and production switch-on is refused.
  - The lock's `reviewer` reads "ai-governance agent (human review pending)".
  - `release_record.py:38-41` refuses sign-off names that read as a tool or an agent. **Gemini must never write the
    sign-off.**

**Evaluation**
- `evaluation/harness.py`: deterministic harness for VS001-VS015 (suite `MDARIX_R1_GOLDEN_SUITE_V2`, dataset
  `r1-ai-golden-v2`, `harness.py:8-13`).
- `evaluation/real_pipeline.py` runs the real investigator and hypothesis pipeline and scores contradictions, unknowns,
  prohibited conclusions, citations and grounding. Adversarial checks are `NO_FORBIDDEN_TEXT` and
  `PERSONAL_DATA_NOT_SENT`. The latter inspects every masked request by observing `GenAIProvider._send`
  (`real_pipeline.py:1-24, 91-118`).
- Ground truth is in `evaluation/ground_truth/VS001..VS015`. The scenario list is in
  `evaluation/scenarios/scenario_index.json`.

**Evaluation endpoints** (`backend/app/evaluation_router.py`)
- `POST /api/v1/evaluations/runs` (`:149`): `ai.run` **and** Platform Admin (SEC-08). Returns 202 and runs in the
  background.
- `GET /api/v1/evaluations/runs/{run_id}` (`:256`).
- `GET /api/v1/evaluations/golden/latest` (`:220`): the latest published `COMPLETED` run of the golden tenant, read
  under system scope. Shown on the Trust page (`features/workspace/sections/TrustEvaluation.tsx`).
- Tables: `evaluation_runs`, `release_assurance_results` (migration `f19b2c3d4e01`).

**Committed artifacts.** There is no `artifacts/eval-*.json` on this branch. The files are:

| File | Passed | Model-written |
|---|---|---|
| `artifacts/real-golden-evaluation.json` | 8/12 | 12 |
| `-v2-groq.json` | 2/12 | 11 |
| `-v3-groq.json` | 3/12 | 2 |

`artifacts/ai-golden-dataset-qualification.json` has status `PASS`, scope "Synthetic internal MVP AI assurance; not
clinical, regulatory, or production certification". These record provider and model names: do not show them in the
demo.

**Trust assurance**
- `GET` and `POST /api/v1/ai-executions/{execution_id}/assurance` (`trust_router.py:49`, `:22`).
- `GET /api/v1/ai/trust-policy` (`trust_router.py:39`).
- `GET /api/v1/ai/assurance/summary` (`stage3_router.py:593`).
- `GET /api/v1/ai/agents/catalog` (`stage3_router.py:567`).
- `GET /api/v1/analytics/ai/cost-reconciliation` (`stage3_router.py:677`).
- `GET /api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/trust/ai-runs` (case_audit router).
- Tables: `assurance_results`, `assurance_checks` (migration `e18a2b3c4d01`).

### 5.10 Retrieval embeddings (`.model-cache`)

- `retrieval/embedding_provider.py`:
  - `MDARIX_EMBEDDING_PROVIDER` is `local` by default: `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5`, ONNX on one CPU thread. The other
    option, `deterministic`, uses word hashes and is for tests (`:213-218`; model constants in
    `backend/app/db/models/retrieval_intelligence.py:20-31`).
  - **Evidence text never leaves the server for embeddings** (`:88`).
  - The cache is `MDARIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_DIR`, default `<repo>/.model-cache` (`:26`). The weights are checksum-pinned.
    The server never downloads them; the API image carries them (`:179-210`).
  - Prepare a local cache once with `python scripts/prepare_embedding_cache.py`. A missing model means
    structured-only search, and the screen says so.
- `config_check` refuses to start staging or production with a missing or changed model
  (`backend/app/config_check.py:190, 441-447`).
- Endpoints:
  - `POST /api/v1/retrieval/index` (`retrieval/router.py:17`, `records.write`)
  - `POST /api/v1/retrieval/query` (`:27`, `ai.run`)
- Tables: `evidence_chunks`, `evidence_chunk_embeddings` (multi-model, migration `md06multimodelembeddings`) and the
  legacy `evidence_embeddings vector(1536)`.

### 5.11 PII/PHI boundary: what may leave, and what is stored

**What may leave**
- Only the masked, redacted request leaves.
- `genai/pii.py` masks emails, phones (including Indian formats), record numbers, dates of birth, names after titles,
  roles or "reported by", age and sex, facilities, and personal dictionary keys. It also masks serial or lot numbers
  in the same sentence as a patient-side identifier (`pii.py:1-27`).
- Masking is forced on in staging and production. `MDARIX_GENAI_PII_MASKING=false` works only in development
  (`pii.py:253-259`).
- Known gap (stated in code): an unusual given name in free text, without a label, title or role word, is not caught
  (`pii.py:15-17`).
- Credentials are redacted from prompt and context by key name and by `key: value` pattern (`provider.py:114, 260-270`).
- Evidence is sent inside `<evidence id="...">` blocks. Tag-like text inside a record is defused so it cannot pose as
  an instruction (`provider.py:199-224`).

**Field allowlists**
- `ai_boundary.py:9-50` has ALLOWED, RESTRICTED (`email`, `phone`, `address`, `display_name`, `organization`) and
  PROHIBITED (passwords, tokens, keys). A tenant mismatch returns an empty context (`ai_boundary.py:84-85`).
- `genai/grounding.py` `ALLOWED_KEYS` applies the same idea to case context.

**Free trials** send unedited `MDARIX_SAMPLE` records only. Every text column is checked against the sample catalogue,
and the typed question has a length cap (`genai/sample_gate.py:1-30`). Otherwise the request is refused with
`GENAI_TRIAL_SAMPLE_ONLY`.

**What is stored**
- `ai_calls` stores the SHA-256 of the prompt and of the masked content, never the content itself (owner decision
  AI-D11). The output is stored as returned (`call_record.py:1-7`).
- `ai_calls` and `ai_output_reviews` are append-only (trigger) under forced row-level security (`ai_records.py:1-11`).
- Hidden chain-of-thought is never stored (`provider.py:1-6, 333`).

**Region**
- Production Vertex must stay in `asia-south1` unless `MDARIX_GENAI_VERTEX_REGION_EXCEPTION=true`
  (`policy.py:139-145`).
- `tenants.ai_processing_region` is only read in `access/platform_tenants.py` (grep). **It is not enforced in the
  provider path.**
- `data_classification.ai_usage_restricted` is only in the model and seed (grep). **It is not read by the AI path.**

**Screens and APIs that show provider or model names** (keep these off camera; "do not name the model"):
- `GET /api/v1/stage3/genai-health`: model in the body
- The release-gate bar detail: `"{provider} {model}"` (`ai_release_gate.py:76`), shown in `AiReleaseGate`
- `frontend/src/features/customer-admin/sections/Security.tsx:320`: "provider: …"
- `frontend/src/features/customer-admin/sections/AiGovernance.tsx:218,237`: "Provider · model"
- `frontend/src/features/workspace/sections/CaseDecision.tsx:547` and `CaseDecisionBrief.tsx:735`: the AI disclosure
  lists provider and model
- `frontend/src/features/workspace/sections/TrustEvaluation.tsx:69,296`: the evaluation model
- `frontend/src/features/admin/areas/AiControlCenter.tsx`: platform admin
- The Ask response JSON carries `provenance.provider/model` (visible in browser dev tools). Not verified: whether
  AskMdarixPage renders it (grep found no render).

### 5.12 Human review and audit

- **Every AI output is reviewed:**
  - `POST /api/v1/ai-outputs/{output_id}/review` (`ai_review.py:180`, `ai.run`) takes `ACCEPTED`, `EDITED` (text and
    reason) or `REJECTED` (reason). DB check constraints enforce these (`ai_records.py:98-104`).
  - The reviewer comes from the session only. Audited `AI_OUTPUT_REVIEWED`.
  - `GET /api/v1/ai-outputs/{output_id}/reviews` (`:250`) lists the decisions.
- **Downstream use:** `usable_output` returns NOT_REVIEWED or REJECTED for unusable model-written results. Rule-written
  results need no review (`ai_review.py:139-155`). The UI component is `frontend/src/features/shared/AiReview.tsx`.
- **Governance:**
  - `GET /api/v1/admin/ai-governance` (`ai_governance.py:350`, admin or trial owner).
  - `GET /api/v1/platform-admin/ai-governance` (`:363`, counts only).
  - `lock_for_signing` freezes the case's AI results and verdicts into the signed decision snapshot
    (`ai_governance.py:11-13, 177`).
- **Audit trail of every AI execution:**
  - `ai_calls` holds every request sent, including failures.
  - `ai_executions` holds every saved AI result, with its trust check.
  - `ai_interactions` holds every Ask, with audit `GENAI_EXECUTION_RECORDED`.
  - `ai_usage_monthly` holds counts and refusals.
  - `case_ai_runs` holds case runs.
- **Labels:** `AIEpistemicLabel` and `AIFallbackNotice` (`genai/epistemic.py:1-10`). AI output is "AI
  interpretation". A rules fallback says why, with `AI_DISABLED`, `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED` or `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`.

### 5.13 Test guard and tests

**Guard** (`tests/conftest.py:76-97`). Unless `MDARIX_TEST_ALLOW_LIVE_PROVIDERS=1`, it forces:
- `MDARIX_GENAI_PROVIDER=controlled`
- empty `MDARIX_GENAI_API_KEY`, `_BASE_URL` and `_FALLBACKS`
- empty Resend and SMTP settings

It also sets `MDARIX_EMBEDDING_PROVIDER=deterministic` (`:61`), `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` (`:33`) and
`MDARIX_GENAI_SHARED_REST=false` (`:75`).

**Gap (risk not verified):** conftest does **not** blank `MDARIX_AI_TRIAL_CHAIN` or per-provider keys
(`MDARIX_GENAI_<NAME>_API_KEY`). `backend/app/db/session.py:12` calls `load_dotenv()`, which fills unset variables
from a local `.env`.
- Tests that touch the trial chain use `tests/fake_ai_gateway.py`, a recording fake `urlopen`, or set or delete the
  variable themselves (for example `tests/test_ai_r_zero_calls.py:59`).
- `test_ai_r_zero_calls.py` also fails on any non-local connection.
- Whether any test could reach a real trial provider with a developer `.env` was **not verified** (no `.env` was
  opened).

### 5.15 Wiring Gemini on Vertex safely (code seams only; switching on is the OWNER's decision)

The provider already exists. Do **not** add a new client.
1. **Settings** (names only):
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_PROVIDER=vertex`
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_PROJECT=<project>`
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_LOCATION` left at `asia-south1`
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_MODEL=<exact pinned id>`. Production refuses moving names such as `-latest` or `auto`
     (`policy.py:91-118`).
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_MODEL_VERSION=<pinned version>`
   - No API key: the token comes from the VM metadata server (`provider.py:78-91, 618-619`).
2. **Contract settings:**
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_CONTRACTED_PROVIDERS=vertex`.
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_VERTEX_ZDR=true` **only once zero data retention is confirmed in writing**, or
     `MDARIX_GENAI_ACCEPT_RETENTION=true` as an owner decision (`policy.py:46-58`).
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_COST_PER_1K_TOKENS > 0`.
   - `MDARIX_GENAI_FALLBACKS` holds only contracted providers, or stays empty.
3. **Features:** `MDARIX_AI_FEATURES=` the features the owner approves (for example `ask,summaries,case_analysis`).
   Never `decision_advisory` or `regulatory_drafting`: they are not offered.
4. **Release gate (production):**
   - A named human signs off the dataset lock: `scripts/qualify_ai_golden_dataset.py --human-sign-off`.
   - `scripts/run_real_evaluation.py --gate --require-model --release-record` must pass 15 of 15 with the Vertex
     model, producing `evaluation/AI_RELEASE_RECORD.json` (`evaluation/release_record.py:1-14`).
   - Not done on this branch.
5. **Per organization:** an admin switches AI on with acknowledgement and re-authentication, then turns on each
   feature. Free trials never reach Vertex: it is a paid provider (`provider.py:919-923`).
6. **Not verified:**
   - that the API container can reach `169.254.169.254` (Docker network);
   - that the VM service account has Vertex permission;
   - that Vertex accepts `reasoning_effort` and `response_format: json_object` for the chosen model.

   Existing fake-network tests cover the code: `tests/test_genai_provider.py:178-235` and
   `tests/test_ai_trial_chain_unit.py:398-447`.
7. **Check before any live call:** `GET /api/v1/stage3/genai-health` and `GET /api/v1/ai/release-gate`. Never show
   either on camera: both name the model.

### 5.16 Invariants (do not break)

- Every model call goes through `GenAIProvider.complete` with a known `feature`. Every refusal raises
  `GenAIUnavailable`, and callers fall back to rules.
- Tenant and actor come from the request context: `set_request_tenant` (`request_context.py:268-271`), or
  `tenant_scope` for background jobs. They never come from the body.
- PII masking is always on in production-like environments. Content hashes only in `ai_calls`. No chain-of-thought is
  stored.
- An answer without its call record is not shown.
- Paid and trial chains stay separate. Gateways run on loopback in production. An unvetted upstream's reply is
  discarded unread.
- AI output is a proposal until a person accepts or edits it. "Confirm all codes" never confirms AI codes. A case run
  never starts automatically.
- `features.py` refuses risk HIGH at import.

## 6. Frontend application and public website

Source read: worktree `C:\Users\ADMIN\MDARIX-R1\.claude\worktrees\trial-132`, branch `feat/app-wide-layout` (0.11.07 candidate, head `4aec8b32`), plus `feat/site-quality` (`74f2921f`) through `git show` for the website. All paths below are relative to `frontend/` unless they start with `backend/` or `access_control/`. Line numbers are from this worktree on 19 Nov 2026 and move when code changes; re-locate by symbol name.

State of the worktree when read (not a code fact, a snapshot): the `feat/site-quality` merge was in progress. `src/site/pages.tsx` and the six `public/site/app/*.webp` screenshots showed as modified in `git status`, but `git diff --stat` showed no content change (line endings only, not verified further). Two local, untracked helper files exist and must not be committed: `vite.awl.config.ts` (dev server on 5251, API on 8151; its own header says "Temporary local config (not committed)") and `e2e/zz-site-shots.spec.ts` / `e2e/zz-site-home.spec.ts` (screenshot helpers, "Temporary local helper (not committed)"). Apart from `site.css` (HEAD has a later hero fix, commit `20006599`) and two e2e specs that exist only on HEAD, `feat/site-quality` and HEAD have the same `src/site/**`, `src/PublicMegaSite.tsx` and `public/site/app/*` (`git diff --stat HEAD feat/site-quality`).

How the endpoint lists in this section were made: a read-only script took every `/api/...` string literal in `src/**` (test files left out), filled in the path constants (`INTAKE`, `REPORTS`, `OVERSIGHT`...) and the dynamic sub-paths (`ai("analysis")`, `/api/v1/auth/${endpoint}`...), and matched them against the 830 operations of Appendix A. 548 of the 749 distinct paths have at least one frontend reference. Where a path is built at run time from a prefix (for example `trustPath(id, part)`), the match lists every route under that prefix and is marked "via path builder"; the exact one used depends on the call. File-level results are in 6.22 (Appendix 6.A). Calls made with `fetch` to non-API static files (`/media/videos/videos.json`) are not in Appendix A.

| Runtime deps | React 19.3.0, react-dom 19.3.0, Vite 8.3.5, `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6.1.1, TypeScript 7.0.2, lucide-react 1.46.0 (icons). No router library, no state library, no CSS framework | `package.json` |
| Dev deps | vitest 5.0.5, `@playwright/test` ^1.63, `@axe-core/playwright` ^4.11, `axe-playwright` ^2.2.2, prettier 3.9.9, postcss + autoprefixer | `package.json` |
| Scripts | `dev` = `vite --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5177`; `build` = `tsc && vite build && vite build --ssr src/site/prerender.tsx --outDir dist-prerender --emptyOutDir && node scripts/prerender.mjs`; `test` = `vitest run`; `test:e2e` = `playwright test`; `test:e2e:install`; `lint` = `tsc --noEmit --noUnusedLocals -p .`; `format` / `format:check` (prettier); `check:phone` = `node scripts/check-phone.mjs` | `package.json` |
| Lint | There is **no ESLint**. "Lint" is the TypeScript compiler with `--noUnusedLocals`. `tsconfig.json`: strict, target ES2020, `moduleResolution: Bundler`, `jsx: react-jsx`, `include: ["src"]` | `package.json`, `tsconfig.json` |
| Format | Prettier, `printWidth` 120, `endOfLine: auto`; ignores `dist/`, `dist-prerender/`, `node_modules/`, `artifacts/`, `test-results/`, `public/`, `package-lock.json` | `.prettierrc.json`, `.prettierignore` |
| Vite | `__MDARIX_VERSION__` is defined from `../VERSION` (used by the new-version notice). React, react-dom and scheduler go into their own `react` chunk. Dev server sends security headers and a dev CSP, and proxies `/api` and `/health` to `http://127.0.0.1:8007`. Vitest excludes `e2e/**` | `vite.config.ts:16-48` |
| Pre-render | After the app build, the website's pages are rendered on the server side (`src/site/prerender.tsx`) and written into `dist/` for every `<loc>` in `public/sitemap.xml`: `/` becomes `dist/home.html`, other pages `dist/<path>/index.html`, plus `dist/404.html`. Inline styles are moved into one stylesheet because the production CSP allows no inline style. `dist/index.html` stays the plain app shell that Caddy serves for `/app`, sign-in and every other address | `scripts/prerender.mjs:1-40` |
| Vitest environment | No jsdom or happy-dom: component tests render with `react-dom/server` `renderToStaticMarkup` and test pure functions | `grep` over `src` |
| Playwright | `testDir: ./e2e`, `workers: 1` (one shared test account), `timeout` 30 s, `retries` 2 on CI, reporter HTML to `../artifacts/playwright-report` plus list, `channel` defaults to installed `chrome` (`PLAYWRIGHT_CHANNEL` overrides), trace kept on failure, screenshots on failure, video off. **Projects: `chromium` (Desktop Chrome) and `mobile-chromium` (Pixel 5, the phone project).** Web server: `E2E_SERVER=dev` (default, `npm run dev`) or `preview` (build then `vite preview`); `E2E_PORT` default 5177; `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` skips starting a server | `playwright.config.ts` |
| Phone check | `npm run check:phone` opens every workspace section/tab and the main website pages at 390x844, fails on sideways scroll or a visible code/UUID, reports controls under 44 px. Needs a running app; the workspace part needs `MDARIX_SESSION` (a session cookie value supplied at run time, never committed) | `scripts/check-phone.mjs:1-12` |

## 6.2 Boot and top-level URL routing

`src/main.tsx:10-14` mounts `<App/>` inside `AppErrorBoundary` with `createRoot` (not `hydrateRoot`: the pre-rendered website markup is replaced in one render, `main.tsx:6-9`).

`src/app/App.tsx` (`Page()`, lines 94-123) routes on `window.location.pathname` only; there is no router library.

| Address | What renders | Loaded | Indexed by search engines |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/signup` | `SignupPage` (free-trial request) | in the first script | yes (`applyPageMeta("/signup")`, `App.tsx:98`) |
| `/signin`, `/forgot-password`, `/activate-account`, `/reset-password`, `/mfa` | `AuthPage mode=<path without slash>` | lazy | no (`PRIVATE_PATHS`, `App.tsx:89`) |
| `/request-demo` | `RequestDemoPage` only if `DEMO_REQUESTS_OFFERED`, else `SignupPage`. It is `false` today (`src/site/offer.ts:4`) | lazy | no |
| `/help/pilot-guide`, `/help/trial-guide`, `/help/user-guide` | `PilotGuidePage`, `TrialGuidePage`, `UserGuidePage` (`features/help/paths.ts`) | lazy | not verified |
| `/app` and `/app/...`, `/platform-admin` and `/platform-admin/...` | `PrivateApplicationGate` (6.3) | lazy | no |
| anything else | `PublicMegaSite path=...` (website, legacy redirects, legal pages, "not found") | first script | per `site/seo.ts` |

Notes:
- There is no `/activate` route. Activation links are `/activate-account?token=...` (`backend/app/trial/service.py:461`, `backend/app/access/platform_customer_admins.py:473`). A bare `/activate` falls through to the website and shows "not found" (from the code path, not run).
- Every lazy screen uses `lazyPage` (`App.tsx:17-43`): if a script chunk fails to load after a deploy, the page reloads once (tracked in `sessionStorage` key `mdarix.reloadedForScript`), and a second failure shows the error boundary.
- `NewVersionNotice` is shown on every page (`App.tsx:80-86`); it polls `GET /api/v1/public/version` (`features/shared/appVersion.ts:10`) and offers a reload. It never reloads by itself.
- Sign-in reasons: `/signin?reason=idle|expired|unsaved&returnTo=<path>` (built by `signInPath`, `app/apiClient.ts:95-104`; read in `features/auth/AuthPage.tsx:34-40`). MFA: `/mfa` (verify) and `/mfa?setup=1` (enrol); `MfaAlreadyOn.tsx` handles setup for someone who already has an authenticator.

## 6.3 The signed-in gate and the consoles (`src/app/PrivateApp.tsx`)

1. `PrivateApplicationGate` (lines 76-130) calls `GET /api/v1/auth/session` with `redirectOnUnauthorized: false`. If `mfa_state` is `VERIFY` it goes to `/mfa`; if `ENROLL` it goes to `/mfa?setup=1`. A server outage (status 0 or 5xx) shows "MDARIX is not reachable, retrying" and retries with backoff (2 s doubling, at most 30 s). `TRIAL_ENDED` lets the person through so the trial-ended page can show. Any other refusal goes to sign-in (`afterSessionRefusal`, lines 359-364).
2. `PrivateApplication` (lines 149-356) reads `GET /api/v1/session/context` (role, personas, allowed menu, `feature_set`, `navigation`, memberships, entitlements). It sets the tab title to `<tenant> · MDARIX`.
3. Who gets what:
   - Business users go to `UserWorkspace` (the workspace shell, 6.7), with `ConsentBanner` (lines 283-295).
   - A customer administrator on the new navigation also gets `UserWorkspace`, with Administration in the avatar menu (`adminInWorkspace`, `app/consoleChoice.ts`). On the classic navigation they switch between the console and the workspace, and the choice is remembered (`rememberMode`).
   - A platform administrator gets `SimplifiedPlatformAdminScreen` (6.13). The old platform addresses in `CUSTOMER_CONSOLE_VIEWS` (lines 34-69) open the matching console section, for example `admin-manage` opens "Identity & Access:Customer administrators".
   - Standalone screens still mounted here: `RegulatoryCopilot`, `RiskTraceabilityWorkspace`, `SignatureVerificationWorkspace`, `Stage2AdminControlPlane`, `PlatformProductConfiguration`, `SubscriptionAdministration`, `MappingStudio`, `PlatformAdministrationAudit` (lines 334-350). Under `feature_set = mvp` most of them are parked (`app/featureSet.ts:26-37`).
4. Role names are checked in `app/navigationPolicy.ts:99-108`: platform admin is `ADMIN`, `ADMINISTRATOR`, `MDARIX ADMINISTRATOR`, `PLATFORM ADMIN` or `PLATFORM_ADMIN`; customer admin is `CUSTOMER ADMIN` or `CUSTOMER_ADMIN`. `deployment_role` `PLATFORM` hides the workspace and `CELL` hides platform screens (`viewsForDeployment`). This is only about what the interface offers; **the server authorizes every call** (AGENTS.md).

## 6.4 App views, URL routes and the link table

- `src/app/router.ts` defines `AppView` (73 view keys, lines 1-74) and `APP_ROUTES` (lines 76-156). `viewFromPath` (line 158) maps a path to a view; `/app/complaints/<ref>` is the Complaints view. `navigateTo` (line 174) uses `history.pushState` and fires `popstate`.
- Workspace addresses: `/app` (home), `/app/my-work`, `/app/complaints`, `/app/complaints/<ref>`, `/app/investigations` (`?case=<ref>`), `/app/product-360`, `/app/trends`, `/app/approvals`, `/app/decision-trees`, `/app/reports`, `/app/records`, `/app/settings`, `/app/team`, `/app/account`, `/app/getting-started`, `/app/import` (Import your data), `/app/import-data`, `/app/feedback`, `/app/look-ahead`, `/app/next-steps`, `/app/customer-admin` (Administration). Classic ones: `/app/readiness`, `/app/products`, `/app/signals`, `/app/evidence`, `/app/decision`, `/app/assurance`, `/app/audit`, `/app/story`, `/app/persona`, `/app/ask`.
- Platform addresses: `/app/admin`, `/app/admin/{stage2,product-configuration,subscriptions,mapping-studio,customers,customers/new,customers/detail,customer-admins,audit}` and `/platform-admin/...` (customers, tenants, onboarding, licenses, tenant-health, tenant-audit, `data-configuration/*`, `integrations/*`).
- `src/app/linkTable.json` (read by `app/linkTable.ts`, checked on the server side by `tests/test_link_targets.py`) is where every link lands. It holds `places`, `records`, `oldViews`, `oldSections`, `oldTabs`, `viewIds` (column-choice ids), `newPlaces`, `newMenu`, `newRecords`, `newHomes` and `classicHomes`. **Old addresses are kept permanently** (e-mails already sent, bookmarks, videos), so never delete entries. `newMenu`: main = My Work, Complaints, Investigations, Product 360, Approvals; top = Records, Team, Settings, My Account, Administration.
- Tabs ride in the address (`?tab=`, `?section=`, `?step=`, and record parameters `ref`, `case`, `report`, `signal`, `request`, `intake`, `product`, `supplier`, `capa`, `communication`, `complaint`): `features/workspace/workspaceAddress.ts`, `newRecords` in `linkTable.json`.
- `feature_set` (`full` or `mvp`) and `tenants.navigation` (`classic` or `new`; since UX2 every organization defaults to `new`) decide the menu. `UserWorkspace.tsx` only runs the new shell when `feature_set` is `mvp` and `navigation` is `new` (line 501: `navigation = featureSet === "mvp" ? navigationOf(...) : "classic"`).

## 6.5 API client conventions (`src/app/apiClient.ts`)

- Use `requestJson<T>(path, options)` (line 135) for new calls (AGENTS.md). `postJson(path, body)` (line 229) is a shortcut. `api<T>(path)` (`app/api.ts`) is a GET wrapper.
- Defaults: `credentials: "same-origin"` (cookie session); a 15 s timeout (`timeoutMs`); GET retries once, non-GET never (`retries`); JSON `Content-Type` only for string bodies (FormData keeps its own).
- `AI_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000` (line 226) is used for AI actions.
- Errors become `ApiError(message, status, code, requestId)`. The message comes from the server's `detail` (string, a 422 list made readable, or `{message}`) or a plain fallback per status (lines 47-77). The request id comes from `detail.request_id`/`correlation_id` or the `X-Request-ID` header and is offered in "Report a problem". `describeError` (line 234) adds "(reference ...)".
- A 401 on a private page that is not a credential call sends the browser to `/signin?reason=...&returnTo=...` once. `unsaved` is used when a save was refused (lines 185-196).
- A 403 `MFA_REQUIRED` goes to `/mfa`; `MFA_ENROLLMENT_REQUIRED` goes to `/mfa?setup=1`, except for the action calls in `ACTION_MFA_CALLS` (`/legal-hold`, `/activation-link`) (lines 110-118).
- `background: true` sends `X-MDARIX-Background: 1`, so a poll does not keep the session alive (server side: `request_context.py`). Successful foreground calls notify `onApiActivity` listeners (the idle sign-out watch).
- Screen hook: `useResource<T>(path | null, {refreshWhile, statusPath, retry})` and `send(path, method, body)` in `features/customer-admin/shared.tsx:147,238`. Most workspace screens use these. A `null` path means "do not read".
- **Known exceptions that use raw `fetch`:**
  - `features/auth/AuthPage.tsx` (sign-in, MFA, activation, reset; it needs the raw response) and `features/auth/SignupForm.tsx`, `RequestDemoPage.tsx`.
  - `features/ask/AskMdarixPage.tsx:107` (`/api/v1/stage3/interactions`).
  - `features/reports/download.ts:56` (file downloads).
  - The website: `site/legal.tsx` (`/api/v1/public/site-info`, `/api/v1/public/legal/{document}`), `site/visuals.tsx` (`/api/v1/public/testimonials`) and `site/videos.tsx` (`/media/videos/videos.json`).
- Client errors are reported by `app/ErrorBoundary.tsx` with `POST /api/v1/client-errors`.

## 6.6 Shared types and other `src/app` modules

- `app/types.ts` holds shared shapes: `Product`, `InvestigationSummary`, `TemporalMode` (`current|event|known`), `TimelineEvent`, `Product360`, `WorkspaceEvidence`, `InvestigationWorkspace`, `AnalysisItem`, `InvestigationAnalysis`, `InvestigationBrief`, `Hypothesis(Set)`, `UnknownSet`, `FailureChainSet`, `ChallengeSet`, `EvidenceSummary`, `DecisionContext`, `AdminTenant`. Workspace-specific types live in `features/workspace/workspaceContext.tsx` (`SessionContext` with feature switches `email_intake`, `routine_path`, `challenge`, `closed_loop`, `rule_register`, `b9.*`, `b6.*`, `rca.*`, `sample_only`, `trial_owner`, `persona*`).
- Other modules:
  - `dates.ts`: all date and time display (6.17).
  - `labels.ts`: human labels for codes.
  - `displayId.ts`: business ids shown, never UUIDs.
  - `featureSet.ts`: parked views, sections, tabs and admin areas under `mvp`.
  - `navigationPolicy.ts`: role to views.
  - `consoleChoice.ts`: customer-admin console or workspace.
  - `roles.ts`, `format.ts`, `chips.tsx`, `tableSort.tsx`, `ui.tsx`.
  - `qrCode.ts` and `QrCodeSvg.tsx`: the MFA QR code, drawn locally.
  - `ErrorBoundary.tsx`: `AppErrorBoundary` and `SectionErrorBoundary`; each screen is wrapped and reset by `resetKey`.
- Tests in `src/app/*.test.ts(x)` (19 files): router, link table, click paths, console choice, feature set, navigation policy, dates, labels, display ids, page scale, QR code, the "session ended on save" redirect, apiClient and PrivateApp.

## 6.7 The workspace shell (`features/workspace/UserWorkspace.tsx` + `HybridShell.tsx`)

`UserWorkspace` (export at line 480) owns navigation, the work context (product, version, case, point in time: `useWorkContext.ts`, `ContextBar.tsx`) and sign-out. It provides `WorkspaceContext` (`workspaceContext.tsx`). The new shell is drawn when `fresh` (new navigation). Every screen renders inside `SectionErrorBoundary` and `ColumnScopeProvider` (column choice per screen).

**Top bar, left to right** (`UserWorkspace.tsx:~855-918`):
- organization name (tooltip: only this organization's data);
- the "Sample data" chip during a trial (line 862; hidden on Getting started, `trialShellShows`, `HybridShell.tsx:1033`);
- "Sample data · Your data" choice when both exist (`DataChoice`, line 425; kept in `localStorage` `mdarix.dataShown.<tenant>.<user>`, lines ~396-414);
- "Import your data" button (`ImportOwnData`; only in a trial and for someone who may change records, `importOwnDataAction` line 384);
- `GlobalSearch` (line 228): complaints, products, reports, investigations, trends;
- `PersonaSwitch` ("Viewing as", line 137);
- `ThemeMenu` (Light/Dark/System);
- `AlertsBell` (`sections/Reminders.tsx`);
- `HelpMenu` (`features/shared/HelpMenu.tsx`);
- `AvatarMenu` (line 452).

**Rail (`NavRail`, `HybridShell.tsx:765`)**:
- Order: brand mark, then **Getting started** (trial only) with a progress ring ("n of 10 completed"; 7 while the flagship story leads: `STORY_STEPS = 10`, `FLAGSHIP_STEPS = 7`, `oneStory.ts:107-108`), then one group "Work" with the five areas in order (`RAIL_GROUPS`, line 645).
- Area labels (`PERSONA_TABS`, line 53): Today (`home` opens My Work), Cases (`complaints` opens Complaints), Investigations, Products (opens Product 360), Decisions & Reports (opens Approvals, or the first screen of the area the person may open: `areaEntry` in `areas.ts:35-39`).
- **Captions** (`TAB_STORY`, line 606): Today "Your work today", Cases "The signal", Investigations "Why it happened", Products "What changed", Decisions & Reports "Decide, report, prove". Captions are `aria-hidden`; the button's name stays the area label (`appLook.test.tsx` "the story rail").
- 0.11.07 look (`appLook.css` part 3): each area is a step in a ring on one line. The line fills up to the open area through CSS `:has(... [aria-current="page"])`.
- **Folding**: a button "Collapse the menu"/"Expand the menu" (local state, not stored). The rail also folds by itself under 1240 px (`appLook.css` `@media (max-width: 1240px)`, `NavRail` doc comment). Icons keep their names for screen readers and tooltips.
- A screen opened from a link that is not in the persona's areas is shown as an extra tab marked "is-extra" (`shownTabs`, line 654).

**Red dot rule (owner, 5 Nov 2026)** (`railAlerts`, line 667; `AlertDot`, line 671):
- A dot appears, never a count, on Cases while any complaint is overdue, and on Products while any product version is rising against its history.
- Screen readers hear " · New items", which is also the tooltip.
- The two reads start 1.5 s after the page (`ringRead`, `UserWorkspace.tsx:797-811`): `GET /api/v1/complaints?view=overdue&page=1&page_size=1` (`home.ts:41`, filtered by the data source) and `GET /api/v1/triage/trends?group=version` (`signalAi.ts:149`). "Rising" means a result of `SIGNIFICANT` or `ELEVATED`.
- On the phone, "More" carries the dot when any hidden area has one.

**Phone bottom bar (`PhoneBar`, line 856)**:
- Slots: `PHONE_SLOTS = ["start","home","complaints","products"]` (line 650), so Getting started, Today, Cases and Products where the persona has them. "More" opens the rest above the bar.
- The doc comment there still says "Trends"; Trends is now inside Products, so the comment is stale but the code is right.
- On the phone, Import your data and the data choice move into the avatar menu.

**Avatar menu (`AvatarMenu`, line 452; `AVATAR_SCREENS`, line 598)**:
- Items: name, Account, [Import your data (phone)], [Data shown (phone)], **Records & trust**, **Team**, **Administration** (customer admins only, `SECTION_MENU_KEYS` `customer-admin`), Import data, Settings, [Admin console (classic customer admin)], Switch organization (only when the e-mail has accounts in several organizations), Sign out.
- Records & trust and Administration are deliberately not work areas (UX2).

**Area menu (`AreaNav.tsx`)**: an area with more than one screen shows a small `<nav aria-label="<Area> pages">` (not a tab list):
- Today: My Work ("Today"), Look ahead, Next steps.
- Products: "Products and suppliers", Trends.
- Decisions & Reports: Approvals, Decision trees, "Authority reports".
- Screen lists are in `areas.ts:9-23`.

**Page head, hero header and figures**:
- Shown unless the screen has its own head: a complaint page, Today, Next steps, Getting started in a trial, one product, supplier or report (`pageHeadShown`, `HybridShell.tsx:1012`).
- Contents (`UserWorkspace.tsx:~1118-1150`): the eyebrow (screen name), the heading as a question (`NEW_HEADS`, line 380, e.g. Cases "What needs you first?", Investigations "Why is it happening?", Trends "Is a product problem rising?", Approvals "What waits for your signature?", Records "Records an inspector can trust"), the guide line (`NEW_NAV`, line 350) and, on pages that show times, "Times are in <zone>, your organization's time zone." (`ZONE_SECTIONS`, line 1001: Complaints, Decision trees, Reports, Investigations, Trends, Records, Settings).
- **Head figures** (`HeadFigures`, line 447):
  - Cases shows "complaints overdue" and opens the Overdue view.
  - Product 360 and Trends show "products rising against their history" and open Trends' "Trend check".
  - The values are the rail's own reads, so there is no second server call. They count up once (`CountUp`, line 423); with reduced motion the value shows at once.
- 0.11.07: the band is an ink-to-teal gradient (`--uw-hero-band: linear-gradient(135deg,#0a1a2f 0%,#0f2e4a 55%,#0b5560 100%)`, `appLook.css` part 1 and 4) with the page's icon. While an investigation is open, its own dark header (`.uw-inv-head`) is the one hero, and the page band is hidden from sight but kept for screen readers (`appLook.css` end; test "one hero per screen").

**Other shell parts**:
- `ColleagueWelcome` (invited colleague's first screens).
- `FeedbackPrompt` (trial).
- `FlagshipBar` / `StoryBar`: the walkthrough bar, not on Today (`trialShellShows`).
- `ChapterDone` banners: classic navigation only.
- Idle sign-out: `useIdleSignOut` and `SessionTimeoutWarning` (two-minute warning: "Stay signed in" / "Sign out now").
- Skip link "Skip to content" to `#app-main`.
- `ContextBar`: inside "Change product or version" / "Change investigation" only on Trends tabs (`contextPickerShown`, line 1027).

## 6.8 Personas, "Viewing as" and the Today screens

Personas are defined on the server: `access_control/personas.py:73-140` (`WORKSPACE_PERSONAS`). The client gets them in `session.persona_options` and `persona_tabs`.

| Code | Label | Role | Areas (rail) | Today shows |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `admin` | Admin (trial owner, sees everything) | LEADER | all five | `HomeSection`, led by the flagship story; legal clocks |
| `quality` | Quality Leader | LEADER | all five | `HomeSection`, led by the flagship story |
| `regulatory` | Regulatory Reviewer | REGULATORY_REVIEWER | Today, Cases, Decisions & Reports | `HomeSection`, lead "Open the nearest clock"; legal clocks |
| `servicedesk` | Service Desk | SERVICE_DESK | Today, Cases | lead "Log a call" (opens Cases "Calls to check?log=1") |
| `handler` | Complaint Handler | TRIAGE_LEAD | Today, Cases | lead "Triage next complaint" (Cases "To triage") |
| `investigator` | Investigator | INVESTIGATOR | Today, Cases, Investigations, Products | lead "Open the investigation"; open investigations card |
| `pqe` | Product Quality Engineer | PRODUCT_QUALITY_ENGINEER | Today, Investigations, Products | **`ProductToday`**: the Product Lifecycle Twin on the version rising most (`sections/ProductToday.tsx`) |
| `risk` | Risk Reviewer | RISK_REVIEWER | Today, Investigations, Products | `RiskReviewsCard` plus lead "Start/Continue the risk review"; open investigations |
| `decision` | Decision Maker | APPROVER | Today, Cases, Investigations, Decisions & Reports | `ApprovalsCard` plus lead "Review and sign" |
| `executive` | Executive (view only) | VIEWER | Today, Investigations, Products, Decisions & Reports | **`PortfolioToday`**: exposure per product, read-only (`GET /api/v1/portfolio`, `sections/Portfolio.tsx:45`) plus the readiness line |

- Where the code sits: `UserWorkspace.tsx:1199-1210` picks `PortfolioToday` / `ProductToday` / `HomeSection`. Persona leads are in `sections/HomeWork.tsx:115-215` (`PersonaLead`, `OWN_LEAD` at line 72). Home cards are in `sections/Home.tsx:57-240`. Home reads overdue complaints, `/api/v1/oversight?show=all`, the trend check, and readiness for the executive.
- **Switching** (`PersonaSwitch`): only the trial owner may switch. The choice is saved with `POST /api/v1/me/persona` (`UserWorkspace.tsx:756-759`) and kept for the next sign-in. Anyone else sees their persona with a lock ("set by the trial owner"). An invited colleague who holds several personas sees only theirs.
- The menu note says: "Changes what you see, not what you may sign. Signing follows your own role." The persona is never sent to the server as a permission (`HybridShell.tsx` header comment).
- Section visibility also needs the server's `allowed_menu` keys (`SECTION_MENU_KEYS`, `workspaceContext.tsx:81`) and `business_permissions`. Trial-only screens (Getting started, Look ahead, Next steps, Import your data) need `trial.is_trial` (`TRIAL_ONLY_SECTIONS`, line 71).

## 6.10 The investigation cockpit (6-step rail)

`sections/Cockpit.tsx` (UX3) replaces the earlier progress line and tab lists. Each step's content is in `sections/CaseSections.tsx`.

- **Summary strip** (`cockpitFigures`, line 41; `Cockpit`, line 91), "The investigation in four figures":
  - Evidence: n for, n against, n missing.
  - Possible causes: n, and n leading.
  - Blocking the decision: open readiness checks, and the first one.
  - Next: the one next step.
  
  Each figure opens the step that holds its records.
- **Rail** (`CockpitRail`, line 182, `<nav aria-label="Investigation steps">`): the six `CASE_SECTIONS` (`caseWorkflow.ts:458`), each with a question (`STEP_QUESTION`, line 469):

  | Step | Question |
  |---|---|
  | Signal | What happened? |
  | Product reality | What changed in the product? |
  | Evidence | What do we know, and what not? |
  | Possible causes | What could have caused it? |
  | Brief | What should we decide? |
  | Decision & action | What was decided, and did it work? |

- Address tabs (`linkTable.json` `newPlaces.Investigations.tabs`): the six steps, plus All investigations, Summary, Decisions & actions, History (older names that still open).
- Below each step, tools sit in named `Fold`s that load only when opened. AI output is always labelled as a proposal (`shared/StatementKind.tsx`, `shared/AiReview.tsx` "AI proposal, not reviewed" with Accept, Edit and Reject). The **decision signature** asks for password and authenticator code; the server enforces this.
- When AI is off, `AiOffNote` (`investigator/InvestigationWorkspacePanel.tsx`) names the manual step. Tests: `aiOffAssurance`, `analysisHiddenWhileAiOff`.

## 6.11 Getting started, Import your data, sample data labels

- **Getting started** (`sections/GettingStarted.tsx:97`):
  - Reads `GET /api/v1/trial/status`, the trial story (`TRIAL_STORY_PATH`), `/api/v1/trial/feedback`, the demo request, and the one story `GET /api/v1/trial/one-story` (`oneStory.ts:16`).
  - The **flagship** (W1): SAMPLE-INV-001, OrbitCare 300 Rev B, **seven steps**: Signal, Product reality, Evidence, AI investigation, Investigation Brief, Human decision, Action and impact (`sections/OneStory.tsx:37-45`). Steps 1-3 tick when their screen loads; the others tick from saved records (`oneStory.ts:64-67`).
  - Otherwise the **ten-step walkthrough** (`STORY_STEPS = 10`), with buttons such as "Start with the hospital's call", "Open the triage", "Open the decision tree", "Open the report", "Open Records" and "Open the OrbitCare 300 trend" (`OneStory.tsx:74-103`).
  - After step 7, the effectiveness check's synthetic follow-up waits for a person's signed result (`effectivenessStages`, `oneStory.ts:390-395`).
  - Also on the page: "What were you cited for?" (`CitedFor.tsx`), the trial journey (`TrialJourney.tsx`), quick rating, Discuss a pilot, "Sample data & trial settings" (folded, `GettingStartedMore.tsx`), restart or remove the sample (trial owner only, `RemoveSample.tsx`), end the trial early (`EndTrial.tsx`), book a demo (`BookDemo.tsx`), support access (`TrialSupportAccess.tsx`).
  - The rail ring shows progress (6.7).
- **Import your data** (`/app/import`, `sections/YourData.tsx:96`):
  - The upload (`ImportPanel`, one server path `/api/v1/data/import...`), the data-quality report of each import, and Automatic insights (`/api/v1/trial/insights`).
  - After an import, one main button "See your complaints". Removing the sample is offered, never forced.
  - A sample-only trial (`sample_only`) or a person who may not change records is told it is not available. The server refuses either way.
- **Sample data labels**:
  - Top bar chip "Sample data" with `TRIAL_DATA_NOTE` as its tooltip (`UserWorkspace.tsx:862`; classic header line 971 says "Sample data" or "Practice records").
  - "Sample data · Your data" switch.
  - Sample references are prefixed `SAMPLE-` (e.g. SAMPLE-INV-001, SAMPLE-RV-11).
  - On the website every screenshot and drawn chart carries `SAMPLE_LABEL = "Illustrative · synthetic sample data"` (`site/motion.tsx:7`).
  - Rule (visual direction 0.11.07): sample data stays labelled.

## 6.12 Customer administration (`features/customer-admin`)

- `CustomerAdminControlPlane.tsx` is the console. It runs inside the workspace shell on the new navigation (avatar menu, Administration) or full-screen on the classic one.
- Sections (`context.tsx:5-17`) are grouped in `ADMIN_GROUPS` (`CustomerAdminControlPlane.tsx:115-125`):
  - Organization: Dashboard, Organization, Plan & Subscription, Notifications.
  - People & access: Users, Roles & Access.
  - Data & connections: Integrations, Data Mapping. Both are parked under `mvp` (`featureSet.ts:72`).
  - AI & security: Security.
  - Audit: Audit, Reviews.
- Files are `sections/*.tsx`. Extra panels: `EmailIntake.tsx`, `SupportAccess.tsx`, `AiGovernance.tsx`, `AiReleaseGate.tsx`, `summaries.tsx`.
- It uses `access.identity`, `access.governance`, `access.configuration`, `access.catalog`, `access.observability`, `access.tenant_settings`, `ai_settings`, `ai_governance`, `audit_chain_api`, `signed_reviews.router` (access, audit-trail and configuration reviews with a signature), `support_access_api`, `tenant_export_api`, `mail_in.router` and `trial.router`.
- Tests: `aiGovernance`, `aiSettings`, `consoleTokens`, `customerAdminModel`, `emailIntake`, `exportPersonal`, `idColumns`, `reviews`, `supportAccessModel`, `sections/Users.test`, `sections/summaries.test`.

## 6.13 Platform administration console (`features/admin`)

- `SimplifiedPlatformAdminScreen.tsx` is the console, for `/app/admin` and the old `/platform-admin/*` addresses. Its areas are in `DOMAINS` (`admin/consoleDomains.ts`):

  | Area | Sections |
  |---|---|
  | Platform Overview | `console/AdminOverview.tsx`, charts |
  | Customers & Tenants | Customers, Tenants (`customers/*`: directory, customer page, add tenant, tenant pages with AI settings and legal hold, customer admins, CSV import, edit details, support workspace under approved support access) |
  | Trial organizations | Requests, Trials, Ending and deletion, Feedback (`trials/*`, `TrialRequestsWorkspace.tsx`, `TrialFeedbackWorkspace.tsx`, `DemoRequestsWorkspace.tsx`, `MvpRequestsWorkspace.tsx`, "Run deletion now") |

- Calls go mainly to `access.platform_*`, `access.observability`, `access.identity`, `ai_release_gate`, `trust_router`, `cell_link_api`, `legal_hold_api`, `support_api`, `support_workspace`, `tenant_export_api`, `trial.router`, `trial.feedback` and `demo_requests` (Appendix 6.A).
- Tests are in `features/admin/**/*.test.*` (25 files), for example `adminMenu`, `customerApproval`, `trialRequestDetail`, `TenantPages`, `trialsPaging`, `trialMfaReset`, `TrialAiPanel`, `operations`, `problemReports`. e2e: `deep-crawl` (platform persona), `pilot-features` (legal hold).

## 6.15 Design tokens, palette, motion, reduced motion, dark mode

- **One token file**: `src/tokens.css`. It holds colours, type scale, spacing, radii, shadows and self-hosted fonts (Inter for text, Archivo for display, IBM Plex Mono for references; OFL licence files in `public/fonts`). Its header (lines 1-21) defines the colour roles.
- Two rules there: **red (`--ms-crit`) is only for overdue or critical, never decoration**; harm has its own hue (`--ms-harm`). `tokens.test.ts` checks WCAG 2.1 AA: text 4.5:1 on every ground, white on every fill, 3:1 for controls and marks, and the same for the dark theme.
- **0.11.07 palette** (`tokens.css:288-311`) applies only while the app shell is on screen in the light theme (`:root:not([data-theme="dark"]):has(.uw-hybrid)`):

  | Token | Value |
  |---|---|
  | ground | `#f4f7fb` |
  | line | `#dce3ec` |
  | teal (brand) | `#0b7a85` (5.1:1 on white) |
  | navy, rail, hero | `#0a1a2f` |
  | rail-2 | `#1e3350` |
  | hero-2 | `#0f2e4a` |
  | glow | `#22b8c4` |
  | h-good | `#10b981` |

  `appLook.css` adds `--uw-brand-400 #22b8c4`, `--uw-signal-400 #38bdf8` and the hero gradient. Source: `MDARIX-planning/whif-demo/ux-review/visual-direction-0.11.07.md`. The website and the classic navigation keep the earlier set.
- **`features/workspace/appLook.css`** (0.11.07, scoped to `.uw-hybrid`):
  1. palette;
  2. canvas fluid to 1680 px, centred, gutters `clamp(20px, 2.6vw, 48px)`;
  3. story rail;
  4. ink-to-teal hero band;
  5. calm motion once per screen: `uw-rise` 320 ms with a 40 ms stagger, `uw-roll` for figures, hover lift. All of it is inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`, and `reduce` turns transitions off.
  
  Other style sheets: `heroShell.css`, `heroPages.css`, `workspace.css`, `areaNav.css`, `productHub.css`, `styles.css` (11,754 lines, the legacy base), `base.css`.
- **Motion rules** (visual direction): CSS, SVG and the Web Animations API only, no new dependency; 150-400 ms for UI; nothing loops forever in the app; no layout shift (head figures have a fixed size); nothing delays a click.
- **Reduced motion**:
  - App: `CountUp` shows the value at once (`UserWorkspace.tsx:423-441`).
  - Site: `useReveal` and `countUp` do nothing under reduce, and `HeroStory`'s resting state is the finished story (`site/motion.tsx:62-64,113,134-162`).
  - CSS media queries are in `appLook.css`, `workspace.css`, `hero.css`, `site.css` and `styles.css`.
  - Tests: `appLook.test.tsx` ("moves only without a reduced-motion preference", "show the value at once with reduced motion").
- **Dark mode status**:
  - It exists for the new-navigation shell only. `ThemeMenu` offers Light, Dark or System (default System), stored per user in `localStorage` key `mdarix.theme.<user_id>` (`features/workspace/theme.tsx`). The shell sets `data-theme` on `<html>` while it is on screen.
  - The website, the auth pages and the classic navigation stay light (`tokens.css` dark block comment, lines ~225-231).
  - The 0.11.07 palette and hero band were designed for the light theme. How they look in dark mode is not verified (no dark-mode screenshots in the 0.11.07 e2e).

## 6.16 Accessibility rules in code

- Landmarks and names:
  - Skip links: app "Skip to content" to `#app-main`; site `.ms-skip` to `#main`.
  - Rail and phone bar are `<nav aria-label="Destinations">`; area menus are labelled `nav`s, not tab lists.
  - Every icon is `aria-hidden` and its button keeps a text name.
  - The current item is `aria-current="page"`.
  - Pop-ups (persona, avatar) close on Escape or a click outside (`useDismiss`).
  - Dialogs trap focus (`shared/DialogBehaviour.tsx`).
  - Tabs move with the arrow keys and Home/End (`shared/a11y.test.ts`).
  - Chip filters use roving tabindex.
  - Drawers go from h2 to h3 (`workspace/accessibility.test.tsx`).
- Status is never shown by colour alone (badges carry words; dots carry "New items"). Disabled controls say why beside them (`boardParts.tsx` `Why`).
- Codes and UUIDs are never shown as visible text (`src/test/noCodes.test.tsx`, `check:phone`). Controls must be at least 44 px on a phone (reported by `check:phone`).
- **axe checks** (WCAG 2a, 2aa, 21a, 21aa; serious or critical fails):
  - `e2e/public-shell.spec.ts` (website shell);
  - `e2e/browser-evidence.spec.ts`;
  - `e2e/trial-journeys.spec.ts` through `noSeriousAxe` and `accessible` in `e2e/support/journey.ts:124-131,320`, which also checks named controls (`namedControls`) and keyboard walks (`keyboardWalk`).

## 6.17 Time zone display (IST)

- `src/app/dates.ts`: dates as "3 Oct 2026" (month name, never 03/10); times 24-hour (`hourCycle: "h23"`).
- On the new navigation the workspace reads `GET /api/v1/oversight/settings` and calls `setDisplayZone(time_zone)` (`UserWorkspace.tsx:505-506`). Every time is then shown in the organization's zone, which is IST (`Asia/Kolkata`) for the Indian sample organization (the value comes from Settings › Working calendar, not hard-coded).
- `zoneName()` gives "India Standard Time", and the page head names it once ("Times are in India Standard Time, your organization's time zone."), only on `ZONE_SECTIONS`.
- Deadline labels come from the server with their zone ("Mon 30 Nov 2026, 23:59 IST"). `zoneless()` drops the zone where the head already names it.
- The classic navigation uses the browser's zone. `formatUtc` is used for audit and signature records. `labels.ts:59-61` shows "Asia / Kolkata" (and maps the old name Asia/Calcutta).

## 6.18 Public website

**Routing**: `src/PublicMegaSite.tsx`.
- Pages: `SITE_PAGES` (`site/pages.tsx:24-32`) = `/`, `/platform`, `/trust`, `/company`, `/videos`, `/questions`, plus 7 question pages (`site/questions.tsx:38-305`):
  - `/questions/how-to-trend-medical-device-complaints`
  - `/questions/when-is-a-complaint-reportable`
  - `/questions/post-market-data-and-the-risk-file`
  - `/questions/complaint-signal-vs-trend`
  - `/questions/show-capa-effectiveness`
  - `/questions/try-complaint-trending-on-your-own-data`
  - `/questions/does-mdarix-replace-our-qms`
- Info pages: `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/trial-agreement`, `/security`, `/accessibility`, `/contact`. Anything else is the "not found" page (served with 404 by Caddy from the pre-rendered `404.html`).
- `LEGACY_PAGES` and `FORMER_SECTIONS` redirect old addresses with `history.replaceState`, for example `/pricing` and `/plans` go to `/contact`, `/roadmap` to `/platform#availability`, and `/platform/<capability>` to a section. `MOVED_ANCHORS` handles moved anchors.
- `resolveSitePath` is tested in `site/routing.test.ts`. The sitemap (`public/sitemap.xml`, `https://mdarix.com/...`) lists the 20 indexed pages, `/signup` included.

**Header** links: Product (`/platform`), Trust, Questions, Company, Sign in, and the CTA "Start free trial" (`/signup`). The CTA is "Request a demo" only if `DEMO_REQUESTS_OFFERED`, which is false.

**Footer** columns:
- Product: How it works, What you get (`/platform#availability`), Markets and regulations (`/trust#markets`), Trust.
- Learn: Questions and answers, Free trial videos.
- Company: About MDARIX, Contact.
- Legal: Privacy, Terms, Free trial agreement, Security, Accessibility.

The footer also has "Start free trial", company name, address and support e-mail from `GET /api/v1/public/site-info` (not hard-coded), and the trademark line.

**Pages** (`site/pages.tsx`):
- Home (`HomePage`, line 206):
  - Hero "Get every complaint to a defensible decision, and the right report on time in every market." with CTAs "Start free trial" and "See how it works", three points, the app screenshot `/site/app/hero.webp` in a browser frame (`Shot`) and the animated `HeroStory` card. The card sits under the screenshot (commit `20006599`, e2e `site-hero.spec.ts`).
  - A facts strip naming 21 CFR 803, EU MDR Art. 87, UK MDR 2002, SOR/98-282 and India MDR 2017.
  - The five-step sample story "Why did pressure-alarm complaints rise after OrbitCare 300 Rev B?", each step with a screenshot and an "In MDARIX: <area>" link. The `SignalChart` (4 before, 15 after, 9.3x expected) comes from the sample data.
  - "Beside your QMS", `CustomerVoices`, and the CTA band (`/signup`, `/contact`).
- How it works (`PlatformPage`, line 318): the five work areas, each with its screenshot (`AREA_SHOTS`: hero, signal, investigation, product, decision), `#beside-your-qms` and `#availability`.
- Trust (`TrustPage`, line 405): `#principles`, `#ai`, `#markets` (`MarketBasis` from `shared/standards.json`, with a disclaimer), `#security`, `#integrations`.
- Company (`CompanyPage`, line 549): `#fit`, `#start` ("A free trial first. No demo call needed.", CTAs `/signup` and `/contact`), `#after-the-trial`, `#learn`.
- Videos (`site/videos.tsx`) reads `/media/videos/videos.json` (served outside the app bundle; not verified).
- Legal pages read `GET /api/v1/public/legal/{document}` (`site/legal.tsx:191`).

**Screenshots**: `public/site/app/{hero,signal,investigation,product,decision,effectiveness}.webp`, captured from a trial's sample data at 2x (`pages.tsx:147,196`). `site/home.test.tsx:9,70` checks that every image used is one of the shipped files under `/site/app/`. Older marketing images live in `public/site/medtech-*.webp` and `public/visual-*.svg`.

**Motion and look**:
- `site/motion.tsx`: `Shot`, `HeroStory`, `useReveal` (reveal on scroll, count-up of `data-count` numbers, draw-on charts; all skipped under reduced motion).
- `site/visuals.tsx`: `SignalChart`, `MarketBasis`, `CustomerVoices`.
- `site/site.css` (2,044 lines, the 0.11.07 palette).
- `site/seo.ts`: per-page title, description, canonical and robots. Private pages are `noindex, nofollow`.
- `site/prerender.tsx` is the server-side entry.

**Honesty rules, enforced in code** (`site/claims.test.tsx`; `site/content.ts:1-3` comment):
- No certification claims. SOC 2, ISO 27001 certification, "Part 11 compliant", "HIPAA compliant" and "certified" are all forbidden, except in the sentences that say MDARIX does *not* claim them. Trust must contain "MDARIX is not yet a validated (GxP) system." and "MDARIX does not claim ISO 27001 certification".
- No price: no currency amounts, USD/INR, "per month" or "per year". `/pricing` redirects to `/contact`. `content.ts:135` describes the pilot as "priced for your scope ... in a written proposal" and names no figure.
- No customers, logos or invented results: no "trusted by", "customers like", "% faster/fewer/less" or ROI.
- **`CustomerVoices` shows nothing until a platform administrator has approved at least one real trial customer's quote** (`GET /api/v1/public/testimonials`, `site/visuals.tsx` "Customer voices"). Never seed invented ones.
- AI only proposes and a person decides and submits. "An AI proposal is never a signature." and "AI may propose; a person reviews." must be present. "MDARIX files/submits the report" is forbidden.
- No warning letters or class actions are named anywhere in the website source (owner rule).
- Every app picture is labelled "Illustrative · synthetic sample data".
- Competitor and product names do not appear (owner rule); the footer carries a generic trademark notice.
