## 6.21 Backend endpoint modules with no frontend caller (API-only)

These are modules from Appendix A that no `src/**` file references. Gemini should treat them as API-only, used by scripts, webhooks, other systems or tests:

| Module | Endpoints | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `backend.app.ask_router` | `POST /api/v1/ask/` | Ask uses `/api/v1/stage3/interactions` instead |
| `backend.app.communication_router` | `GET /api/v1/communication/provider-health`, `POST /api/v1/communication/resend/webhook` | The webhook is called by the mail provider |
| `backend.app.integration_router` | `POST /api/v1/integrations/preview`, `GET /api/v1/integrations/health/{connector_type}` | |
| `backend.app.retrieval.router` | `POST /api/v1/retrieval/index`, `POST /api/v1/retrieval/query` | |
| `scenario_intelligence.router` | `POST /api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/scenarios` | The "Constrained scenario" panel calls `counterfactuals`, not `scenarios` |

Every other module of Appendix A has at least one reference. Caveats:
1. The module list may wrongly include a module that is only reached through a path builder whose run-time sub-path never hits it. Those cases are marked "via path builder" in 6.22.
2. Inside a called module many single endpoints have no caller: 201 of 749 distinct paths have no reference.
3. Also API-only: `/health` (proxied by Vite), the OpenAPI pages, and the server-to-server and platform-provisioning routes that the console does not use. Check Appendix A's Guards column before changing any of them.

## 6.23 Not verified, and risks for whoever changes the frontend

- Not verified by running anything. This section was written from source only: no `npm`, no server, no browser. Screen behaviour described here is what the code says.
- The `feat/site-quality` merge was in progress in this worktree. Re-check `src/site/pages.tsx` and `public/site/app/*.webp` after the merge commit.
- Dark theme with the 0.11.07 palette and hero band: not verified.
- `/media/videos/videos.json` and the `public/help` assets are served outside the bundle: not verified.
- The PhoneBar doc comment (`HybridShell.tsx:854`) names "Trends" while the code uses Products. The code is correct; the comment is stale.
- Do not remove entries from `src/app/linkTable.json` or `LEGACY_PAGES`: e-mails, bookmarks, videos and guides depend on them.
- Do not add certification wording, prices, logos, invented quotes or numbers to the website: `src/site/claims.test.tsx` fails the build gate (`npm test`).
- New API calls go through `requestJson`. Never send a tenant id or actor from the client: the server takes them from the session (AGENTS.md).

## 7. Infrastructure, integrations, operations and quality gates

Scope: the 0.11.07 candidate, branch `feat/app-wide-layout` (`VERSION` = `0.11.07`), read from the worktree
`.claude/worktrees/trial-132`. Every statement cites the file it comes from. "Not verified" means it was not checked
in code or by running anything; nothing was run for this section (no docker, servers or tests). Older
`docs/handover/*` pages are out of date; the code and `docs/runbooks/` win.

Rules that override everything below (AGENTS.md): tenant and actor come only from `get_request_context`; business
data via `get_data_plane_db`, identity/tenancy/config via `get_db`; authorization on the server; never commit
secrets or `.env`; every behaviour change ships with a failing-first test; no new names after day/stage/part numbers.

### 7.1 Runtime architecture

#### 7.1.1 Compose files

| File | Purpose | Started how |
|---|---|---|
| `docker-compose.yml` | Local development stack: control DB, 4 tenant data-plane DBs, outbox worker, MinIO, Mailpit, 14 provider simulators | `docker compose up -d` (README.md) |
| `docker-compose.prod.yml` | Production (and staging) application stack, project name `mdarix` | `docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml --env-file .env.production ...` (header of the file) |
| `docker-compose.staging-oracle.yml` | Layer on the prod file: Postgres on the server with TLS, ARM ClamAV image. **Production on GCP also uses this layer** (Postgres runs on the VM) | `COMPOSE_FILE=docker-compose.prod.yml:docker-compose.staging-oracle.yml` (RELEASE_0.11.05.md, GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md section 1) |
| `infrastructure/security-scan/docker-compose.scan.yml` | Disposable copy for attack-style scans, project `mdarix-scan`, ports on 127.0.0.1 only | only via `scripts/security_scan.sh` |

#### 7.1.2 Production services (`docker-compose.prod.yml`)

Shared anchors: `x-app` (image `${MDARIX_IMAGE_REPOSITORY:-mdarix}/api:${MDARIX_RELEASE:-latest}`, `Dockerfile.api`,
`env_file: .env.production`, `read_only: true`, tmpfs `/tmp` 256m, `no-new-privileges`, `cap_drop: [ALL]`, network
`internal`), `x-logging` (json-file, 10 x 20 MB), `x-db-ca` (`${MDARIX_DB_CA_DIR:-./certs}` -> `/etc/mdarix/certs:ro`),
`x-ai-forwarder` (same API image, 64m, 0.25 CPU, runs `scripts/ai_loopback_forwarder.py`).

| Service | Image / command | Ports | Health check | Volumes | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `web` | `mdarix/web` (`Dockerfile.web`: Caddy 2.11.7 rebuilt with patched Go deps + built frontend in `/srv`) | 80, 443, 443/udp | `wget http://127.0.0.1:2019/reverse_proxy/upstreams` every 30 s | `caddy_data`, `caddy_config`, `./videos:/media/videos:ro` | Runs as uid 10002, no capabilities, `net.ipv4.ip_unprivileged_port_start=80`; env only `MDARIX_DOMAIN`, `ACME_EMAIL`, `MDARIX_SITE_ACCESS`, `MDARIX_SITE_LOGIN_USER/HASH`, `MDARIX_WWW_REDIRECT`, `MDARIX_REDIRECT_DOMAINS`; depends on `api` healthy and `web-volumes` done |
| `web-volumes` | `mdarix/web`, entrypoint `caddy-volumes` (`infrastructure/docker/caddy-volumes.sh`) | none (`network_mode: none`) | none | caddy volumes | One-shot chown of Caddy volumes (root -> uid 10002); runs as 0:0 with only CHOWN, DAC_READ_SEARCH |
| `migrate` | API image; entrypoint swaps in owner credentials; `alembic upgrade head && configure_runtime_role.py && grant_runtime_privileges.py && set_rls_system_token.py && migrate_data_planes.py --apply` | none | disabled | db-ca | Only service (with `backup`) that loads `.env.migrate`; `MDARIX_DB_HARDENING_APPLY=1`; `restart: "no"`; api and background wait for it to exit 0 |
| `api` | API image; `uvicorn backend.app.main:app --workers 2 --no-proxy-headers --no-access-log --timeout-graceful-shutdown 20 --timeout-worker-healthcheck 30` (Dockerfile.api CMD) | 8000 internal only | Dockerfile `HEALTHCHECK` on `http://127.0.0.1:8000/health/live` every 15 s | `objects:/var/lib/mdarix/objects`, `reports:/var/lib/mdarix/reports`, `./legal:/var/lib/mdarix/legal:ro`, db-ca | Networks `internal` + `ai-host`; sets `MDARIX_TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS=1`, `MDARIX_CLAMD_HOST=clamav`, `MDARIX_LEGAL_DIR`, `MDARIX_OBJECT_STORAGE_ROOT`, `MDARIX_REPORT_TEMP_ROOT`; 2 GB, 2 CPU; depends on `migrate` done and `clamav` healthy |
| `background` | API image; `python scripts/run_background.py` (outbox worker + scheduler as two child processes) | none | both heartbeats fresh: worker JSON < 60 s, scheduler file < 120 s; start period 120 s | objects, reports, db-ca, legal | 1.5 GB, 1.5 CPU, `cpu_shares: 256`; `stop_grace_period: 60s`; if either child dies the container exits (run_background.py) |
| `ai-loopback-api` | API image, forwarder `3011=10.99.211.1:3011`, `20128=10.99.211.1:20128` | shares api's network namespace | UDP "connect" to 10.99.211.1 | none | Must be recreated when `api` is recreated: `docker compose up -d --force-recreate ai-loopback-api ai-loopback-background` (comment in the file) |
| `ai-loopback-background` | same, in background's namespace | - | same | none | same |
| `ai-host-bridge` | forwarder on host network, listens on 10.99.211.1 only, forwards to host 127.0.0.1:3011/20128 | host network | disabled | none | Bridge `mdarix-ai`, subnet 10.99.211.0/29; host firewall must allow only this bridge to reach 3011/20128 |
| `mail-in` | API image; `python -m backend.app.mail_in.receiver` | `25:2525` | SMTP NOOP on 127.0.0.1:2525 | objects, db-ca, legal, `${MDARIX_MAIL_IN_CERT_DIR:-./certs/mail-in}` | **Profile `mail-in`**, OFF unless `MDARIX_EMAIL_INTAKE=on`; not running in production (see 7.3.2) |
| `clamav` | `clamav/clamav:1.4@sha256:57deb1...` (x86 only); staging/GCP layer overrides with `clamav/clamav-debian:1.4@sha256:6d0680...` | 3310 internal | `clamdcheck.sh` every 60 s, start period 900 s | `clamav_db` | `CLAMD_CONF_ConcurrentDatabaseReload=no`, 1.5 GB |
| `backup` | `mdarix/backup` (`infrastructure/docker/backup/Dockerfile`: postgres:16-alpine + age + aws-cli + curl) | none | none | `${MDARIX_BACKUP_DIR:-/var/backups/mdarix}:/backups`, `objects:/objects:ro`, db-ca | **Profile `backup`**; loads `.env.production` + `.env.migrate`; entrypoint `mdarix-backup` (backup.sh) |
| `postgres` (staging/GCP layer only) | `pgvector/pgvector:pg16@sha256:7b822b...` with `ssl=on`, TLS >= 1.2, own `pg_hba.conf`, scram-sha-256 | none published | `gosu postgres pg_isready` | `postgres_data`, `./staging-secrets/db-tls/*`, `infrastructure/staging/pg_hba.conf` | admin password from Docker secret `./staging-secrets/postgres-admin-password`; no PITR (comment in file) |

Networks: `internal` (default bridge), `ai-host` (fixed bridge name `mdarix-ai`, 10.99.211.0/29, gateway
10.99.211.1). Volumes: `caddy_data`, `caddy_config`, `objects`, `reports`, `clamav_db` (+ `postgres_data` from the layer).

#### 7.1.3 Images and Dockerfiles

| File | What | Key facts |
|---|---|---|
| `Dockerfile.api` | API, background, migrate, mail-in, forwarders (one image) | `python:3.13-slim-bookworm` pinned by digest; `pip install --require-hashes`; embedding model `Qdrant/bge-small-en-v1.5-onnx-Q` at pinned revision baked into `/opt/mdarix/embeddings`, `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1`; user `mdarix` uid 10001; `MDARIX_ENV=production` baked in |
| `Dockerfile.web` | Caddy front door + built SPA | node:22 build stage (`npm ci`, `npm run build`, reads `../VERSION`); golang 1.26.10 stage rebuilding Caddy v2.11.7 with `golang.org/x/net v0.62.0` and `go-jose/v4 v4.1.6`; final `caddy:2.11-alpine` |
| `Dockerfile.worker` | Local dev only (service `mdarix-outbox-worker` in `docker-compose.yml`) | copies only the packages the worker imports |
| `infrastructure/docker/backup/Dockerfile` | Backup/restore tools | `backup.sh` -> `/usr/local/bin/mdarix-backup`, `retention.sh`, `restore.sh` -> `mdarix-restore`, `scripts/check_backup_age.sh` -> `mdarix-check-backup-age`; runs as uid 70 |
| `source_simulator/Dockerfile` | Provider simulator (local only) | `uvicorn source_simulator.persistent_app:app --port 8099` |

#### 7.1.4 Caddy front door (`infrastructure/docker/Caddyfile`)

- Automatic TLS (Let's Encrypt, `ACME_EMAIL`); `MDARIX_SITE_ACCESS` = `public` or `private` (basic auth except
  `/health/live`, `/health/status`, `/health/version`); optional `www` redirect and `MDARIX_REDIRECT_DOMAINS` 301s.
- Body limits: 25 MB generally, 1 MiB for JSON or no Content-Type.
- Security headers: HSTS (1 year, includeSubDomains, no preload), nosniff, `X-Frame-Options DENY`, strict CSP
  (`script-src 'self'`, no inline), COOP/CORP same-origin, Permissions-Policy; `Server` removed.
- `/health` and `/health/ready` answer **404** publicly (operator-only); `/health/live`, `/health/status`,
  `/health/version` proxied to `api:8000`.
- `/api/*`: `Cache-Control: no-store`, proxied with `X-Forwarded-Proto` and `X-Client-Certificate-Fingerprint`
  (cell mTLS, set by Caddy, never from the client).
- `/media/videos/*` from the mounted `./videos`; `/assets/*` immutable for a year; pre-rendered pages
  (`/home.html`, `/platform/index.html`); app-shell regex list (`/app`, `/platform-admin`, `/signin`, ... ) rewrites to
  `/index.html`; everything else is the 404 page with status 404. **If you add a client-side route, update the
  `@shell` regex here and `frontend/src/site/routing.test.ts` keeps them in step** (comment in Caddyfile).

#### 7.1.5 Development stack (`docker-compose.yml`)

| Service | Port(s) | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `postgres` (`pgvector/pgvector:pg16`, container `mdarix-r1-postgres`) | 5433 | volume `mdarix-r1-postgres-data`; holds the demo DB `mdarix_r1` and the test DB `mdarix_r1_test` |
| `tenant-a-data` .. `tenant-d-data` | 5541-5544 | DBs `mdarix_tenant_a..d`, user `mdarix_data`, password `${MDARIX_DATA_PLANE_PASSWORD}` |
| `mdarix-outbox-worker` | - | `Dockerfile.worker`; heartbeat < 30 s; `MDARIX_OUTBOX_MAX_ATTEMPTS` default 5 here (code default 12) |
| `minio` (`minio/minio:latest`, unpinned) | 9000, 9001 | optional S3-compatible store; CI does not use it |
| `mailpit` (`axllent/mailpit:v1.27`) | 1025 SMTP, 8025 UI | catches local mail |
| 14 provider simulators | 8101-8114 (container 8099) | TRACKWISE, PLM, ERP, SUPPLIER, VEEVA_QMS, TEAMCENTER, ORACLE_ERP, COUPA (each own `postgres:16` DB), SAP_ERP, WINDCHILL, MES, LIMS, RISK, SALESFORCE_SERVICE (shared `source-systems-db`); admin token `${MDARIX_SIMULATOR_ADMIN_TOKEN}` |

There is no API or web container in the dev stack: run uvicorn on 8007 and Vite on 5177 (README.md).

### 7.2 Production hosting (from the runbooks)

Source: `docs/runbooks/GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md`, `DEPLOYMENT.md`, `RELEASE_0.11.05.md` (base), `RELEASE_0.11.06.md`,
`RELEASE_0.11.07.md`, `BACKUP_AND_RESTORE.md`. Identifiers below are placeholders.

- **One VM** on Google Cloud, Mumbai region (`<zone>` in asia-south1), `e2-standard-2` (2 vCPU, 8 GB), Ubuntu 24.04,
  50 GB disk, static IP; VM name `<vm-name>`. Caddy, API, background, ClamAV and PostgreSQL all run on it
  (GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md section 1).
- **Access**: SSH only through Identity-Aware Proxy (`gcloud compute ssh <vm-name> --zone <zone> --tunnel-through-iap`);
  VPC firewall opens 80/443 only to the web tag. Docker's port publishing bypasses `ufw`, so the cloud firewall is the
  outer layer (DEPLOYMENT.md 2.1).
- **Deploy user and directory**: user `deploy`, checkout `/home/deploy/mdarix` (`~/mdarix`); `COMPOSE_FILE` and
  `COMPOSE_ENV_FILES=.env.production` exported in deploy's `~/.profile`; enter with `sudo -iu deploy`.
- **Database**: `postgres` service from the staging layer, database `mdarix`, TLS `verify-full` with its own CA
  (`infrastructure/staging/prepare-database.sh`). **No point-in-time recovery: RPO 24 h today**, RTO 4 h
  (BACKUP_AND_RESTORE.md top).
- **Files**: `MDARIX_OBJECT_STORAGE_PROVIDER=local` (encrypted on the `objects` volume), up to 24 h of uploads at
  risk; the template default is `s3` for pilots (GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md, `.env.production.example`).
- **Images are built on the VM** from the checked-out tag (`MDARIX_PUBLISH_IMAGES` off, `MDARIX_IMAGE_REPOSITORY=mdarix`).
  Release = `git checkout v0.11.0x` + `MDARIX_RELEASE=v0.11.0x` in `.env.production` + `dc build` (RELEASE_0.11.05.md step 4).
- **Shorthands** (RELEASE_0.11.05.md):
  ```bash
  dc() { docker compose "$@"; }     # COMPOSE_FILE / COMPOSE_ENV_FILES from ~/.profile
  ro() { docker compose exec -T -u postgres -e PGOPTIONS='-c default_transaction_read_only=on' postgres \
           psql -X -A -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d mdarix -c "$1"; }   # read-only SQL
  ```
- **Disk check before any build** (the 0.10.47 build died on a full disk): `df -Pk / | awk 'NR==2 { exit ($4 >= 8*1024*1024) ? 0 : 1 }'`
  -> `DISK=OK` / `DISK=STOP`; free space by removing old `mdarix/*:v*` images by release name, never backups.
- **Backup**: `dc --profile backup run --rm backup; echo BACKUP=$?`, then off-site copy
  `sudo /snap/bin/gcloud storage rsync --recursive /var/backups/mdarix gs://<bucket>/prod` (bucket in asia-south2,
  versioning, retention policy, lifecycle delete after 89 days), optional disk snapshot
  (`gcloud compute disks snapshot ...`); daily snapshot schedule 14 days.
- **Manifest check** (restore-verify without the private key): `sha256sum -c MANIFEST.sha256` in the newest
  `/var/backups/mdarix/<UTC stamp>/`, size near previous (~12 MB in Nov 2026), `gcloud storage ls` shows the off-site copy.
  Full restore needs the offline `age` private key (laptop only).
- **Smoke checks** (RELEASE_0.11.05.md step 7, 0.11.07 step 3): `curl https://<domain>/health/status` (ok/degraded,
  200), `curl https://<domain>/api/v1/public/version` (must print the release, route in `backend/app/site_info.py`),
  `/health/ready` must be **404** from outside, the former domain 301s; on the VM the readiness report
  (`dc exec -T api python -c "from backend.app.readiness import readiness_report as r; ..."`) and
  `dc exec -T api python scripts/deploy_gate.py --release v0.11.0x --timeout 180 --require worker,scheduler`
  (`DEPLOY_GATE=PASS`). E-mail storm check on `event_outbox` (unsent count must not grow).
- **Rollback** (RELEASE_0.11.05.md section 10): **A** = no migration ran: restore `.env.production.pre-<rel>`, check out
  previous tag, `dc up -d --no-build --wait` if anything was recreated. **B** = migration failed: read
  `alembic_version`, fix-forward or go to C. **C** = migrated but cannot serve: `alembic downgrade <prev>` with the new
  image if nothing customer-made, else restore from snapshot or backup (+ `reapply_erasures.py`). Old images are removed
  by release name after acceptance (step 11), keeping current and rollback target.
- **Monitoring** (GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md, DEPLOYMENT.md section 6): external uptime check on `/health/status` every 5 min;
  dead-man heartbeats for scheduler (`MDARIX_SCHEDULER_HEARTBEAT_URL`, 5 min / 15 min grace) and backup
  (`MDARIX_BACKUP_HEARTBEAT_URL`, daily / 2 h grace); app alerts to `MDARIX_ALERT_WEBHOOK` / `MDARIX_ALERT_EMAIL`.
- **Trial AI gateways on the host** (RELEASE_0.11.05.md step 1, `scripts/install_trial_ai_gateways.sh`): FreeLLMAPI on
  127.0.0.1:3011 and OmniRoute on 127.0.0.1:20128 as systemd units `mdarix-freellmapi`, `mdarix-omniroute` under user
  `mdarix-gw`. **OmniRoute 3.8.52 has a known critical vulnerability; loopback binding is the only control. Never bind it
  to 0.0.0.0, open its port, or put it behind Caddy/tunnels.**
- **Production pause / owner gate**: every WRITES step on production is OWNER-approved; per RELEASE runbooks Claude runs
  steps only in the owner's session after explicit go. Treat production as owner-only for Gemini as well.
- Staging alternative: Oracle Always Free ARM (`docs/runbooks/STAGING_ORACLE.md`), private site with basic auth,
  `MDARIX_PRIVATE_STAGING_ACKNOWLEDGED=true`. Not verified whether it currently runs.

### 7.3 External integration points

#### 7.3.1 SMTP outbound (`auth/emailer.py`)

- One SMTP path for every e-mail (STARTTLS), plain text + HTML from structured blocks; returns `SENT`,
  `NOT_CONFIGURED` (no SMTP account, dev only) or raises `EmailDeliveryError` without credentials (docstring).
- Settings: `MDARIX_SMTP_HOST`, `_PORT` (587), `_USERNAME`, `_PASSWORD`, `_FROM`, `_VERIFIED_DOMAIN`;
  `MDARIX_EMAIL_DELIVERY=off` (never hand mail to SMTP; refused in staging/production; `mdarix_whif_*` DBs are always off).
  Any SMTP provider works; the one in use is named in GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md.
- What sends mail: invitations and activation, password reset, security notices (`handle_security_notice`), trial
  reminders/declines/invitation retries, subscription and lifecycle notices, work notices, urgent alerts, connector sync
  failures, AI budget thresholds, mail-intake limits, feedback, support access, team changes, readiness digest,
  deadline list, support problem reports (`backend/app/notifications.py` `_handle_event`, `scripts/run_outbox_worker.py`
  `publish`). Almost all go through the outbox (7.3.5), so a stopped `background` delays e-mail but loses nothing.
- Local: Mailpit (1025/8025). Journeys: `scripts/journey_api.py` writes `.eml` files to `MDARIX_JOURNEY_MAIL_DIR`
  instead of sending; it refuses production.
- `communication/email/resend_provider.py` and `inbound.py`: optional Resend inbound evidence e-mail (settings
  `RESEND_*`). Two webhook routes exist: `POST /api/v1/communication/resend/webhook` (signature-verified, body capped,
  `backend/app/communication_router.py`) and `POST /api/v1/stage3/resend/webhook` (persisting,
  `backend/app/stage3_workflow_router.py`). Whether Resend is configured in production: not verified (the GCP runbook
  lists only SMTP).

#### 7.3.2 Mail-in (e-mail to case, B7b)

`backend/app/mail_in/` (`receiver.py` on `aiosmtpd`, `processing.py`). OFF unless `MDARIX_EMAIL_INTAKE=on` and the
compose profile `mail-in` is started; needs inbound port 25, an MX record for the intake subdomain
(`MDARIX_INTAKE_DOMAIN`, default `intake.mdarix.com`) and a STARTTLS certificate. Only known active intake addresses
accepted (550 otherwise), 25 MB per message, 30 msgs/h per IP, org daily limit (200 default); raw message to object
storage, row in `inbound_messages`, outbox event `INBOUND_MAIL_RECEIVED` processed by the worker (attachments are
virus-scanned). Off: routes 404 and the screen is hidden. Production status: off (operations tasks not done, per
`mail_in/__init__.py` docstring).

#### 7.3.3 Object storage (`object_storage/service.py`)

- Backends `local` (AES-GCM encrypted files under `MDARIX_OBJECT_STORAGE_ROOT`; dev default `.mdarix-object-storage/`,
  untracked) and `s3` (any S3-compatible bucket, SSE `AES256`). Keys and signed URLs never cross a tenant prefix;
  download links signed with `MDARIX_OBJECT_STORAGE_SIGNING_SECRET`.
- Key rotation with a keyring (`security/keyring.py`, `MDARIX_OBJECT_STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_ID` / `_KEYS`,
  `scripts/reencrypt_object_storage.py --apply`); keep old keys as long as backups made with them are kept
  (ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLES.md).
- Limits: 10 MB per file, 100 MB per tenant, retention 2555 days by default.
- The module docstring still says production must use s3; the live GCP deployment uses `local` (GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md).

#### 7.3.4 Virus scanning (ClamAV)

Every upload is scanned with clamd `INSTREAM` over TCP (`MDARIX_CLAMD_HOST`, `_PORT` 3310, `_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`);
any failure to get a clean verdict refuses the file (`object_storage/service.py`). Alternative
`MDARIX_MALWARE_SCANNER_COMMAND` (stdin, exit 0 = clean). The API will not start in prod without one scanner, and the
`api` service waits for `clamav` healthy (compose). A fresh signature volume can take > 10 minutes (start period 900 s).

#### 7.3.5 Outbox and background worker (`outbox/worker.py`, `scripts/run_outbox_worker.py`)

- Transactional outbox table `event_outbox` in the control DB; one event per transaction; retries with backoff 30 s
  doubling to 3600 s max, **12 attempts** then dead letter (code defaults `DEFAULT_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 12`,
  `DEFAULT_BASE_DELAY_SECONDS = 30`, `DEFAULT_MAX_DELAY_SECONDS = 3600`); every 5 s, 50 events per cycle, 10 report
  jobs per cycle (`MDARIX_REPORT_WORKER_BATCH_SIZE`). Dead letters alert operations (`alert_dead_letter`, no payload
  data) and are replayed in Platform Admin > Operations > Dead letters or `scripts/replay_outbox.py EVENT_UUID --reason ...`.
- Event dispatch (`publish` in run_outbox_worker.py):
  - `PROVIDER_RECORD_RECEIVED` -> stores the provider record in the tenant's registered data plane (plane resolved only
    from `tenant_data_planes`, never from the event);
  - `TENANT_EXPORT_REQUESTED` -> `backend/app/tenant_export.run_export` (zip of all org data);
  - `RECORD_EMBEDDINGS_REQUESTED` -> `retrieval/record_evidence.run_embeddings_job`;
  - `SUPPORT_PROBLEM_REPORTED` -> e-mail to support;
  - `INBOUND_MAIL_RECEIVED` -> `mail_in/processing.process`;
  - everything else -> `notifications.handle_event` (`SUBSCRIPTION_REMINDER`, `TENANT_LIFECYCLE_NOTIFICATION`,
    `TRIAL_REMINDER`, trial declined, trial invitation retry, work events, urgent alerts, `CONNECTOR_SYNC_FAILED`,
    AI budget threshold, mail-intake limit, feedback, support access awaiting/granted/ended, team change,
    `READINESS_DIGEST`, `DEADLINE_LIST`, security notices); unhandled names are acknowledged by the `noop` publisher
    (`MDARIX_OUTBOX_PUBLISHER`, only `noop` is supported; any other value stops the worker at start).
  - Report jobs (`reports/service.run_pending_report_jobs`) run in the same loop.
- Heartbeat: JSON file `MDARIX_OUTBOX_HEARTBEAT_FILE` + DB `worker_heartbeats` (platform console).

#### 7.3.6 Scheduler (`backend/app/scheduler.py`, `scripts/run_scheduler.py`)

Each job runs when its interval has passed; Postgres advisory lock per job (a spare instance is safe); every run in
`scheduler_runs`; 3 consecutive failures alert, as does recovery; a job is "missed" after 2 intervals; heartbeat file
every cycle (30 s) and external heartbeat via the operations-alerts job. Roles: `PLATFORM_JOBS` run on MULTI and
PLATFORM, `CELL_JOBS` on MULTI and CELL (`MDARIX_DEPLOYMENT_ROLE`).

| Job | Every | Role | What |
|---|---|---|---|
| `platform-sync` | 15 min | CELL only | Report to the platform, fetch licence and packages (`backend/app/cell_link.py`) |
| `tenant-lifecycle` | 15 min | platform | Apply scheduled tenant status changes |
| `subscription-lifecycle` | 1 h | platform | Expiry, grace, renewal reminders |
| `trial-reminders` | 6 h | platform | E-mail trial owners before/at trial end |
| `trial-retention` | 24 h | platform | Delete trial data 30 days after end; anonymous client error reports after 90 days |
| `trial-sample-clock` | 1 h | platform | Move each trial's sample story dates with the trial clock |
| `embedding-catch-up` | 15 min | cell | Embed evidence without an embedding |
| `support-access-expiry` | 5 min | platform | Close expired support access, notify the org |
| `operations-alerts` | 5 min | all | Dead letters, stopped worker; pings scheduler heartbeat URL |
| `export-expiry` | 1 h | cell | Delete exports after their download period |
| `connector-sync` | 15 min | cell | Sync connectors that have a schedule |
| `purge-completion` | 24 h | cell | Finish incomplete organization deletions |
| `signal_detection` | 60 min | cell | Raise/update signals from new complaints |
| `late_evidence_sweep` | 60 min | cell | Ask for impact assessment when evidence reaches a decided investigation |
| `reporting_reminders` | 6 h | cell | Reporting clocks and CAPA effectiveness checks due |
| `due-alerts` | 15 min | cell | Due-soon reminders, overdue escalation |
| `signature-settlement` | 10 min | cell | Complete/abandon half-done dedicated-plane signatures |
| `readiness-digest` | 6 h | cell | Weekly quality-loop e-mail (queued once per ISO week from Monday 06:00 UTC) |
| `deadline-list` | 15 min | cell | Daily open-deadlines e-mail from 06:00 org time (switch `MDARIX_B6_DEADLINE_LIST`) |
| `audit-chain-verification` | 24 h | all | Verify every org's tamper-evident audit hash chain (`backend/app/audit_chain.py`) |

Commands: `dc exec background python scripts/run_scheduler.py --list` / `--once` (DEPLOYMENT.md section 5).

#### 7.3.7 Connectors, integration, ingestion, source simulators

- **CSV/Excel import** (the one upload path): `backend/app/data_import_router.py` -> `backend/app/trial/imports.py`;
  whole file validated before writing, problem rows can be set aside into stored quarantine
  (`backend/app/import_quarantine.py`, free text masked). Heavy-work rate limits apply (`upload:user` 60/h,
  `upload:org` 300/h, `backend/app/rate_limit.py`). Templates: `data/source-templates/`, `scripts/generate_source_templates.py`.
- **API connectors**: `backend/app/access/connector_sync.py` (paged reads, `next`/`next_cursor`, max pages,
  `MDARIX_CONNECTOR_MAX_BYTES` 50 MB total, 5 MB per page, 2 retries on 5xx/429) through
  `backend/app/access/outbound.py` (SSRF guard: http(s) only, no private/loopback/link-local unless in
  `MDARIX_CONNECTOR_ALLOWED_HOSTS` **and** `MDARIX_CONNECTOR_ALLOWED_HOSTS_ACKNOWLEDGED=true` in prod, no redirects,
  re-check on the open socket). Connector API keys sealed with `MDARIX_CONNECTOR_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (keyring, rotate with
  `scripts/reseal_connector_keys.py`). Secret names must start with the tenant identifier in staging/production
  (`MDARIX_CONNECTOR_SECRET_<TENANTID>_<NAME>`). Scheduled by the `connector-sync` job.
- **Provider contract**: `integration/api_contract.py` (idempotency keys, `fetch_page`), `integration/gateway.py`,
  `integration/simulator_ingestion.py` (provider reads -> idempotent outbox events, never direct business inserts).
  Mapping manifest `scripts/provider_mapping_manifest.json` (status DRAFT, generated from `source_simulator/provider_catalog.py`).
- **Ingestion package** (`ingestion/`: adapters, cli, mappings, models, normalization, services): the golden-dataset
  file ingestion + normalization used by tests, CI and the AI evaluation (`IngestionService(GOLDEN_SOURCE)` in
  `ai-evaluation.yml`).
- **Source simulators** (`source_simulator/persistent_app.py`): one process per simulated customer system, per-tenant
  DB and API key, CSV upload page at `/`. Local only (dev compose and CI integration job). Runbooks:
  `CUSTOMER_SOURCE_SYSTEMS.md`, `CUSTOMER_SYSTEM_INTEGRATION.md`, `SIMULATOR_CONNECTOR_ENDPOINTS.md`.

#### 7.3.8 Public regulatory data (FDA and others)

No code calls openFDA or any FDA API at runtime (`grep` for `api.fda.gov` / `openfda` in non-test Python found
nothing). Public reference files are vendored and loaded by scripts: `data/fda_forms/` (FDA 3500A PDF),
`data/code_lists/` (EMDN, FDA-CDRH NCIt subsets, FDA product classification `foiclass.zip`, IMDRF annexes, with
`manifest.json` provenance), `data/mir_forms/`, `data/hc_forms/`, `data/mvpi_forms/`; loaded with
`scripts/load_code_lists.py`, `scripts/load_imdrf_annex.py`, `scripts/export_us_emdr_rules.py`.

#### 7.3.9 Platform-cell link and mTLS

ADR-013/014. A `CELL` calls the platform outbound every 15 min (`backend/app/cell_link.py`): enrollment with a one-time
token, heartbeat (versions, health, counts only), signed packages verified with `MDARIX_PLATFORM_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY`.
Platform side: small CA (`MDARIX_PLATFORM_CA_KEY_FILE`, `_CERT_FILE`, `backend/app/cell_pki.py`); Caddy forwards the
client certificate fingerprint; `MDARIX_CELL_MTLS_REQUIRED` (default yes outside dev) makes the platform API require it
(`backend/app/cell_link_api.py`). Tools: `scripts/cell_platform.py`, `scripts/cell_install.py`,
`scripts/move_tenant_to_cell.py`; runbooks `CELL_INSTALL.md`, `CELL_LINK.md`. Production runs `MULTI`.

#### 7.3.10 Proxy hops, client IP, rate limits

- `backend/app/client_ip.py`: with `MDARIX_TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS=N` the client is the N-th X-Forwarded-For entry from the
  right; 0 (default) uses the socket peer. Prod compose sets 1 (Caddy); uvicorn runs `--no-proxy-headers`.
- `backend/app/rate_limit.py`: fixed windows, counters in Postgres (shared across processes), keys salted with
  `MDARIX_RATE_LIMIT_SALT`. Scopes: `signin:ip` 30/5 min, `signin:account` 10/15 min (failures only), `signup:ip`
  `MDARIX_SIGNUP_PER_ADDRESS_PER_HOUR` (30)/h, `signup:email` 3/day, `forgot:*`, `token:ip` 20/h, `mfa:*`,
  `password-change:user`, `demo:ip`, `feedback:user`, `client-errors:ip` 30/5 min, `invite*`, `team-*`, `search:user`
  60/5 min, heavy work `upload/export/sync` per user and org. Auth scopes fail closed (503 + alert) in staging/prod
  when the counter table is unreachable; the rest fail open. `MDARIX_RATE_LIMITS_ENABLED=false` only in tests.
- Mail-in and demo/trial request limits are separate settings (7.4).

#### 7.3.11 Alerting, client errors, observability, audit

- **Alerts** (`backend/app/alerts.py`): `send_alert` posts `{"text": ...}` to `MDARIX_ALERT_WEBHOOK` (Slack/Teams
  compatible) and e-mails `MDARIX_ALERT_EMAIL`; `MDARIX_ALERT_FALLBACK_WEBHOOK` only when both failed; never customer
  records. Staging/prod with no destination is refused by `config_check`. Test: `scripts/send_test_alert.py`.
- **Client error reporting** (`backend/app/client_errors.py`): public `POST /api/v1/client-errors`, rate limited, capped,
  always 202; filed under the org when a session cookie is valid, else anonymous (deleted after 90 days by
  `trial-retention`). Optional Sentry: `MDARIX_SENTRY_DSN`, `MDARIX_SENTRY_TRACES_SAMPLE_RATE` (only error shape
  leaves, `backend/app/logging_setup.py`).
- **Logging/correlation** (`backend/app/logging_setup.py`, middleware `request_id_and_log` in `backend/app/main.py`):
  one JSON line per record in staging/prod (`MDARIX_LOG_FORMAT`, `MDARIX_LOG_LEVEL`); every request gets an id (caller's
  `X-Request-ID` or `X-Correlation-ID` if plain, else new), returned in `X-Request-ID` and error bodies.
- **Other middleware** (main.py, order as declared): `enforce_cookie_csrf`, `limit_heavy_work`,
  `audit_mutating_requests` (audit envelope for every state change), `security_headers`, `genai_request_scope`,
  `deployment_role_gate`, `feature_set_gate`, `licence_read_only_gate`, `request_id_and_log`.
- **Health**: `/health` and `/health/ready` (detailed, operator only; 503 when not ready), `/health/live`,
  `/health/status` (one word), `/health/version` (`{"release": "v0.11.07"}`), plus `/api/v1/public/version`.
  Readiness checks DB-as-runtime-role, schema head, data planes, object storage, worker, scheduler, e-mail, clock
  (`backend/app/readiness.py`); clock check vs DB and optional `MDARIX_NTP_SERVER` within 1 s (`backend/app/clock_check.py`).
- **Audit**: `audit_events` hash-chained by DB trigger, updates/deletes rejected, verified daily
  (`backend/app/audit_chain.py`); archival tool `scripts/archive_expired_audit_events.py` (does not delete rows).

#### 7.3.12 AI providers (summary; details in the AI section)

`genai/provider.py` knows `controlled` (no model, default), `anthropic`, `vertex`, `groq`, `gemini`, `cerebras`,
`openrouter`, `mistral`, `ollama`, `freellmapi`, `omniroute`; vetted list `genai/vetted_providers.json`. Production
trials use `MDARIX_AI_TRIAL_CHAIN=freellmapi,omniroute,groq` through the loopback forwarders (7.1.2). Emergency off:
empty `MDARIX_AI_TRIAL_CHAIN`, `dc up -d --no-build --wait api background`, recreate the two `ai-loopback-*`
sidecars (RELEASE_0.11.05.md step 9). PII masking always on in staging/prod.

### 7.4 Settings catalogue

The authoritative list is `docs/runbooks/ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLES.md`; `tests/test_environment_reference.py` fails when
code reads a variable that page does not list, so **any new setting must be added there**. Templates:
`.env.example` (developer, `MDARIX_ENV=development`), `.env.production.example`, `.env.migrate.example` (owner/backup
secrets, loaded only by `migrate` and `backup`), `.env.test.example`. The startup gate is `backend/app/config_check.py`
(`python -m backend.app.config_check [--offline]`); unset or unknown `MDARIX_ENV` means production. Values are never
reproduced here.

| Group | Setting names (default / meaning) |
|---|---|
| Environment & address | `MDARIX_ENV` (dev/test/staging/production); `MDARIX_FEATURE_SET` (`full` default, `mvp` in production template; trials always mvp); `MDARIX_DEPLOYMENT_ROLE` (`MULTI` default, `PLATFORM`, `CELL`); `MDARIX_CELL_TENANT_ID`, `MDARIX_CELL_TENANT_KEY`, `MDARIX_PLATFORM_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY`, `MDARIX_PLATFORM_URL`, `MDARIX_CELL_ENROLLMENT_TOKEN`, `MDARIX_CELL_IDENTITY_DIR`, `MDARIX_PLATFORM_CA_FILE`, `MDARIX_PLATFORM_CA_KEY_FILE`, `MDARIX_PLATFORM_CA_CERT_FILE`, `MDARIX_CELL_MTLS_REQUIRED`; `MDARIX_APP_URL` (required; e-mail links, trusted origin); `MDARIX_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`; `MDARIX_DOMAIN`, `ACME_EMAIL`, `MDARIX_WWW_REDIRECT` (off), `MDARIX_REDIRECT_DOMAINS`, `MDARIX_SITE_ACCESS` (public), `MDARIX_SITE_LOGIN_USER`, `MDARIX_SITE_LOGIN_HASH`; `MDARIX_RELEASE`, `MDARIX_IMAGE_REPOSITORY`; `MDARIX_TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS` (0; 1 behind Caddy), `MDARIX_TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS` (old form); `MDARIX_COOKIE_SECURE` (dev only); `MDARIX_ENABLE_API_DOCS` (dev only); `MDARIX_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES` (30; hard max 60) |
| Database | `POSTGRES_HOST`, `POSTGRES_PORT`, `POSTGRES_DB`, `POSTGRES_USER`, `POSTGRES_PASSWORD`, `POSTGRES_SSLMODE` (verify-full prod), `POSTGRES_SSLROOTCERT`, `MDARIX_DB_CA_DIR` (./certs); `MDARIX_DB_RUNTIME_USER` (mdarix_runtime), `MDARIX_DB_RUNTIME_PASSWORD`; `MDARIX_DB_OWNER_USER` (prod file), `MDARIX_DB_OWNER_PASSWORD` (.env.migrate only); `MDARIX_DB_HARDENING_APPLY` (0; 1 for migrate); `MDARIX_DB_POOL_SIZE` 5, `MDARIX_DB_MAX_OVERFLOW` 10, `MDARIX_DB_POOL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` 30, `MDARIX_DB_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` 75, `MDARIX_DB_STATEMENT_TIMEOUT`, `MDARIX_DB_IDLE_IN_TRANSACTION_TIMEOUT`, `MDARIX_DB_JOB_STATEMENT_TIMEOUT` 300s, `MDARIX_DB_JOB_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` 330; `MDARIX_RLS_SYSTEM_TOKEN` (required; DB stores its hash); `MDARIX_DATA_PLANE_<NAME>_DATABASE_URL`, `..._URL_OWNER` (.env.migrate); `MDARIX_DATA_PLANE_RUNTIME_USER/PASSWORD`, `MDARIX_DATA_PLANE_PASSWORD` (local); `MDARIX_DATA_PLANE_POOL_SIZE` 3, `MDARIX_DATA_PLANE_MAX_OVERFLOW` 5; `MDARIX_TRIAL_SHARED_CONTROL_PLANE` (connection-secret marker for the shared plane); `MDARIX_MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL` (point Alembic at another DB) |

Gotchas: compose reads `${...}` in `docker-compose.prod.yml` only with `--env-file .env.production`; `docker compose
restart` does not re-read settings, `up -d` does (RELEASE_0.11.05.md step 9); the API/worker refuse to start if
`MDARIX_DB_OWNER_PASSWORD`, `MDARIX_BACKUP_DB_PASSWORD` or any `..._OWNER` URL reaches them (`.env.migrate.example`).

### 7.5 CI/CD, scanning and repository hygiene

#### 7.5.1 GitHub workflows (`.github/workflows/`)

Every job uses `runs-on: ${{ vars.CI_LINUX_RUNNER || 'ubuntu-latest' }}` (self-hosted option, `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER.md`,
scripts in `infrastructure/ci/self-hosted/`). Actions are pinned by SHA.

| Workflow | Triggers | What runs |
|---|---|---|
| `ci.yml` "MDARIX R1 CI" | push to `main`, pull requests (both ignore `**/*.md`, `docs/**`), weekly cron Mon 02:41 UTC, manual | Jobs: `ai-golden-qualification` (lock + `tests.test_ai_golden_qualification`); `backend` (hashed install, `pip check`, `pip-audit`, embedding cache, `alembic upgrade head`, **downgrade -1 and upgrade again**, `alembic check`, runtime role, `validate_db_hardening.py`, Day 35 fixture, `seed_demo_expansion.py`, `pytest -q` with `MDARIX_REQUIRE_LOCAL_MODEL=1`, `docker compose config`); `dedicated-plane`, `deployment-roles` (CELL vs PLATFORM 404s and job lists), `dataplane-tests` (four tenant DBs) - not on PRs; `production-mode` (TLS Postgres via `infrastructure/docker/ci/pg-tls.sh`, generated secrets, `config_check --offline` then full, migrate steps, API in production mode + `production_smoke.py`); `prod-compose-config` (shell scripts parse, compose resolves with `write-env-files.sh`, owner/backup secrets reach only migrate/backup); `prod-stack` (full Caddy/TLS/ClamAV stack, smoke, every scheduled job once, backup with and without BYPASSRLS, restore into scratch DB); `frontend` (npm ci, `npm_audit_gate.py` twice incl. `--omit=dev`, build, lint, format:check, vitest); `browser` (Playwright + axe; chromium only on PRs); `container-validation` (not on PRs); `image-scan` (build api/web/backup, import check, Trivy HIGH/CRITICAL with `.trivyignore.yaml`); `security` (`pip-audit`, `ruff check`, `ruff format --check`, gitleaks action); `integration` (Mailpit + 8 simulators, `validate_local_stack.py`, connector tests; not on PRs); `release-report` (requires all; heavy jobs may be skipped only on PRs) |
| `codeql.yml` | pull requests, weekly Mon 03:17 | CodeQL python + javascript-typescript, `security-extended`, fails on severity >= 7.0, no upload |
| `release.yml` | push of tag `v*.*.*` | tag must equal `VERSION`; build api/web/backup; import check of `backend.app.main`, background scripts, mail-in receiver; Trivy; publish to GHCR + cosign sign + SBOM only if `vars.MDARIX_PUBLISH_IMAGES == 'true'`. **For 0.11.05, 0.11.06 and 0.11.07 the owner cancels this run (no GitHub release)** (RELEASE_0.11.07.md "Known and accepted") |
| `ai-evaluation.yml` | manual only | Paid-model golden evaluation; refuses unless `confirm_spend` = `SPEND` and a numeric token cap; needs secret `MDARIX_EVALUATION_API_KEY` (skips otherwise); `run_real_evaluation.py --gate --require-model --switch-ai-on` |
| `security-scan.yml` | manual only, runner label `mdarix-linux` (self-hosted) | `scripts/security_scan.sh --release <name> <steps> summary`; reports stay on the runner, never uploaded |

Dependabot (`.github/dependabot.yml`): monthly grouped updates for pip, npm (`/frontend`), docker (root and
`infrastructure/docker/backup`), docker-compose (ClamAV digest), GitHub Actions.

CI usage note: CI minutes/billing have constrained runs in the past; the runbooks record local gates as the evidence
for 0.11.0x releases (RELEASE_0.11.06.md, 0.11.07.md). Whether CI ran on this branch: not verified.

#### 7.5.2 Reviewed scan exceptions

- `.trivyignore.yaml`: **25** CVE entries, all `expired_at: 2027-01-06` (after that date image scans fail until
  re-reviewed). Reasons by group: zlib MiniZip/gz* functions not used (CVE-2023-45853, CVE-2026-76844, CVE-2026-85091);
  SQLite never opened (CVE-2025-7458, CVE-2026-11822, -11824, -119373, -119374, -119379); ncurses `infocmp` never run
  (CVE-2025-69720); systemd-homed not present (CVE-2026-16742); gzip program not run (CVE-2026-41992); util-linux
  mount/nsenter/partition parsing, no local users, `no-new-privileges` (CVE-2026-53613, -76642, -78408, -78409, -78410,
  -53615); libacl not used (CVE-2026-54369); OpenSSL DTLS not used (CVE-2026-84782); Perl Archive::Tar not used
  (CVE-2026-9538); GnuTLS not called (CVE-2026-88647, -88648); go-jose v3 only in Caddy's unused internal CA
  (CVE-2026-116587); Linux-PAM `pam_namespace`, no logins (CVE-2025-6020). Several carry "owner sign-off" dates.
- `.npm-audit-exceptions.toml`: one entry, `GHSA-fc5q-39xc-vjpf` (picomatch ReDoS, build-time only), reviewed by the
  owner, **expires 2026-12-31**; read by `scripts/npm_audit_gate.py`.
- `.gitleaks.toml`: allowlists `tests/test_api_connector_contract.py` and `tests/test_day26_security_closure.py`
  (synthetic sentinels).
- `.git-blame-ignore-revs`: the ruff-format and prettier mass-format commits (`git config blame.ignoreRevsFile
  .git-blame-ignore-revs`). Pure formatting commits must be added here (AGENTS.md).
- Dependency pinning: `requirements.txt` hashed lock (from `requirements.in`), `requirements-dev.txt` (ruff pinned),
  `frontend/package-lock.json`; base images by digest.

### 7.7 Local development setup

- Windows laptop, Docker Desktop, Python 3.13 venv `.venv`, Node/npm, Git (README.md). PostgreSQL driver is `pg8000`
  (psycopg's DLL is blocked by Windows Application Control).
- `copy .env.example .env` (keep `MDARIX_ENV=development`); `docker compose up -d`; `pip install -r requirements.txt`;
  `MDARIX_DB_HARDENING_APPLY=1` then `alembic upgrade head`; set `MDARIX_DB_RUNTIME_PASSWORD` (24+ chars) and run
  `scripts/configure_runtime_role.py`; fixtures via `provision_day35_fixture.py` and `demo_tenant.py setup`.
- API `uvicorn backend.app.main:app --port 8007`; web `npm --prefix frontend run dev` on 5177 (proxies `/api` to 8007).
- **Databases**: control Postgres on host port 5433. Demo DB `mdarix_r1` (never used by tests); test DB
  `mdarix_r1_test` (default chosen by `tests/conftest.py` unless `POSTGRES_DB` is exported); other dated test DBs
  appear in STATUS.md (`mdarix_p2_test`, `mdarix_whiftrial_test`). Note `.env.test.example` still says
  `POSTGRES_DB=mdarix_r1`: do not copy it blindly; export the test DB name.
- **Journey/Playwright server**: run `scripts/journey_api.py` with `MDARIX_ENV=development` (STATUS.md journeys
  section). With `MDARIX_ENV=test` the session cookie becomes `__Host-mdarix_session`, which browsers accept only with
  `Secure`, so plain-HTTP journeys cannot sign in (`auth/cookies.py`: secure name outside local development; dev uses
  `mdarix_session` unless `MDARIX_COOKIE_SECURE=true`). `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` points Playwright at the running Vite;
  otherwise `frontend/playwright.config.ts` starts its own dev or preview server.
- **Git Bash first on PATH** on Windows: `tests/test_backup_age_check.py`, `test_backup_retention.py`,
  `test_enter_trial_ai_keys.py`, `test_trial_ai_gateway_units.py` call `bash`; picking WSL's bash instead gives false
  failures (owner's laptop notes; the test files are verified, the WSL symptom is not).
- **Embedding cache**: `MDARIX_EMBEDDING_CACHE_DIR` default `.model-cache` (untracked); a worktree without one uses the
  main checkout's cache automatically; prepare with `scripts/prepare_embedding_cache.py`; `model` tests skip without it
  unless `MDARIX_REQUIRE_LOCAL_MODEL=1` (README.md).
- Untracked in this worktree: `frontend/e2e/zz-site-shots.spec.ts` and `frontend/vite.awl.config.ts` (git status);
  decide whether they belong in the release before committing.

### 7.8 Quality gates and tests

#### 7.8.1 Gates (exactly as AGENTS.md lists)

Before committing: `python -m pytest` (uses `mdarix_r1_test`), `python -m ruff check .`, `python -m ruff format --check .`,
and in `frontend/`: `npm run lint`, `npm run format:check`, `npm test`, `npm run build`. Every behaviour change ships
with a test that fails without it; prefer behavioural tests over source-grepping tests. Formatting: ruff (120 cols,
`ruff.toml`), prettier (120 cols, `frontend/.prettierrc.json`).

#### 7.8.2 Test guards (`tests/conftest.py`, `pytest.ini`)

- `POSTGRES_DB` defaults to `mdarix_r1_test` before any backend import (engine is created at import time).
- Unless `MDARIX_TEST_ALLOW_LIVE_PROVIDERS=1`: `MDARIX_GENAI_PROVIDER=controlled`, empty API key, base URL and
  fallbacks, `RESEND_API_KEY=""`; `MDARIX_GENAI_SHARED_REST=false`. E-mail-no-send is covered by `tests/test_email_no_send.py`.
- Markers: `unit` (no DB; prove with `MDARIX_TEST_NO_DATABASE=1 pytest -m unit`), `db`, `dataplane` (excluded by
  default via `addopts = -m "not dataplane"`), `model`, `requirement(id)`, `slow`.

#### 7.8.3 Layout and counts

- Backend: `tests/` has 567 `test_*.py` files plus helpers (`fake_ai_gateway.py`, `runtime_role.py`, `route_inventory.py`,
  seeds), `tests/fixtures/` (`emdr_examples`, `indian_register`), `tests/snapshots/`. Largest prefixes by file count:
  trial 28, case 21, b9 19, b8 15, ai 15, b6a 14, platform 10, day35 10, customer 10, b5 10, readiness 7, auth 7.
  Last full run recorded: 5,585 passed, 20 skipped, 4 deselected, 1 xfailed in 2 h 0 min (STATUS.md, 17 Nov, WHIF push 2);
  no full-suite result for 0.11.07 is recorded in STATUS.md (not verified).
- Frontend: 268 `*.test.ts(x)` files under `frontend/src` (workspace 158, admin 25, app 19, shared 12, site 11,
  customer-admin 11, ...); last recorded 1,914 tests in 259 files (STATUS.md, 17 Nov). Playwright specs in
  `frontend/e2e/` (19 tracked specs plus the untracked `zz-site-shots.spec.ts`, with `fixtures/`, `support/`, `personas.ci.json`; incl. `app-wide-layout.spec.ts`, `persona-journeys.spec.ts`, `trial-journeys.spec.ts`,
  `your-data-journey.spec.ts`, `getting-started-journey.spec.ts`, `site-hero.spec.ts`).

#### 7.8.4 Known flaky / environment issues

- Four backend tests compare the local IST date with the UTC date and fail only between 00:00 and 05:30 IST; they pass
  with the clock on UTC (`TZ=UTC`). Decision and effectiveness dates stay UTC days; owner question open
  (RELEASE_0.11.06.md, 0.11.07.md). The four test names are not given in the runbooks (not verified).
- Playwright was green per spec, partly on reruns; no single uninterrupted green run (RELEASE_0.11.06.md).
- Local activation throttle (15 min) stops a journey run that follows another quickly (STATUS.md).
- Full backend suite takes about 2 hours.

### 7.9 Release process 0.11.07 (`docs/runbooks/RELEASE_0.11.07.md`)

Running before: `v0.11.06`, Alembic head `ux2navigationnew`. **No database change** (no new revision; `migrate` runs
and changes nothing). Steps:

| # | Step | Who |
|---|---|---|
| 0.1 | Owner's go recorded | OWNER |
| 0.2 | Disk >= 8 GB free; release `v0.11.06`, revision `ux2navigationnew` (read-only) | assistant |
| 0.3 | Copy settings `.env.production.pre-01107` | assistant |
| 0.4 | Fresh backup + off-site copy + manifest check, even with no DB change | OWNER |
| 1 | `git checkout v0.11.07`, `MDARIX_RELEASE=v0.11.07`, build; `config_check --offline` (CONFIG=0, no output); `python -c "import backend.app.main"` (IMPORT=0) | assistant |
| 2 | **STOP for owner's OK**, then `dc up -d --wait` | OWNER says go |
| 3 | Smoke: `/api/v1/public/version` = 0.11.07, health, site home/platform, one trial sign-in: Today, Cases, cockpit at 1920 and 1440 | assistant + OWNER |
| 4 | Rollback A only if needed (images back to `v0.11.06`; nothing in DB to roll back) | OWNER decides |

Owner-only: the go, the backup/off-site copy, pushing the tag, the OK before restart, any rollback decision,
cancelling the `release.yml` run started by the tag, any `refresh_trial_story.py --apply`, any AI switch-on.

### 7.10 Known issues and technical debt

- **Production has no PITR**: RPO 24 h, files on the VM disk (`local`), only daily backup + snapshot protect them
  (BACKUP_AND_RESTORE.md, GCP_DEPLOYMENT.md).
- **OmniRoute 3.8.52 critical vulnerability** on the host; loopback-only is the control (RELEASE_0.11.05.md step 1).
- **Scan exceptions expire**: npm exception 2026-12-31; all Trivy exceptions 2027-01-06.
- **`MDARIX_BACKUP_KEEP_DAYS`**: template says 14, code and runbook default 30 (`.env.production.example` vs `backup.sh`).
- **`.env.test.example`** names `mdarix_r1` (the demo DB) as `POSTGRES_DB`; conftest only protects runs that do not
  export `POSTGRES_DB`.
- **`object_storage/service.py` docstring** says production must use s3; production uses local.
- **Two Resend webhook routes** (`/api/v1/communication/resend/webhook` and `/api/v1/stage3/resend/webhook`); whether
  either is configured is not verified.
- **`minio/minio:latest`** in the dev compose is unpinned (prod images are pinned).
- **Mail-in is built but off**; enabling needs port 25, MX, certificate and `--profile mail-in`.
- **`release.yml`** is cancelled by hand for every 0.11.0x tag; images are built on the VM, not published/signed.
- **STATUS.md "Known issues listed on 2026-09-30"**: items not marked addressed have "not been re-verified since"
  (platform-admin role strings, governance bootstrap granting Viewer signing authority, Decision Center reviewer from
  body, `provision_tenant` retry bug, lower-case `active` comparisons, tenant lifecycle 500, provider records stopping
  at the connector ledger, missing UI for roles/connectors/SoD). Current status of each: not verified.
- **STATUS.md was last updated 17 Nov 2026** (before 0.11.06/0.11.07); add 0.11.07 results only from real output.
- **Legacy day/stage-numbered scripts and modules** (`day35_*`, `stage3_workflow_router.py`, `stage4_qualification.py`)
  remain; do not add new ones (AGENTS.md naming rule).
- **Old handover docs** `docs/handover/24, 31-45` are out of date (task brief); trust code and runbooks.

## 7.12 Endpoints not described in sections 2-7 (coverage catch-up)

A mechanical check found 26 endpoint paths that no section above names. They are described here so that every one
of the 830 operations in Appendix A is covered somewhere. Guards are the FastAPI dependencies resolved for the route.

### 7.12.1 Platform-cell link (`backend/app/cell_link_api.py`, `backend/app/cell_link.py`, ADR-014)
Part of the cell architecture plan (tenant "cells" that run separately and call the platform). Mounted in
`backend/app/main.py:600-601`. **A cell calls in; the platform never calls a cell.** Enrollment exchanges the
one-time token from an enrollment bundle for a certificate (under the platform's authority) and a credential; every
later call carries `Authorization: Bearer <credential>` and, through the front door, the client-certificate
fingerprint header `X-Client-Certificate-Fingerprint` (required outside local development; see `mtls_required`).
Heartbeats are metadata only: the schema refuses unknown fields and free text. Packages are signed envelopes the
platform stores as uploaded (reference data, mapping templates, policies). `cell_link.read_only` puts a cell in
read-only mode (keeps data, refuses state changes except signing in; nothing is deleted; `main.py:380-387`).

| Method | Path | Function | Who |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/cells/enroll` | `enroll` | a cell, with the one-time enrollment token |
| POST | `/api/v1/cells/heartbeat` | `heartbeat` | an enrolled cell (credential + certificate) |
| GET | `/api/v1/cells/packages` | `packages` | an enrolled cell: released packages |
| GET | `/api/v1/cells/packages/{package_id}` | `package_content` | an enrolled cell: one package |
| POST | `/api/v1/platform-admin/packages` | `upload_package` | Platform Administrator: upload a package signed by the release pipeline (the platform verifies the signature) |
| GET | `/api/v1/platform-admin/packages` | `list_packages` | Platform Administrator |
| POST | `/api/v1/platform-admin/packages/{package_id}/withdraw` | `withdraw_package` | Platform Administrator |
| GET | `/api/v1/platform-admin/console/cells` | `fleet` | Platform Administrator: every cell as its heartbeats describe it |

Status: built and tested in code; production runs as one shared platform (no cells enrolled) as far as the
runbooks show (not verified on the server). Do not remove: the cell plan is approved design work.

### 7.12.2 Tenant data-plane read API (`backend/app/data_plane_router.py`, prefix `/api/v1/data-plane`)
"Tenant business-data API backed by the registered customer data plane." Read-only lists for the signed-in tenant,
all guarded by `get_request_context` + `get_data_plane_db` (tenant from the session only). Mounted at `main.py:887`.

| Method | Path | Function |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/products` | `products` |
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/investigations` | `investigations` |
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/evidence` | `evidence` |
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/suppliers` | `suppliers` |
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/capas` | `capas` |
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/search` | `search` |
| GET | `/api/v1/data-plane/export.csv` | `export_csv` (extra permission dependency; CSV export is audited) |

### 7.12.3 Data mapping catalogue (`backend/app/mapping_catalog_router.py`, prefix `/api/v1/admin/mapping`)
Customer administrator tooling to map a source system's objects and fields onto MDARIX's canonical entities
(frontend `frontend/src/features/mapping/`, section 6). Guards: `get_request_context`, control plane `get_db`.
Endpoints not named elsewhere: `GET source-priorities`, `GET families`, `GET initial-mappings`,
`POST schema-drift` (compare a source schema with the mapped one), `POST validation-decisions` and
`GET validation-decisions` (a person records a validation decision on a mapping), `GET impact-history`,
`POST suggestions` and `POST suggestions/decision` (mapping suggestions are proposals a person accepts or
rejects). The rest of this router (`applications`, `objects/{id}/fields`, `canonical/entities`, `transforms`,
`validation-rules`, `override-policies`, `connector-versions`, `dry-run`, `validate`, `schema-discovery`,
`compare`) is in section 6.

### 7.12.4 Others
| Method | Path | Module.function | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/trial/imports/{entity}/check` | `data_import_router.check_import` | "Import your data" (trial): checks a CSV for one entity without saving. Guards: trial organizations only (`_trial_only`), refuses a sample-only state (`refuse_sample_only`), tenant from the session |
| POST | `/api/v1/trial/imports/{entity}` | `data_import_router.run_import` | Same, and saves the rows |
| PUT | `/api/v1/regulatory-reference/products/{product_id}/identity` | `regulatory_reference_router.set_identity` | Sets a product's regulatory identity (e.g. device class, codes) used by reportability (section 4) |
| POST | `/api/v1/regulatory-reference/products/{product_id}/registrations` | `regulatory_reference_router.add_registration` | Adds a market registration for a product |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/regulatory-reference/products/{product_id}/registrations/{registration_id}` | `regulatory_reference_router.remove_registration` | Removes one |
| POST | `/api/v1/workspace/context/reset` | `workspace_context.reset_context` | Clears the user's saved workspace context (last product/investigation in view) |
| GET | `/api/v1/stage3/provider-health` | `stage3_workflow_router.stage3_provider_health` | AI provider health for the Stage 3 interaction workflow (section 5). Shows the provider name: keep off camera in the demo |

The exact permission each of these needs is enforced inside the handler or its permission dependency; read the
function before changing it.

## 8. Release 0.11.07 state, demo readiness, open items, never-do list

### 8.1 What 0.11.07 contains (on top of 0.11.06)
| Commit | What |
|---|---|
| `63978413` | App uses wide screens (fluid to 1680 px, centred); one palette (ink navy, brand teal, cool page background) scoped to the app shell in light mode; story-rail navigation with captions; compact gradient hero headers per area (Cases: complaints overdue; Products: products rising against their history); calm motion (CSS only, reduced-motion respected); phone fix (Cases no longer scrolls sideways) |
| `bbe22330` | Persona journeys capture each first screen at 1920x1080 |
| `684c9196` | One hero on an open investigation (page band visually hidden while the investigation header `uw-inv-head` is shown); wide-layout check waits for Today's data |
| `586da2f1` | Merge of the website build: dark gradient hero with a real product screen in a browser frame and an animated story card (Signal -> Product version -> Evidence -> Decision), facts strip, real product screens for the five steps, dark bands, scroll reveal (`frontend/src/site/motion.tsx`), site-only palette in `site.css` |
| `20006599` | Site hero: the story card sits under the screenshot, not over its words |
| `4aec8b32` | `VERSION` 0.11.07; `docs/runbooks/RELEASE_0.11.07.md` (no database change); 0.11.06 runbook corrected on what its downgrade does |
Later commits on the same branch (website product screens recaptured from the new app look, gate fixes) are listed
in `git log v0.11.06..HEAD`.

0.11.07 also fixes one bug found while recapturing the website (`36efe6d8`): a CAPA effectiveness check signed
inconclusive showed the badge "No check planned"; it now reads "Signed inconclusive" (test added).

**Gates run on the final 0.11.07 code (19 Nov 2026, commit `667b8607`):** pytest on a fresh test database with
`TZ=UTC`: 5,699 passed, 1 failed, 20 skipped (the failure, `test_resolve_and_close::test_rt08`, compares three
audit events written in the same instant; re-run alone it passed 3 of 3: an ordering flake, open item 17);
ruff check and format clean; frontend lint, format check and build pass; vitest 2,042 passed (268 files);
Playwright public-shell, site-hero, app-wide-layout, did-it-work, persona-journeys, trial-journeys,
one-navigation and flagship-journey all pass; gitleaks clean.

### 8.2 How it is released (owner approves each step)
1. Owner: fresh database backup + off-site copy + manifest check; settings copy (owner runs these one-liners;
   assistants never touch `.env.production`).
2. Merge the release branch into `release/0.10`, run all gates (0.4 and section 7), `gitleaks`, push, tag
   `v0.11.07`, cancel the `release.yml` run.
3. On the VM: check out the tag, set `MDARIX_RELEASE=v0.11.07`, build, offline config check, API import check.
4. Restart with `up -d --wait` (no migration in this release; Alembic head stays `ux2navigationnew`). One-shot
   containers (migrate, web-volumes) exit and can make `--wait` return non-zero: check each service's health.
5. Smoke checks: `/api/v1/public/version` = 0.11.07, health, site pages, one sign-in on the new navigation.
6. Rollback: images only (previous tag), since there is no schema change.

### 8.3 Buildathon demo readiness (20-21 Nov 2026)
- Demo runs on production `mdarix.com` in a **free trial organization with sample data** ("Orbit Medical Devices
  (Demo)"), trial owner plus two colleagues: one with the preparing personas (Complaint Handler, Investigator,
  Product Quality Engineer, Risk Reviewer; Service Desk) and one with the deciding personas (Regulatory
  Reviewer, Decision Maker, Executive). Mailboxes are demo aliases owned by the company; the owner types every
  password and authenticator code.
- **Persona caveat (section 2):** a second persona of Service Desk or Executive is saved but grants no extra
  rights; only the 8 workflow roles add rights. Dry-run every step for the person who will click it.
- **Segregation of duties:** keep preparer and signer different people in every recorded step (see 8.4 #1).
- **AI:** off by default for the trial. If shown, show it as proposals a person accepts or rejects. **Never
  name the model or provider.** Screens that display the provider or model name and must stay off camera:
  AI provider health (`/api/v1/stage3/genai-health`, `/api/v1/stage3/provider-health`), the AI release-gate bar,
  Security, AI governance, the decision and decision-brief screens' AI panels, trust evaluation (section 5.11).
- Sample data is labelled "Sample" on every screen; keep it so.
- Videos: one hero video (3 min) and short story videos; narration and scripts live outside this repository.

### 8.4 Open items found while writing this handover (not fixed in 0.11.07)
Priority: **H** fix before the first paid pilot, **M** fix soon, **L** tidy.

| # | P | Item | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | H | Segregation of duties is not enforced in five places: brief approver can also sign the decision; CAPA effectiveness planner can also sign its result; lot-impact signing; evidence and evidence-request review; an approval task can be reassigned or accepted by its own preparer (claiming is blocked) | section 3.32 |
| 2 | H | Decision Maker's "Return" asks for no authenticator; brief and decision immutability is enforced in application code only (no database constraint) | section 3.32 |
| 3 | H | AI release gate cannot pass: the human sign-off on the golden dataset and the AI release record are missing in `evaluation/` (dataset lock reviewer reads "human review pending"). Owner must re-approve before any AI is switched on for customers | section 5 |
| 4 | H | Data migrations under forced RLS need the system scope; `ux2navigationnew` moved 0 rows on production and was repeated by hand. Add system scope + a row-count test pattern for every future data migration | sections 0.3, 2 |
| 5 | H | No point-in-time recovery: up to 24 h of data can be lost (backups are daily dumps). Approved SLO wants 15 min RPO | section 7 |
| 6 | M | Health Canada form file is missing (route answers 409 `FORM_FILE_NEEDED`); India MvPI field map not yet checked by a person; all 61 market rule files are still "starter" (consultant confirmation pending); EU MIR form title says build SB-11154 while bundled schemas are SB-11252 | section 4 |
| 7 | M | Uploaded files live on the VM disk, while the object-storage code comment says production must use S3-compatible storage | section 7 |
| 8 | M | The AI gateway program on the host has a known critical vulnerability; its only protection is listening on 127.0.0.1. Scan exceptions expire 2026-12-31 (npm) and 2027-01-06 (Trivy) | section 7 |
| 9 | M | Test isolation for AI: `tests/conftest.py` may not blank every provider key / trial-chain setting while `backend/app/db/session.py` loads the local settings file (not verified) | section 5 |
| 10 | M | Dates: a blank time zone at intake falls back to UTC; the Cases "overdue" view uses UTC midnight; four backend tests fail 00:00-05:30 IST. Owner decision on IST dates is open | sections 3, 7 |
| 11 | M | Platform admin creating a user in another tenant via `POST /admin/identity/users` may get 404 (tenant-bound route; not verified) | section 2 |
| 12 | L | Docs drift: STATUS.md says seven workflow roles (code has 8); `scripts/create_platform_admin.py` docstring says 72 h link (code default 48 h); `.env.test.example` names the demo DB `mdarix_r1`; backup keep-days 14 in template vs 30 default in `backup.sh`; dev compose uses unpinned `minio:latest` | sections 2, 7 |
| 13 | L | Sign-in, sign-up, Ask and file downloads use raw `fetch`, not `requestJson`; `npm run lint` is TypeScript only (no ESLint) | section 6 |
| 14 | L | "Expected vs observed" exists in the trend check and Today, not yet inside the Lifecycle Twin screen | section 3 |
| 15 | L | Five backend routers have no frontend caller (API-only): `ask_router`, `communication_router`, `integration_router`, `retrieval.router`, `scenario_intelligence.router` | section 6 |
| 16 | L | Untracked local helpers in the release worktree must never be committed: `frontend/vite.awl.config.ts`, `frontend/e2e/zz-site-shots.spec.ts`, `frontend/e2e/zz-site-home.spec.ts` | section 6 |
| 17 | L | Audit events written in the same instant have no fixed order, so `test_rt08` (resolve and close) can fail at random; order by a sequence, not the timestamp | section 3 |

### 8.5 Owner decisions that shape the code (do not reverse without the owner)
- One navigation for every organization (classic navigation retired for all tenants on 0.11.06).
- Navigation rail shows a red dot, not a number, for areas with urgent work (5 Nov decision; a test enforces it).
- The three-click confirmation dialog stays.
- No password expiry; no SSO claim (the SSO route answers 409).
- AI off by default; Vertex (Gemini on Google Cloud, Mumbai region) is the chosen paid provider and the code seam
  exists (`genai/provider.py`), but switching it on is an owner decision after qualification, a written
  zero-data-retention confirmation and a billing upgrade.
- MDARIX never submits to authorities directly.
- No prices, competitor names, logos, testimonials or certification claims anywhere public.

### 8.6 Never do (summary)
- Never read, print, edit or commit `.env*` files, keys, tokens or the owner's secret folders.
- Never take tenant or actor from a request body; never bypass RLS in a request path.
- Never point tests at the demo database `mdarix_r1` or call real AI / e-mail from tests.
- Never deploy, push, tag or write to the production database without the owner's OK.
- Never name the AI model in the product or the demo; never present AI output as fact.
- Never add a schema change without an Alembic migration (single head) and a test.
- Never `git stash`, `docker system prune`, force-push, or skip hooks.

### 8.7 First hour for a new assistant on this code
1. Read `AGENTS.md`, this document's sections 0 and 1, then `STATUS.md`.
2. Set up locally per `README.md` (Python 3.13 `.venv`, Docker Desktop, `alembic upgrade head` with
   `MDARIX_DB_HARDENING_APPLY=1`, runtime role, fixture or demo tenant).
3. Run the gates in 0.4 once to get a baseline (`TZ=UTC` for pytest), before changing anything.
4. For any change: find the module in Appendix A (endpoint) and B (table), read the section that owns it, write
   the failing test first, change the smallest amount of code, run the gates.
